=======================================================================
   RETURN OF THE UNRIVALED SPEAR KNIGHT — ARC 1 (CHAPTERS 1 TO 30)
   Total Chapters: 30 | Total Shots: 1620 | Total Words: 37710
   Estimated Audio Duration: ~164.0 Minutes (~2.7 Hours)
=======================================================================


======================================================================
CHAPTER 01 VOICEOVER
======================================================================
CONTINUOUS RECORDING TEXT:
In the Avalon Empire, magic and bloodlines decide who commands and who crawls in the dirt. Nobles obsess over sacred swords, treating swordsmanship as the only noble art... ...while sneering at spears as cheap sticks meant for disposable peasant conscripts. But our boy, Joshua Sanders, decided that tradition was garbage, picked up a spear, and rewrote continental history. Smashed through every noble boundary, slaughtered every army sent his way, and single-handedly brought an end to the Empire's catastrophic civil war as the undisputed strongest warrior alive. But if you saw him right now, you would never guess he was the continent's apex predator. Our boy is slumped face-first in the mud, bleeding out like a slaughtered animal. He tries to speak, but only mouthfuls of thick crimson blood bubble up from his throat. Even his partner, the soul-drinking spear Lugia, lies discarded on the frozen ground. Surrounding him in the clearing are five figures—the highest-ranking leaders of the very empire he fought to build. And the man who orchestrated this slaughter? None other than his supreme lord and supposed closest brother-in-arms, the newly crowned Emperor, Kaiser von Britten. Talk about peak corporate betrayal after doing all the heavy lifting. An old man in long black robes steps forward with a cold sneer, observing that for an elite martial artist, holy divine power and ancient magic are fundamentally lethal opposites. The shockwave of those two cosmic energies violently colliding inside a mortal body would have instantly vaporized an ordinary knight into red mist. Through the blinding pain, Joshua can only muster one broken question: *Why?* He never wanted the throne. He never cared about politics or sitting in golden halls. His entire dream was simple—to strip away the world's prejudice against the spear, stand beside his friend as the Empire's unbreakable shield, and live with honor. But stepping into the clearing is a man wearing a pristine white cloak—the supreme Master of the Continental Magic Tower, Archmage Evergrant. Evergrant looks down at him like a disappointed professor, calmly explaining that in this cruel world, there are people whose sheer existence is a threat to the state. With the civil war finished, nothing on the continent can challenge Kaiser's grand ambition to conquer the world—except for one single anomaly. Most veteran knights spend forty years meditating just to coat a three-inch dagger in basic aura. But Joshua? Our boy was swinging a twelve-foot colossal spear coated in apocalyptic mana! Worse still, Evergrant knows the awful truth: Joshua secretly broke through the legendary mortal ceiling, achieving a realm of power where even emperors can be slaughtered in their sleep. In the palm of his hand, the archmage cradles a mysterious grey sphere that radiates a sickeningly bright flare—a forbidden relic excavated from the ancient ruins of Amon. It is the only known medium that can simultaneously conduct opposing magic and holy energy. Joshua's mind reels—the expedition to Amon wasn't an imperial mission; it was prep work for his execution! Next, a middle-aged knight in shimmering golden armor steps up, sporting the roaring dragon crest of Avalon's most elite royal bodyguard. The veteran admits with a nervous shudder that merely standing in the presence of Joshua's spear gave him chronic nightmares, convinced the peasant spearman would turn on them at any moment. Putting on an Oscar-worthy performance, Evergrant bows with theatrical humility, thanking Joshua for his service before giving him a polite farewell. But as their eyes meet, Joshua catches the subtle smirk curling the corner of the archmage's mouth. It isn't sorrow—it's pure, disgusting mockery. Then Kaiser speaks, whispering in a soft, trembling voice that he cannot bear to watch, yet feels obligated to witness the end of his dear companion. The sheer hypocrisy makes our boy physically nauseous. The man who drank wine with him by the fire last night is now having him butchered in the woods like a rabid hound. Right beneath his chest, massive red and violet runes ignite across the mossy floor—a high-grade strategic destruction circle designed to vaporize everything within fifty meters! Joshua knows this formula well. At the rate the ambient mana is condensing, he has five minutes at most before the clearing detonates with the force of a falling meteor. In the face of certain death, panic disappears, replaced by an inferno of pure, cold hatred. He carves the faces of all five conspirators into his memory, engraving every wrinkle. Swallowing his own blood, he enters deep meditation, desperately attempting to suppress the feral beast of clashing divine and magical energies tearing through his organs. What feels like two lifetimes of torment is only three minutes on the clock. His physical vessels are completely wrecked; the biological limit of his mortal frame has shattered. But right as his consciousness begins to fade into eternal blackness, a low, rhythmic vibration rings in his ears—a dark heartbeat calling out to him. Resting in the dirt a few feet away, Lugia radiates a menacing crimson pulse that defies all mortal magic. The weapon is awakening on its own! For two decades of relentless warfare, the spear had served him as an unyielding blade of iron, never revealing the dormant primordial demon sealed within its core. But now, bathed in his dying hatred, the spear screams with conscious fury, commanding its master not to bow his neck to these hypocrites! Moving like a possessed revenant, our boy chokes down the excruciating agony, clawing his fingers into the soil and dragging his shattered body forward. With a final surge of willpower, his bloody hand firmly clamps around the spear's grip. A savage grin cuts across his lips—he made it. Right at that exact microsecond, the strategic circle reaches maximum capacity, detonating with an earth-shattering roar that levels the forest canopy into dust! But instead of vaporizing his soul, the catastrophic collision of three absolute forces—holy light, ancient magic, and Lugia's demonic core—tears open a rift in space-time! Sucked into the temporal whirlpool, Joshua's adult consciousness hurtles backward through time, retaining every memory, battle, and technique intact. The blinding light fades, replaced by the pungent, familiar stench of dry straw and horse manure. Our boy opens his eyes, groaning in mild confusion. Looking down, he doesn't see the scarred, battle-hardened hands of a legendary grand duke. He is staring at the smooth, tiny fingers of a scrawny child! Scrambling out of the stables, he rushes over to a muddy puddle on the cobblestone path, staring down at his watery reflection in shock. Staring back is the face of a nine-year-old boy with dark blue locks and deep indigo eyes. He hasn't just survived; he has regressed thirty years into his own childhood! He recognizes the grim stone walls around him—the outer servant quarters of House Agnus, where he and his concubine mother were discarded and shunned like vermin. A sudden realization hits him like lightning: at this exact age, his mother Lucia is still alive. The woman who suffered in poverty for his sake is waiting for him in the cottage. Checking his internal channels, our boy notices a strange complication—a dense, turbulent knot of energy is lodged firmly in his dantian, sealing his natural mana circulation. The explosion didn't vanish; the trinity of Amon's divine power, Evergrant's high magic, and Lugia's demonic aura traveled back with his soul, locked inside his child meridians! Before he can finish unknotting the seal, heavy footsteps break the silence as three familiar bullies swagger into the alleyway—Gort, Roid, and Rols, the Duke's bottom-feeder stable hands. In his previous life, these three grown men beat and tormented him daily, using his mother's safety as leverage to force him into doing their dirty chores. But today, instead of trembling and cowering in the corner, the scrawny child stands upright, fixing them with the cold, bored stare of a veteran executioner. Annoyed by the brat's disrespect, Gort steps forward with an outstretched hand, completely oblivious to the fact that he is approaching a returned war god. With a minimal shift in weight, our boy redirects Gort's clumsy forward momentum, using pure physics and zero mana to send the burly guard face-planting hard into the cobblestone! Roid and Rols freeze in utter disbelief, unable to process how their scrawny ten-year-old punching bag just dropped their leader without breaking a single drop of sweat. Looking down at the groaning man, Joshua softly warns them that the past hierarchy is officially dead. Cross his path again, and the next lesson will cost their lives. Turning his back on the trembling fools, Joshua looks toward the distant imperial capital. Kaiser, Evergrant, and the corrupt lords of Avalon have thirty years to enjoy their thrones—because their reckoning has just arrived!

======================================================================
CHAPTER 02 VOICEOVER
======================================================================
CONTINUOUS RECORDING TEXT:
Waking up face-down in a pile of damp hay surrounded by the pungent perfume of fresh horse manure is definitely not on anyone's reincarnation bingo card. Joshua snaps his eyes open with an excruciating migraine that feels like a dwarven blacksmith hammering white-hot iron directly against his skull. In his previous life as the supreme champion of the empire, he fell asleep under royal chandeliers and woke up in silk sheets imported from the eastern continent. But right now? Cobwebs, rusty nails, and literal stable compost. Yet the longer he breathes in the dust, the faster a chilling sense of dread crawls up his spine. He jumps to his feet and sprints like a madman toward the stable entrance, throwing both hands forward and slamming the heavy oak doors wide open. Crisp morning sunlight blinds him for a split second before revealing an enormous estate that stretches as far as the eye can see. And right there, blanketing the palace grounds, are acres of legendary blue roses. Across the entire empire, only one royal house cultivates blue roses: the Duchy of Agnus. Joshua rushes over to a puddle on the cobblestone path to check the water's reflection. Gone is the scarred, battle-hardened veteran with bleached gray hair. Staring right back at him is a scrawny ten-year-old boy with dark blue locks, completely smooth skin, and piercing, abyss-like sapphire eyes. He didn't just survive the emperor's betrayal—he regressed over forty whole years into the past! And you want to know what this absolute menace does first? He doesn't shed a tear, he doesn't panic—our boy throws his head back into the sky and lets out the most unhinged villain laugh you have ever heard! Because think about the mathematical advantage here: Kaiser Van Britten isn't an untouchable emperor sitting atop an imperial fortress. Right now, that snake is just an insecure ten-year-old fourth prince getting scolded by royal tutors. The chessboard just reset, and Joshua has the first move. But before he can draft a twenty-year plan to dismantle the imperial bloodline, three local rent-a-cops show up to ruin the mood. Enter Rols, Roid, and Gort—three bottom-tier centurions who made it their personal life mission to make Joshua's childhood a living hell. In high noble estates, regular guards would never dare lay a finger on ducal blood. But Joshua was an illegitimate bastard born from Duke Aden forcefully bedding a maid named Lucia. To the Duke, Joshua was nothing more than an embarrassing byproduct of a drunken night. He gave them a barn stall to live in and never bothered looking back. Seeing that the boy had zero protection, these three centurions derived a sick, twisted pleasure from bullying someone carrying actual ducal blood. Gort barks at him for slacking off, while Roid laughs that the little trash can't even speak—because throughout his childhood, Joshua stayed dead silent to survive. But then Rols and Roid cross the line into absolute suicide territory. They start giggling about Duchess Vanessa's private guarantee. Bragging that the Duchess herself promised them complete legal immunity, even if they violated his mother Lucia and threw Joshua's corpse in a ditch. And right there, something inside Joshua's soul snaps. He originally planned to lie low, avoid drawing attention, and build an army in the shadows. But you do not talk about his mother. Lucia was the only pure light in his miserable early life, and these walking corpses just signed their death warrants. To understand what makes our boy ridiculous, look at how magic works in this realm. An average knight meditates five grueling years just to construct a mana hall below their navel. Anyone who can manipulate mana by age fifteen is celebrated across the continent as a generational genius whose name gets carved into kingdom history. But the ancient technique Joshua unsealed alongside Lugia operates on completely inverted logic. It doesn't accumulate mana in a stomach vessel—it draws mana directly through the flesh! It bypasses the five-year mana hall grind entirely. In his first life, it took Joshua ten painful years to awaken his first handful of energy because nobody taught him how. But today? The grandmaster's soul already knows every spiritual junction by heart. He just needs to coax a single handful of atmospheric mana into his knuckles. Thinking the mute kid is paralyzed with fright, Gort rolls his shoulders forward, complaining that he got rejected by a girl and needs to blow off some steam. He raises a heavy wooden club high into the morning air, fully intending to fracture the defenseless ten-year-old's ribs for cheap amusement. But right as the club rips downward at lethal velocity, Joshua's eyelids snap open with a brilliant flash of azure lightning. With veteran muscle memory that laughs at basic club swings, Joshua slips the descending weapon by a fraction of a single hair's breadth. He pivots his back foot, ignites raw pale blue mana around his tiny knuckles, and plants a counter-punch directly into Gort's exposed stomach. The sound that reverberates across the stables is like wet leather ripping in two. Gort can't even manage a groan before his two-hundred-pound frame collapses limp into the mud. While the two guards stand frozen in shock, Joshua suddenly feels something terribly wrong. A sticky, repulsive knot pulses inside the pit of his stomach. His ten-year-old body has never circulated energy before. His meridians are clogged, burning like molten lead. He calculates he has enough power for exactly one more strike. Tactically, Rols is a calculating rat who relies on his eyes rather than his blade. If Joshua takes down Roid, Rols will never have the courage to charge. Furious that a toddler knocked out his partner, Roid unsheathes his iron longsword with a screeching clang, shouting that he's going to carve the brat into pieces. The blade whistles in a lethal horizontal arc aimed directly at Joshua's throat. But our boy bends his spine backward like a coiled steel spring. The steel breezes right past his eyelashes. As Roid overextends his swing, he looks down and sees the boy's fist igniting in pure sapphire flame. Joshua snaps back upward with vicious momentum, driving an absolute textbook hundred-point uppercut directly into Roid's jaw! A brutal crunch echoes across the courtyard as Roid's jaw shatters on impact, launching his airborne body backward like a ragdoll. Two seasoned imperial centurions, both flattened within thirty seconds by an unarmed ten-year-old child who was supposedly mute. Rols stumbles backward in absolute panic. He realizes that knocking out Gort wasn't some lucky fluke—this child has been concealing monstrous combat power all along! But Joshua is hanging on by sheer willpower. His juvenile chest is heaving, his vision is darkening, and every muscle fiber is screaming in agonizing mana backlash. He knows that if Rols smells even an ounce of weakness, the guard might attack. So our boy straightens his spine and locks eyes with him. For the first time in ten years, Joshua opens his mouth and speaks with the chilling authority of an emperor delivering a royal decree. He tells Rols that from this exact moment forward, anyone—including every centurion in the estate—who dares insult his mother will never be spared. Rols once survived the horrific slaughter at the Battle of Kraden Plains. And the aura pouring off this ten-year-old child feels identical to the mythical Nine Stars of the continent! The terrified guard completely loses his mind, shrieking that the kid is a literal demon, and sprints away as fast as his legs can carry him. Joshua stands in silence until the guard's footsteps completely fade into the distance, maintaining his intimidating battle posture until the coast is clear. But the second Rols is gone, the severe backlash of forcing foreign mana through an untrained ten-year-old vessel crashes down like a tidal wave. His legs give out completely, face-planting into the turf with a muffled curse as his consciousness fades into total blackness. Two armed centurions knocked cold, a survivor spreading rumors of an awakened prodigy, and the ducal palace about to explode into sheer chaos.

[02:48 – 02:54] Shot 29: Thinking the mute kid is paralyzed with fright, Gort rolls his shoulders forward, complaining that he got rejected by a girl and needs to blow off some steam.
[02:54 – 03:00] Shot 30: He raises a heavy wooden club high into the morning air, fully intending to fracture the defenseless ten-year-old's ribs for cheap amusement.
[03:00 – 03:06] Shot 31: But right as the club rips downward at lethal velocity, Joshua's eyelids snap open with a brilliant flash of azure lightning.
[03:06 – 03:12] Shot 32: With veteran muscle memory that laughs at basic club swings, Joshua slips the descending weapon by a fraction of a single hair's breadth.
[03:12 – 03:18] Shot 33: He pivots his back foot, ignites raw pale blue mana around his tiny knuckles, and plants a counter-punch directly into Gort's exposed stomach.
[03:18 – 03:24] Shot 34: The sound that reverberates across the stables is like wet leather ripping in two. Gort can't even manage a groan before his two-hundred-pound frame collapses limp into the mud.
[03:24 – 03:30] Shot 35: While the two guards stand frozen in shock, Joshua suddenly feels something terribly wrong. A sticky, repulsive knot pulses inside the pit of his stomach.
[03:30 – 03:36] Shot 36: His ten-year-old body has never circulated energy before. His meridians are clogged, burning like molten lead. He calculates he has enough power for exactly one more strike.
[03:36 – 03:42] Shot 37: Tactically, Rols is a calculating rat who relies on his eyes rather than his blade. If Joshua takes down Roid, Rols will never have the courage to charge.
[03:42 – 03:48] Shot 38: Furious that a toddler knocked out his partner, Roid unsheathes his iron longsword with a screeching clang, shouting that he's going to carve the brat into pieces.
[03:48 – 03:54] Shot 39: The blade whistles in a lethal horizontal arc aimed directly at Joshua's throat. But our boy bends his spine backward like a coiled steel spring.
[03:54 – 04:00] Shot 40: The steel breezes right past his eyelashes. As Roid overextends his swing, he looks down and sees the boy's fist igniting in pure sapphire flame.
[04:00 – 04:06] Shot 41: Joshua snaps back upward with vicious momentum, driving an absolute textbook hundred-point uppercut directly into Roid's jaw!
[04:06 – 04:12] Shot 42: A brutal crunch echoes across the courtyard as Roid's jaw shatters on impact, launching his airborne body backward like a ragdoll.
[04:12 – 04:18] Shot 43: Two seasoned imperial centurions, both flattened within thirty seconds by an unarmed ten-year-old child who was supposedly mute.
[04:18 – 04:24] Shot 44: Rols stumbles backward in absolute panic. He realizes that knocking out Gort wasn't some lucky fluke—this child has been concealing monstrous combat power all along!
[04:24 – 04:30] Shot 45: But Joshua is hanging on by sheer willpower. His juvenile chest is heaving, his vision is darkening, and every muscle fiber is screaming in agonizing mana backlash.
[04:30 – 04:36] Shot 46: He knows that if Rols smells even an ounce of weakness, the guard might attack. So our boy straightens his spine and locks eyes with him.
[04:36 – 04:42] Shot 47: For the first time in ten years, Joshua opens his mouth and speaks with the chilling authority of an emperor delivering a royal decree.
[04:42 – 04:48] Shot 48: He tells Rols that from this exact moment forward, anyone—including every centurion in the estate—who dares insult his mother will never be spared.
[04:48 – 04:54] Shot 49: Rols once survived the horrific slaughter at the Battle of Kraden Plains. And the aura pouring off this ten-year-old child feels identical to the mythical Nine Stars of the continent!
[04:54 – 05:00] Shot 50: The terrified guard completely loses his mind, shrieking that the kid is a literal demon, and sprints away as fast as his legs can carry him.
[05:00 – 05:06] Shot 51: Joshua stands in silence until the guard's footsteps completely fade into the distance, maintaining his intimidating battle posture until the coast is clear.
[05:06 – 05:12] Shot 52: But the second Rols is gone, the severe backlash of forcing foreign mana through an untrained ten-year-old vessel crashes down like a tidal wave.
[05:12 – 05:18] Shot 53: His legs give out completely, face-planting into the turf with a muffled curse as his consciousness fades into total blackness.
[05:18 – 05:24] Shot 54: Two armed centurions knocked cold, a survivor spreading rumors of an awakened prodigy, and the ducal palace about to explode into sheer chaos.

======================================================================
CHAPTER 03 VOICEOVER
======================================================================
CONTINUOUS RECORDING TEXT:
In medieval high society, news travels fast—but scandalous castle rumors travel faster than lightning. By the time the morning bells ring across the Agnus Duchy—one of the five sovereign pillars holding up the Avalon Empire—the mansion is in absolute uproar. Down by the servant quarters, three kitchen workers—Paul, Brown, and Bob—are huddled together like gossiping teenagers trading top-secret intelligence. Paul drops the bombshell: that supposed mute orphan living out by the horse dung just pounded three seasoned imperial centurions into mashed rice cakes! Brown panics, hissing at Paul to watch his mouth. Because if the boy really has that kind of demonic combat skill, every servant who bullied him is in mortal danger. But Bob points out the most terrifying detail: Joshua is barely ten years old! That makes him four years younger than Master Babel, the family's crown prodigy. For perspective, the head of this family, Duke Aden von Agnus, is one of the legendary Nine Stars of the continent—a monster who swings a two-meter blade with one hand. Even the great Duke didn't awaken mana until age sixteen. So when his eldest son Babel unlocked mana at age fourteen, the empire declared him an immortal genius. Paul laughs it off, claiming the centurions must have faked the whole thing just to get closer to Joshua's gorgeous mother, Lucia. The cook starts bragging aloud about how he would love to get a taste of Lucia's body—completely unaware of the reaper walking up right behind him. Standing in the doorway with his back straight and shoulders squared like an imperial general is our boy Joshua, staring straight through their souls. Gone is the hunched, filthy boy who took beatings in silence. The crushing dignity rolling off him makes the three grown men choke on their own spit. In a voice completely devoid of childhood fear, Joshua gives them a single directive: go to the master's study and deliver a message. 'Tell the Duke that on his summons... I, Joshua von Agnus, have arrived.' He doesn't call himself a bastard—he claims the ducal name without flinching. Meanwhile, up in the Duke's inner sanctum, the atmosphere is heavy enough to snap iron. Flanked by Count Chiffon—the ruthless commander of the elite Red Knights—Duke Aden is staring down at Centurion Rols, who is trembling on his belly. Aden confirms Rols's rank in the 29th Battalion with a quiet, terrifying calm, before gesturing toward the evidence lying on the table. Resting on the desk is Rols's issued cuirass, crafted from the bulletproof hides of northern black orcs. But the breastplate is blown wide open like paper! Duke Aden runs his fingers along the torn leather, asking with dangerous stillness if a nine-year-old child really shattered black orc armor with his bare knuckles. Rols sobs that he isn't lying, but Count Chiffon refuses to buy a single word of it. Chiffon points out that even high-tier B-class knights need full-body mana infusion to tear orc hide. A nine-year-old awakening mana is completely unprecedented in empire history! The Red Knight commander concludes that Rols is spinning a fairy tale to cover up some shady criminal blunder, and offers to drag him outside for immediate execution. Rols weeps in terror, begging for mercy—until Duke Aden speaks three words that freeze everyone in their tracks: 'That is enough, Chiffon.' Duke Aden stands up, his towering two-meter frame casting a shadow that swallows the entire room. He decides he will inspect the boy with his own two eyes. Chiffon is stunned speechless. Even the legitimate heir Babel never gets personal visits from the Duke! Yet Aden is going down to see an illegitimate stable boy. The Duke strides out of his office with Chiffon trailing right behind, leaving a puddle of yellow shame underneath the sobbing centurion. Down in the grand corridor, our boy Joshua is standing motionless in front of an ornate oil painting of Emperor Marcus Van Britton. Looking at the imperial features, Joshua's mind immediately superimposes Kaiser's smug, treacherous face over the canvas. The sheer fury of being butchered like a dog in his past life bubbles up, and an oppressive heat haze begins leaking from his juvenile frame. But right in the middle of his emotional breakdown, someone decides to interrupt. Enter fourteen-year-old Babel von Agnus, dressed in fine silk. Babel scoffs in disgust, demanding to know what business a manure-shoveling bastard has inside the main palace corridor. Joshua doesn't even blink. He treats the family's prized heir like empty space, which sends Babel's fragile teenage ego into absolute orbit. Screaming that a peasant will pay for ignoring him, Babel winds up and throws an arrogant sucker punch straight at the back of Joshua's head! Big mistake. Joshua doesn't even flinch. He reaches behind his shoulder and snatches Babel's incoming punch right out of the air like catching a fly! And then our boy slowly tightens his fingers. A horrific series of bone-crunching pops echoes down the vaulted hallway: crack, crack, crack! Babel drops to his knees in sheer agony. His prized knuckles are being crushed into dust, and the realization hits him: the stable rumors were one hundred percent real! Humiliated beyond belief, Babel ignites mana into his free fist, preparing to incinerate his little brother where he stands. But before mana can erupt, a thunderous voice shakes the foundations of the palace: 'WHAT DO YOU THINK YOU ARE DOING?!' The Duke has arrived. Babel immediately snuffs out his flame, drops both knees to the marble floor, and stammers that they were merely sharing fraternal greetings. Aden doesn't even look at his kneeling heir. His piercing gaze locks straight onto Joshua, waiting to see how the illegitimate son will greet his sovereign lord. And our boy Joshua? He doesn't kneel. He doesn't prostrate. He gives Duke Aden a casual, respectful nod—like two guild masters crossing paths on the road! Count Chiffon loses his absolute mind, screaming if the kid is insane for not bowing before one of the five continental Masters. But Duke Aden is fascinated. He raises a hand to quiet Chiffon, taking slow, deliberate steps toward Joshua until their boots are inches apart. Joshua forces his racing pulse to slow down. Aden is a Master whose sensory acuity can detect an errant heartbeat from fifty paces. The Duke asks the million-dollar question: 'Is it true that you manipulated mana?' From the floor, Babel grits his teeth until blood seeps from his gums. To Babel, the thought of his peasant half-brother having magic is an unforgivable blasphemy. But Aden isn't interested in words—he wants physical proof. Faster than human eyes can track, Duke Aden's white-gloved hand clamps around Joshua's slender wrist like a vice! A concentrated river of pure Master-level mana floods into Joshua's arm, scanning every meridian, blood vessel, and spiritual node in his body. At first, Aden expects to find an empty vessel. But as his energy pushes deeper toward the boy's lower abdomen, the Duke's stone-cold face completely shatters. The Duke gasps aloud, his grip trembling. In his entire four decades of warfare, he has never encountered an internal anatomy like this! Resting right where the mana hall should be is a monstrous, ancient blockage—a temporal seal left behind by Lugia's dimensional regression! To Count Chiffon and Babel, seeing the continent's greatest warlord look terrified is more shocking than an enemy army invading the castle. Aden stammers in disbelief: 'This is...!' He knows that according to standard imperial medicine, this boy should be a crippled invalid who can't even stand. Yet this supposed invalid just shattered orc hide armor with his bare knuckles, forcing the greatest Master in the empire to question everything he knows.

======================================================================
CHAPTER 04 VOICEOVER
======================================================================
CONTINUOUS RECORDING TEXT:
Right in the middle of the grand palace corridor, Duke Aden von Agnus has his iron grip clamped around our boy's wrist, scanning his internal mana channels. But instead of finding the roaring aura of a martial genius, the Duke's stoic warlord expression completely shatters into disbelief. The Duke mutters in utter astonishment: 'There is nothing... not a single drop of mana inside this child.' From the floor, Babel instantly smirks like a spoiled brat who just got handed a win, while Count Chiffon chuckles that the rumors were pure nonsense. But Duke Aden cuts them off immediately. He isn't talking about a missing mana hall; in this world, every living creature possesses baseline biological mana! Commoners have it, woodland rabbits have it, even magical beasts have it. A total zero-mana biological signature only belongs to one thing: a walking undead corpse. And right where Joshua's lower abdomen should circulate energy, the Duke detects something foul, sticky, and revolting—a bizarre temporal blockage. Count Chiffon brings up the dreaded 'Congenital Mana Disorder'—a rare one-in-a-hundred-thousand curse where children are born unable to store mana, guaranteed to die before eighteen. But Duke Aden is no ordinary knight. He knows a true cripple could never fracture northern black orc skin with bare hands. Something far deeper is happening here. So the Duke turns on his heel and drops a bombshell: 'Prepare a private guest room in the main mansion. Joshua will stay here from now on.' Babel loses his composure, shouting that an illegitimate servant's son has no right to live in the ducal mansion! But Aden's icy glare shuts him up instantly. 'Is there an issue, Babel?' Aden asks with razor-sharp quietness. Babel swallows his golden pride, lowers his head, and mumbles that there is no problem at all. Next, the Duke commands Count Chiffon to oversee Joshua's basic training starting tomorrow. And if the boy shows aptitude, to teach him the Red Knights' secret arts! Before departing, Aden locks eyes with Joshua one last time, murmuring that he looks forward to seeing what becomes of him when he returns from the capital in one year. Count Chiffon escorts Joshua down the executive wing to a private VIP room that is larger and more lavish than an entire peasant family estate. Chiffon is visibly annoyed that this former stable boy doesn't even show a shred of gratitude for living like royalty, before slamming the door shut. Left alone, our boy lets his small frame sink deep into the plush feather mattress, exhaling a heavy breath as the setting sun paints the room crimson. He replays Duke Aden's parting words. The Duke spends nearly all his time in the imperial capital of Arcadia serving the crown. Why would he set a one-year deadline? In his previous life, having his secrets known was the leash they used to enslave him. If he wants absolute sovereignty, he needs his grandmaster strength back—fast. He avoided visiting his mother during the day to prevent Duchess Vanessa's spies from targeting her. But once the castle clock strikes midnight, our boy makes his move. He vaults over the marble railing, dropping three stories into the moonlit gardens with the silent grace of a ghost stepping on silk. Touching down in the shadows, he tests his internal energy flow. The muscular soreness from earlier has subsided, but the second he pulls mana inward... ...the repulsive knot in his core detonates like a powder keg! Joshua drops to one knee, clutching his stomach as two opposing divine and demonic forces collide! Right in the center of the spiritual storm, the phantom silhouette of his demonic spear Lugia flares to life, actively suppressing both warring energies! Lugia didn't just reverse his age—it bound divine and demonic powers together in his soul! If Joshua learns to control all three, he will transcend mortal knight limits entirely. Hearing patrolling sentries approach, Joshua slips past the torchlight like a shadow, sprinting straight toward his old childhood barn to see his mother. But just as the weathered wooden cabin comes into sight, a blood-curdling scream rips through the midnight silence: 'KYAAAA! Let go of me!' It is his mother Lucia! Every ounce of rational restraint burns away in a fraction of a microsecond as our boy bolts toward the doorway. Inside the dimly lit barn, centurion Roid is forcibly pinning weeping Lucia against the wooden floorboards, while Gort steps forward unsheathing a hunting knife! Gort spits venom, screaming that because her demonic brat humiliated them, their military standing was ruined, and he is going to make her pay in blood! Gort rips Lucia's collar, while Rols frantically begs him to stop, terrified that the ten-year-old monster from this morning might walk through that door. Gort shoves Rols to the ground, roaring that Duchess Vanessa herself promised them total immunity. If Rols is terrified of a toddler, he can get lost! Gort raises the knife to Lucia's throat, grinning like a rabid animal—until a freezing chill drops the barn temperature below freezing. A single word echoes across the barn with the crushing resonance of an executioner's bell: 'STOP.' Standing in the moonlit doorway is Joshua, shrouded in an overwhelming crimson aura so thick you could carve it with a broadsword. In a voice drenched in absolute slaughter, he delivers a verdict: 'Don't even dream of walking out of this room alive.' Rols practically weeps in terror, screaming that the demon has returned. But Gort stubbornly tries to laugh it off, barking that Joshua is just an unarmed kid. Joshua takes three slow, deliberate steps forward, and utters two chilling words: 'Get lost.' Roid sneers, thinking the boy is talking to them. But Joshua wasn't looking at the centurions—his eyes are locked directly onto the ceiling rafters! 'Oh? How on earth did you manage to sense me?' a suave voice chuckles from above as a cloaked figure drops lightly to the floorboards. Standing before them is a devastatingly handsome warrior with sharp amber eyes and a rapier at his hip. The centurions choke on their breath: 'SIR CAIN?!' Glinting on his chest is the legendary Golden Red Cross—marking him as one of only three supreme champions appointed directly by Duke Aden himself! Joshua deduces that Count Chiffon sent this elite stalker to watch him. The three guards quickly scramble into clumsy salutes, praying Cain won't intervene. Cain studies Joshua with fascinated amusement, wondering how a ten-year-old detected his master-tier stealth when even veteran knights couldn't. But our boy doesn't act like a frightened child begging for rescue. He steps toward Cain and poses a calculated legal question. 'These soldiers attempted to assault a member under the Duke's direct protection. According to the Duke's martial penal code... how do we handle this?' As Joshua speaks, he unleashes the soul pressure of an unvanquished Grandmaster who conquered empires in his previous life! Sir Cain's breath hitches. His knees physically buckle under the tyrannical gravity of this ten-year-old child's sheer presence! The playful smirk vanishes from Cain's face. He realizes he isn't speaking to a noble concubine's bastard—he is answering an absolute monarch. Cain draws his sword an inch from its scabbard, delivering the law with chilling finality: 'Under the Duke's law... it would be IMMEDIATE EXECUTION.' The centurions freeze in paralyzing terror as they realize Duchess Vanessa's promises of immunity mean nothing in front of Duke Aden's personal executioner. Sir Cain drops into a lethal low-stance lunge, scarlet Red Knight aura igniting along his steel blade like a butcher's fire. While the crimson execution begins behind him, Joshua kneels beside his trembling mother, wrapping his arm around her shoulders to shield her eyes. The past life of helplessness is over. The bullies will bleed, the elite Red Knight will be bent into Joshua's first loyal vassal, and the real war has only just begun.

[01:12 – 01:18] Shot 13: Next, the Duke commands Count Chiffon to oversee Joshua's basic training starting tomorrow. And if the boy shows aptitude, to teach him the Red Knights' secret arts!
[01:18 – 01:24] Shot 14: Before departing, Aden locks eyes with Joshua one last time, murmuring that he looks forward to seeing what becomes of him when he returns from the capital in one year.
[01:24 – 01:30] Shot 15: Count Chiffon escorts Joshua down the executive wing to a private VIP room that is larger and more lavish than an entire peasant family estate.
[01:30 – 01:36] Shot 16: Chiffon is visibly annoyed that this former stable boy doesn't even show a shred of gratitude for living like royalty, before slamming the door shut.
[01:36 – 01:42] Shot 17: Left alone, our boy lets his small frame sink deep into the plush feather mattress, exhaling a heavy breath as the setting sun paints the room crimson.
[01:42 – 01:48] Shot 18: He replays Duke Aden's parting words. The Duke spends nearly all his time in the imperial capital of Arcadia serving the crown. Why would he set a one-year deadline?
[01:48 – 01:54] Shot 19: In his previous life, having his secrets known was the leash they used to enslave him. If he wants absolute sovereignty, he needs his grandmaster strength back—fast.
[01:54 – 02:00] Shot 20: He avoided visiting his mother during the day to prevent Duchess Vanessa's spies from targeting her. But once the castle clock strikes midnight, our boy makes his move.
[02:00 – 02:06] Shot 21: He vaults over the marble railing, dropping three stories into the moonlit gardens with the silent grace of a ghost stepping on silk.
[02:06 – 02:12] Shot 22: Touching down in the shadows, he tests his internal energy flow. The muscular soreness from earlier has subsided, but the second he pulls mana inward...
[02:12 – 02:18] Shot 23: ...the repulsive knot in his core detonates like a powder keg! Joshua drops to one knee, clutching his stomach as two opposing divine and demonic forces collide!
[02:18 – 02:24] Shot 24: Right in the center of the spiritual storm, the phantom silhouette of his demonic spear Lugia flares to life, actively suppressing both warring energies!
[02:24 – 02:30] Shot 25: Lugia didn't just reverse his age—it bound divine and demonic powers together in his soul! If Joshua learns to control all three, he will transcend mortal knight limits entirely.
[02:30 – 02:36] Shot 26: Hearing patrolling sentries approach, Joshua slips past the torchlight like a shadow, sprinting straight toward his old childhood barn to see his mother.
[02:36 – 02:42] Shot 27: But just as the weathered wooden cabin comes into sight, a blood-curdling scream rips through the midnight silence: 'KYAAAA! Let go of me!'
[02:42 – 02:48] Shot 28: It is his mother Lucia! Every ounce of rational restraint burns away in a fraction of a microsecond as our boy bolts toward the doorway.
[02:48 – 02:54] Shot 29: Inside the dimly lit barn, centurion Roid is forcibly pinning weeping Lucia against the wooden floorboards, while Gort steps forward unsheathing a hunting knife!
[02:54 – 03:00] Shot 30: Gort spits venom, screaming that because her demonic brat humiliated them, their military standing was ruined, and he is going to make her pay in blood!
[03:00 – 03:06] Shot 31: Gort rips Lucia's collar, while Rols frantically begs him to stop, terrified that the ten-year-old monster from this morning might walk through that door.
[03:06 – 03:12] Shot 32: Gort shoves Rols to the ground, roaring that Duchess Vanessa herself promised them total immunity. If Rols is terrified of a toddler, he can get lost!
[03:12 – 03:18] Shot 33: Gort raises the knife to Lucia's throat, grinning like a rabid animal—until a freezing chill drops the barn temperature below freezing.
[03:18 – 03:24] Shot 34: A single word echoes across the barn with the crushing resonance of an executioner's bell: 'STOP.'
[03:24 – 03:30] Shot 35: Standing in the moonlit doorway is Joshua, shrouded in an overwhelming crimson aura so thick you could carve it with a broadsword.
[03:30 – 03:36] Shot 36: In a voice drenched in absolute slaughter, he delivers a verdict: 'Don't even dream of walking out of this room alive.'
[03:36 – 03:42] Shot 37: Rols practically weeps in terror, screaming that the demon has returned. But Gort stubbornly tries to laugh it off, barking that Joshua is just an unarmed kid.
[03:42 – 03:48] Shot 38: Joshua takes three slow, deliberate steps forward, and utters two chilling words: 'Get lost.'
[03:48 – 03:54] Shot 39: Roid sneers, thinking the boy is talking to them. But Joshua wasn't looking at the centurions—his eyes are locked directly onto the ceiling rafters!
[03:54 – 04:00] Shot 40: 'Oh? How on earth did you manage to sense me?' a suave voice chuckles from above as a cloaked figure drops lightly to the floorboards.
[04:00 – 04:06] Shot 41: Standing before them is a devastatingly handsome warrior with sharp amber eyes and a rapier at his hip. The centurions choke on their breath: 'SIR CAIN?!'
[04:06 – 04:12] Shot 42: Glinting on his chest is the legendary Golden Red Cross—marking him as one of only three supreme champions appointed directly by Duke Aden himself!
[04:12 – 04:18] Shot 43: Joshua deduces that Count Chiffon sent this elite stalker to watch him. The three guards quickly scramble into clumsy salutes, praying Cain won't intervene.
[04:18 – 04:24] Shot 44: Cain studies Joshua with fascinated amusement, wondering how a ten-year-old detected his master-tier stealth when even veteran knights couldn't.
[04:24 – 04:30] Shot 45: But our boy doesn't act like a frightened child begging for rescue. He steps toward Cain and poses a calculated legal question.
[04:30 – 04:36] Shot 46: 'These soldiers attempted to assault a member under the Duke's direct protection. According to the Duke's martial penal code... how do we handle this?'
[04:36 – 04:42] Shot 47: As Joshua speaks, he unleashes the soul pressure of an unvanquished Grandmaster who conquered empires in his previous life!
[04:42 – 04:48] Shot 48: Sir Cain's breath hitches. His knees physically buckle under the tyrannical gravity of this ten-year-old child's sheer presence!
[04:48 – 04:54] Shot 49: The playful smirk vanishes from Cain's face. He realizes he isn't speaking to a noble concubine's bastard—he is answering an absolute monarch.
[04:54 – 05:00] Shot 50: Cain draws his sword an inch from its scabbard, delivering the law with chilling finality: 'Under the Duke's law... it would be IMMEDIATE EXECUTION.'
[05:00 – 05:06] Shot 51: The centurions freeze in paralyzing terror as they realize Duchess Vanessa's promises of immunity mean nothing in front of Duke Aden's personal executioner.
[05:06 – 05:12] Shot 52: Sir Cain drops into a lethal low-stance lunge, scarlet Red Knight aura igniting along his steel blade like a butcher's fire.
[05:12 – 05:18] Shot 53: While the crimson execution begins behind him, Joshua kneels beside his trembling mother, wrapping his arm around her shoulders to shield her eyes.
[05:18 – 05:24] Shot 54: The past life of helplessness is over. The bullies will bleed, the elite Red Knight will be bent into Joshua's first loyal vassal, and the real war has only just begun.

======================================================================
CHAPTER 05 VOICEOVER
======================================================================
CONTINUOUS RECORDING TEXT:
If there is one rule in the Agnus estate you never break, it's this: when an elite Red Knight unsheathes steel, bodies are about to hit the floor. With a single fluid step that barely disturbs the air, Sir Cain's rapier flashes in an arc of scarlet mana, cleaving Gort open before he can blink. Roid tries to swing his broadsword in wild panic, but Cain parries the blade with a deafening metallic clang that rattles Roid's teeth. Cain's counter-thrust pierces clean through Roid's chest, leaving the second centurion gurgling apologies into a pool of his own crimson blood. In under five seconds, two armed thugs are erased from existence. Cain turns his icy amber eyes toward the last surviving coward: Rols. Rols sobs and claws at the floorboards as Cain raises his sword for the decapitation blow—until a calm voice cuts through the barn: 'STOP.' Cain's blade halts a hair's breadth from Rols's throat. The Red Knight frowns, reminding Joshua that insulting ducal kin requires total execution. But our boy Joshua is already analyzing Cain's fencing form. Young, disciplined, and clearly the strongest C-rank talent in the Red Knight order. Instead of thanking him for the rescue, Joshua looks Cain dead in the eyes and asks two simple questions that shift the entire balance of power. 'Who is your master?' Joshua asks. Cain puffs his chest with military pride, answering that he serves only Duke Aden von Agnus. Then our boy points a thumb at his own chest and drops the hammer: 'Then who am I?' The room goes dead silent. The unspoken subtext hits Cain like a physical blow: *I carry Duke Aden's blood. If you serve the Duke, you obey me.* 'Sheathe your sword,' Joshua commands. Cain hesitates for a heartbeat, then slowly slides his blade home with a crisp metallic click. Seeing his life spared, Rols frantically bangs his head against the wood, cutting his own forehead while swearing eternal loyalty to Joshua. But our boy scoffs in cold disgust. He didn't spare Rols out of mercy, and he certainly doesn't want the loyalty of an unprincipled turncoat. Joshua gives him a single job: run back to the barracks and tell every single soldier in the 40,000-man army exactly what happened here tonight. And if anyone else in this estate dares bare their fangs at his mother again, Joshua won't summon a knight—he will tear their pathetic lives apart himself! Rols scrambles out of the barn on all fours, shrieking in pure terror and sprinting into the darkness like the devil himself is biting his heels. Once the coward is gone, Sir Cain crosses his arms and chuckles, asking if this poised warlord is really the manure-shoveling mute from the rumors. Joshua laughs it off: 'The Duke's mistake? The common maid's bastard? Believe whatever rumors help you sleep at night.' But Cain doesn't laugh. He explains the ancient imperial code: at age twenty-three, every knight of the realm has the sacred right to choose which master they will serve for life. Cain reveals that his choice arrives next year. And while common sense says to back the legitimate heir Babel, Cain trusts his instincts: in one year, he will pledge his sword to Joshua! Before Joshua can even reply, Cain vanishes into the midnight air. Our boy shakes his head with a smirk: 'What an absolute oddball.' A soft, fragile voice pulls him back to reality: 'J-Joshua?!' Lucia slowly opens her emerald eyes, sitting up from the straw. She throws her arms around him, weeping that her baby is safe. And right there, the cold, ruthless Grandmaster melts into a sobbing child in his mother's embrace. In his previous timeline, she died cold and alone from illness when he was fourteen. But this time, he thanks the heavens for a second chance—he will protect her with his life. Stepping out under the moonlight, our boy reaffirms his true identity: he is no longer the timid stable boy—he is Joshua Sanders, the Strongest Spear Knight in human history! Meanwhile, back at the main ducal palace, high-stakes political maneuvering is already brewing. Count Chiffon informs Duke Aden that an influential guest has arrived unannounced: Count Ponselle Jin Rebreka, head of an imperial magic powerhouse. Even more surprising, the Count didn't come alone—he brought his prized daughter, the young Lady Isellin Jin Rebreka. While Count Rebreka holds lower noble rank than a Great Duke, his family's magical research and political clout across Avalon cannot be ignored. And his nine-year-old daughter, Isellin, is the talk of the entire empire. She is an unprecedented magic prodigy who reached 2nd-Class magic by age nine! If congenital mana disorder is the continent's worst curse, then Isellin received the divine opposite: the legendary 'Mana Blessing,' allowing her to commune with nature effortlessly. Chiffon assumes Rebreka is here to pitch a political marriage for Master Babel, who turns fifteen next year and needs an illustrious bride for his coming-of-age ceremony. But Duke Aden immediately shakes his head. Babel is already informally promised to Lady Cheryl of the Pontier family—a house with bottomless mercantile wealth. The Duke stops dead in his tracks. Count Chiffon turns back, wondering why his lord is staring off into space. Then Duke Aden drops a totally unexpected question: 'How is Joshua doing?' Chiffon stammers in confusion. Why on earth is the Duke asking about the concubine's cripple when imperial nobility is waiting in the parlor? Duke Aden flashes a wicked smirk and drops the biggest bombshell in ducal history: 'I'm thinking of betrothing Lady Isellin to Joshua.' Count Chiffon's brain practically short-circuits. He screams aloud, asking if his lord has completely lost his mind! 'You want to marry Lady Isellin—who received the supreme Mana Blessing—to a stable boy suffering from the terminal Mana Curse?!' Aden doesn't explain his logic. A continental Master judges value through raw combat results, not superstitious gossip. Little does our boy know he's being drafted into a political power play, because right now, Joshua is focused on an ancient mystery. Thousands of years ago, before the continent split into East and West, the mighty Aslan Empire ruled the world from its capital, Iris—which lies buried beneath the Agnus estate! Only Joshua knows this secret. North of the stables, right behind a house-sized boulder, lies a hidden entrance down into the forgotten abyss. He slips into the fissure before dawn breaks, descending ancient stone stairs worn smooth by forgotten millennia. As the air grows heavy with primordial mana, Joshua whispers a single name: 'Lugia.' The very weapon that made him the continent's apex predator. The narrow tunnel suddenly opens up into a jaw-dropping cavern the size of a mountain valley, illuminated by pulsating ancient runes. Sprawling before his eyes are the colossal ruins of Iris—majestic white marble bridges spanning bottomless chasms and monolithic cathedrals untouched by time. Standing like a tiny speck in the colossal subterranean metropolis, our boy walks forward with zero hesitation. At the end of the sacred plaza stands a soaring altar. And inside that altar rests the legendary red spear that chose him as its master. The closer he steps, the more his internal energy lump stabilizes—humming in perfect harmony with the dormant relic waiting above. In his previous life, finding this took him decades of hardship. But today, at age ten, the prodigy claims his god-slaying weapon on day one. The spear that conquered an empire is about to be unsealed forty years early—and the entire continent's destiny is about to shatter.

======================================================================
CHAPTER 06 VOICEOVER
======================================================================
CONTINUOUS RECORDING TEXT:
High in the northern garden of the Agnus estate sits a massive five-meter boulder known to guards as the sacred 'Guardian Stone.' In his previous life, after his mother died, young Joshua would climb this forbidden rock every night just to talk to the stars and pretend she was listening. Running his fingers across the top, he locates a microscopic needle-thin crevice—the secret trigger to the forgotten subterranean capital of Iris. Without hesitating, our boy bites his right thumb, letting drops of fresh crimson blood drip down into the ancient mechanism. The rock shudders as concentric ancient runes flare with blinding cyan light, tearing open a spatial rift right in front of him! Joshua dives straight into the swirling vortex, letting the spatial currents carry his small body down into the forgotten abyss beneath the castle. He touches down inside the cavernous temple of Iris, but immediately pauses. The grand hall is cracked, weathered, and covered in millennia of dust. In his previous life, he found this place at age fifteen, where his demonic spear Lugia stood bound in sacred silver chains atop the central altar. But reaching the pedestal, his jaw drops in utter disbelief. There is no demonic spear. Resting on the cold stone is a rusty, plain iron rod! Our boy stares in total confusion: did someone rebuild the temple over the next six years, or is this battered metal stick really his weapon? The exact millisecond his skin touches the iron, an explosion of blinding white radiance detonates, pulling his soul into another dimension! Joshua blinks his eyes open to find himself standing in an infinite, pure white mental space where neither shadows nor sound exist. 'Over here,' a deep, timeless voice echoes from behind. Joshua spins around, bracing for battle as a pillar of dark smoke coalesces in the void. Floating inside the dark mist is the iron rod. But before Joshua can call it a stick, the weapon speaks with thunderous majesty. 'I am not an iron rod,' the weapon rumbles. 'I am the Demon God's Spirit, Lugia!' The realization hits our boy: when Lugia used its life essence to rewind forty years of continental time, its godly form degraded into this primordial state! Lugia warns him that time is rapidly expiring. The two opposing divine and demonic energies inside his ten-year-old body are slowly devouring his lifespan. There is only one way to harmonize the warring powers and unseal his dormant god-tier strength: Joshua must reach Magic Spear Arts Stage 4 in record time! To understand why Stage 4 is ridiculous, look at how martial rankings work in the Avalon Empire. Nobles treat swordsmanship as the only sacred path, mocking spears as flimsy sticks meant for disposable commoner conscripts. A warrior who can materialize pure mana into an aura blade is ranked as an A-class Master—an 'Absolute Being' like Duke Aden. Across the continent, only five exist. In his past life, Joshua achieved Stage 5—the pinnacle of the Magic Spear Arts—becoming the undisputed strongest human being alive with a weapon nobles laughed at. Stage 4 is the equivalent of an imperial Master. While our boy already knows the techniques by heart, cultivating without an active mana hall should be impossible. But Lugia smirks: 'Do not worry. As long as you wield me, I will regulate your core, allowing you to channel mana with zero physical recoil!' With a brilliant flash, Joshua's consciousness snaps back into reality. His hands are firmly clamped around the iron rod, and the connection is electric! The agonizing knot in his abdomen instantly subsides. Atmospheric mana pours through his arms like a rushing waterfall, completely regulated by Lugia's soul. He drops into a low combat stance, spinning the heavy iron rod around his back with the effortless fluidity of an immortal spear divinity. He executes the opening foundation of the ancient art: Magic Spear Arts Stage 1! He condenses his entire mana reserve into a single microscopic point at the rod's tip. And thrusts! An explosive azure shockwave detonates with the force of an artillery shell, tearing a supersonic sonic boom clean across the subterranean temple! Dust and shattered stone rain down as our boy exhales a vapor breath, grinning from ear to ear. Stage 1 is mastered on his very first attempt! 'This is where it truly begins,' Joshua whispers into the darkness. 'Kaiser Van Britton... I will grow stronger, reclaim my throne, and tear your empire to ash.' Meanwhile, up in the lavish ducal reception hall, the atmosphere is dripping with aristocratic tension. Duke Aden and Count Chiffon enter with military precision, but Aden immediately halts. The reception hall holds an unexpected crowd. Standing near the window is Princess Cheryl of the Pontier family—a fiery red-haired prodigy whose family controls the empire's largest merchant fleet. Beside her, Count Lok Roman Pontier—the financial brain of the Pontier clan—frantically bows, apologizing that Princess Cheryl was too impatient to wait for an invitation. Duke Aden laughs it off with characteristic swagger, casually joking that if they feel that guilty, they should just pledge their entire fortune to the Agnus family! Cheryl playfully scoffs that the Duke is as greedy as ever, trying to rob another noble house of its crown jewel in broad daylight. She cuts straight to business, asking if it's true that young Babel reached C-rank knight status at age fourteen, hinting that she wants to recruit him as her future consort. Roman panics over the girl's bluntness, but Aden waves it off, enjoying her sharp wit—before turning his gaze across the opposite side of the hall. His expression shifts from grandfatherly amusement to cold political calculation as his eyes land on the second delegation waiting in the wings. 'My apologies, Ponselle,' Aden says with smooth mockery. 'I was so distracted by the Pontier family, I nearly forgot you were standing there.' Standing beside the Count is young Lady Isellin, dressed in an immaculate navy gown with turquoise hair that seems to pulse with natural ambient mana. Even at nine years old, her mana control is breathtaking. Duke Aden can feel the atmospheric spirits bowing to her presence like a little magic queen. Count Rebreka came here with one singular objective: to link his magical dynasty to Duke Agnus's military juggernaut through a marriage alliance. Aden looks at Princess Cheryl, then looks at Lady Isellin. The pieces on the continental chessboard are falling into place faster than anyone anticipated. If Babel is promised to the gold of House Pontier, then pairing Lady Isellin's god-given Mana Blessing with Joshua's mysterious power creates an unstoppable faction. Deep beneath the palace foundations, oblivious to the political storm brewing above his head, our boy Joshua rests his iron rod against his palm. He runs his thumb across the iron rod. One thin layer of rust has already flaked off, revealing the faint demonic crimson sheen of Lugia's true body! Every stage of the Magic Spear Arts he unlocks will restore a portion of Lugia's god-tier form until the legendary red weapon is fully reborn. The first rays of morning sun filter down through the ceiling fissures. Time is up—knight training with Count Chiffon begins at daybreak. He climbs the ancient stairs, returning to the surface world not as a broken concubine's bastard, but as an awakened dragon sharpening its claws. Across the estate, the Red Knights are gathering for morning drills, unaware that their newest recruit is about to dismantle their entire worldview. Babel is nursing his bruised knuckles, waiting for a chance to humiliate his little brother during formal combat training. An illegitimate son carrying a rusty rod, a furious heir thirsty for revenge, and the first official spar that will set the entire empire on fire.

TIMESTAMPS:
[00:00 – 00:06] Shot 01: High in the northern garden of the Agnus estate sits a massive five-meter boulder known to guards as the sacred 'Guardian Stone.'
[00:06 – 00:12] Shot 02: In his previous life, after his mother died, young Joshua would climb this forbidden rock every night just to talk to the stars and pretend she was listening.
[00:12 – 00:18] Shot 03: Running his fingers across the top, he locates a microscopic needle-thin crevice—the secret trigger to the forgotten subterranean capital of Iris.
[00:18 – 00:24] Shot 04: Without hesitating, our boy bites his right thumb, letting drops of fresh crimson blood drip down into the ancient mechanism.
[00:24 – 00:30] Shot 05: The rock shudders as concentric ancient runes flare with blinding cyan light, tearing open a spatial rift right in front of him!
[00:30 – 00:36] Shot 06: Joshua dives straight into the swirling vortex, letting the spatial currents carry his small body down into the forgotten abyss beneath the castle.
[00:36 – 00:42] Shot 07: He touches down inside the cavernous temple of Iris, but immediately pauses. The grand hall is cracked, weathered, and covered in millennia of dust.
[00:42 – 00:48] Shot 08: In his previous life, he found this place at age fifteen, where his demonic spear Lugia stood bound in sacred silver chains atop the central altar.
[00:48 – 00:54] Shot 09: But reaching the pedestal, his jaw drops in utter disbelief. There is no demonic spear. Resting on the cold stone is a rusty, plain iron rod!
[00:54 – 01:00] Shot 10: Our boy stares in total confusion: did someone rebuild the temple over the next six years, or is this battered metal stick really his weapon?
[01:00 – 01:06] Shot 11: The exact millisecond his skin touches the iron, an explosion of blinding white radiance detonates, pulling his soul into another dimension!
[01:06 – 01:12] Shot 12: Joshua blinks his eyes open to find himself standing in an infinite, pure white mental space where neither shadows nor sound exist.
[01:12 – 01:18] Shot 13: 'Over here,' a deep, timeless voice echoes from behind. Joshua spins around, bracing for battle as a pillar of dark smoke coalesces in the void.
[01:18 – 01:24] Shot 14: Floating inside the dark mist is the iron rod. But before Joshua can call it a stick, the weapon speaks with thunderous majesty.
[01:24 – 01:30] Shot 15: 'I am not an iron rod,' the weapon rumbles. 'I am the Demon God's Spirit, Lugia!'
[01:30 – 01:36] Shot 16: The realization hits our boy: when Lugia used its life essence to rewind forty years of continental time, its godly form degraded into this primordial state!
[01:36 – 01:42] Shot 17: Lugia warns him that time is rapidly expiring. The two opposing divine and demonic energies inside his ten-year-old body are slowly devouring his lifespan.
[01:42 – 01:48] Shot 18: There is only one way to harmonize the warring powers and unseal his dormant god-tier strength: Joshua must reach Magic Spear Arts Stage 4 in record time!
[01:48 – 01:54] Shot 19: To understand why Stage 4 is ridiculous, look at how martial rankings work in the Avalon Empire.
[01:54 – 02:00] Shot 20: Nobles treat swordsmanship as the only sacred path, mocking spears as flimsy sticks meant for disposable commoner conscripts.
[02:00 – 02:06] Shot 21: A warrior who can materialize pure mana into an aura blade is ranked as an A-class Master—an 'Absolute Being' like Duke Aden. Across the continent, only five exist.
[02:06 – 02:12] Shot 22: In his past life, Joshua achieved Stage 5—the pinnacle of the Magic Spear Arts—becoming the undisputed strongest human being alive with a weapon nobles laughed at.
[02:12 – 02:18] Shot 23: Stage 4 is the equivalent of an imperial Master. While our boy already knows the techniques by heart, cultivating without an active mana hall should be impossible.
[02:18 – 02:24] Shot 24: But Lugia smirks: 'Do not worry. As long as you wield me, I will regulate your core, allowing you to channel mana with zero physical recoil!'
[02:24 – 02:30] Shot 25: With a brilliant flash, Joshua's consciousness snaps back into reality. His hands are firmly clamped around the iron rod, and the connection is electric!
[02:30 – 02:36] Shot 26: The agonizing knot in his abdomen instantly subsides. Atmospheric mana pours through his arms like a rushing waterfall, completely regulated by Lugia's soul.
[02:36 – 02:42] Shot 27: He drops into a low combat stance, spinning the heavy iron rod around his back with the effortless fluidity of an immortal spear divinity.
[02:42 – 02:48] Shot 28: He executes the opening foundation of the ancient art: Magic Spear Arts Stage 1! He condenses his entire mana reserve into a single microscopic point at the rod's tip.
[02:48 – 02:54] Shot 29: And thrusts! An explosive azure shockwave detonates with the force of an artillery shell, tearing a supersonic sonic boom clean across the subterranean temple!
[02:54 – 03:00] Shot 30: Dust and shattered stone rain down as our boy exhales a vapor breath, grinning from ear to ear. Stage 1 is mastered on his very first attempt!
[03:00 – 03:06] Shot 31: 'This is where it truly begins,' Joshua whispers into the darkness. 'Kaiser Van Britton... I will grow stronger, reclaim my throne, and tear your empire to ash.'
[03:06 – 03:12] Shot 32: Meanwhile, up in the lavish ducal reception hall, the atmosphere is dripping with aristocratic tension.
[03:12 – 03:18] Shot 33: Duke Aden and Count Chiffon enter with military precision, but Aden immediately halts. The reception hall holds an unexpected crowd.
[03:18 – 03:24] Shot 34: Standing near the window is Princess Cheryl of the Pontier family—a fiery red-haired prodigy whose family controls the empire's largest merchant fleet.
[03:24 – 03:30] Shot 35: Beside her, Count Lok Roman Pontier—the financial brain of the Pontier clan—frantically bows, apologizing that Princess Cheryl was too impatient to wait for an invitation.
[03:30 – 03:36] Shot 36: Duke Aden laughs it off with characteristic swagger, casually joking that if they feel that guilty, they should just pledge their entire fortune to the Agnus family!
[03:36 – 03:42] Shot 37: Cheryl playfully scoffs that the Duke is as greedy as ever, trying to rob another noble house of its crown jewel in broad daylight.
[03:42 – 03:48] Shot 38: She cuts straight to business, asking if it's true that young Babel reached C-rank knight status at age fourteen, hinting that she wants to recruit him as her future consort.
[03:48 – 03:54] Shot 39: Roman panics over the girl's bluntness, but Aden waves it off, enjoying her sharp wit—before turning his gaze across the opposite side of the hall.
[03:54 – 04:00] Shot 40: His expression shifts from grandfatherly amusement to cold political calculation as his eyes land on the second delegation waiting in the wings.
[04:00 – 04:06] Shot 41: 'My apologies, Ponselle,' Aden says with smooth mockery. 'I was so distracted by the Pontier family, I nearly forgot you were standing there.'
[04:06 – 04:12] Shot 42: Standing beside the Count is young Lady Isellin, dressed in an immaculate navy gown with turquoise hair that seems to pulse with natural ambient mana.
[04:12 – 04:18] Shot 43: Even at nine years old, her mana control is breathtaking. Duke Aden can feel the atmospheric spirits bowing to her presence like a little magic queen.
[04:18 – 04:24] Shot 44: Count Rebreka came here with one singular objective: to link his magical dynasty to Duke Agnus's military juggernaut through a marriage alliance.
[04:24 – 04:30] Shot 45: Aden looks at Princess Cheryl, then looks at Lady Isellin. The pieces on the continental chessboard are falling into place faster than anyone anticipated.
[04:30 – 04:36] Shot 46: If Babel is promised to the gold of House Pontier, then pairing Lady Isellin's god-given Mana Blessing with Joshua's mysterious power creates an unstoppable faction.
[04:36 – 04:42] Shot 47: Deep beneath the palace foundations, oblivious to the political storm brewing above his head, our boy Joshua rests his iron rod against his palm.
[04:42 – 04:48] Shot 48: He runs his thumb across the iron rod. One thin layer of rust has already flaked off, revealing the faint demonic crimson sheen of Lugia's true body!
[04:48 – 04:54] Shot 49: Every stage of the Magic Spear Arts he unlocks will restore a portion of Lugia's god-tier form until the legendary red weapon is fully reborn.
[04:54 – 05:00] Shot 50: The first rays of morning sun filter down through the ceiling fissures. Time is up—knight training with Count Chiffon begins at daybreak.
[05:00 – 05:06] Shot 51: He climbs the ancient stairs, returning to the surface world not as a broken concubine's bastard, but as an awakened dragon sharpening its claws.
[05:06 – 05:12] Shot 52: Across the estate, the Red Knights are gathering for morning drills, unaware that their newest recruit is about to dismantle their entire worldview.
[05:12 – 05:18] Shot 53: Babel is nursing his bruised knuckles, waiting for a chance to humiliate his little brother during formal combat training.

======================================================================
CHAPTER 07 VOICEOVER
======================================================================
CONTINUOUS RECORDING TEXT:
Back inside the palace reception hall, Princess Charles de Pontier decides she's bored with aristocratic etiquette and drops a conversational nuclear bomb. With a cheeky smile, she asks Duke Aden if it's true that his illegitimate concubine's son living out by the stables just battered three armed centurions! Her guardian, Count Cox, nearly has a fatal stroke on the spot, begging the princess to remember whose terrifying palace she is sitting in. But Duke Aden doesn't get angry. He calmly admits the boy is his blood, but adds that Joshua was born with the fatal Congenital Mana Curse. Hearing the diagnosis, Count Ponselle Jin Rebreka—one of the few 5th-Class archmages in the entire empire—looks over with intense academic curiosity. Duke Aden seizes the opportunity, asking Ponselle to use his supreme magical sensitivity to inspect Joshua's core and verify if any cure exists. Ponselle eagerly agrees to score political points, but standing right behind the Duke, Count Chiffon smells an opportunity to eliminate an unwanted variable. Chiffon steps forward, suggesting that before the Duke leaves for the capital, they should hold a formal combat spar to see if the stable rumors hold water. Aden questions sending an elite Red Knight against a nine-year-old boy, but Chiffon smiles smoothly, claiming he has the ideal candidate for a gentle test. Cut straight to Chiffon's dimly lit office. Standing at attention with his chest squared is Sir Cain, freshly returned from the midnight stable incident. Chiffon questions why Cain executed two ducal centurions over an altercation in the stables, his fingers tapping impatiently against the desk. Cain stands firm: 'They attempted to harm the Duke's wife.' Chiffon leaps to his feet in rage, assuming they touched Duchess Vanessa! But Cain calmly clarifies: 'Not Duchess Vanessa... but Lady Lucia, the Duke's second wife.' Chiffon's fury instantly cools into contemptuous laughter. Chiffon can't believe Cain butchered two imperial centurions over a mere maid. But Cain replies that ducal honor cannot be stained by common thugs. Unable to refute military law, Chiffon lets it pass—before dropping his real mission: 'Cain, you will spar with Young Master Joshua today.' Cain blinks in disbelief. A seasoned knight sparring with a nine-year-old child? But then Chiffon unveils the horrifying true objective. Chiffon commands Cain to secretly inject a sliver of mana into Joshua's flesh during the duel! If he has the Mana Curse, foreign mana acts as lethal poison! 'His body will violently reject the mana,' Chiffon whispers coldly. 'He won't survive ten days.' This isn't a training session—it's a covert murder order! Chiffon declares that Babel is an immortal genius whose path to succession cannot be blocked by any pebble—even an illegitimate younger brother. 'A pebble?' Cain thinks to himself with a hidden smirk. 'That boy isn't a pebble—he is an apocalyptic meteor destined to rip the sky in two!' Cain accepts the command, salutes, and heads straight to the private training arena, resolved to protect Joshua from his own superior's venom. Light footsteps echo across the arena as our boy Joshua strolls in with hands in his pockets, looking as relaxed as someone taking a Sunday walk. 'You're Joshua, right?' Cain greets him. Joshua grins back, remarking that he heard the Duke ordered a friendly spar to test his combat aptitude. Cain leans down and whispers with sincere chivalry: 'You don't need to worry. I have no intention of fighting you for real.' But instead of being grateful, our boy scoffs in icy disgust: 'How utterly ridiculous. Are you daring to look down on me?!' Cain stammers in shock. Joshua steps into his personal space, lecturing the older knight with the haughty authority of an ancient battlefield commander. 'You knights brag about having the right to choose your lord at twenty-three,' Joshua speaks with chilling elegance... '...but I, too, have the absolute right to choose my subordinates! And I have zero use for a weak-minded vassal who pulls his punches!' 'If you truly want to prove yourself worthy of standing beside me next year,' Joshua commands, 'draw your steel and give me your absolute best!' Before Cain can answer, the arena doors swing wide. Duke Aden arrives flanked by Count Chiffon, Count Rebreka, Lady Isellin, and Princess Charles! Cain snaps into a salute. Count Chiffon sneers inwardly, expecting Joshua to disrespect the Duke with another careless head nod. But our boy shocks the entire room. He drops his left knee, straightens his right leg, and plants his fist over his breastplate in textbook royal etiquette! 'Joshua von Agnus pays his respects to the Duke!' he proclaims. Duke Aden blinks, genuinely impressed by the boy's impeccable aristocratic posture. Aden chuckles that he never ordered etiquette lessons for the boy, while Joshua smoothly replies that observation is the greatest teacher. Our boy wastes no time: 'I heard that Your Grace arranged a little test for me today.' Chiffon immediately jumps in with sweet poison, claiming this isn't a test at all, but the Duke's benevolent concern to diagnose his medical disability! Joshua reads Chiffon like an open book: *This bastard is completely in Babel's pocket. He arranged this spar to secretly poison my core.* Sir Cain steps into the ring, announcing with loud clarity that he is ready to begin the spar under the Duke's command. But Duke Aden raises his deep, commanding voice: 'Cain, it seems you are mistaken about something.' 'You are not the person Joshua will be fighting today.' Chiffon and Cain freeze, their heads whipping toward their lord in utter shock. Duke Aden gestures toward the arena corridor and drops the single most explosive name in the estate: 'IT'S BABEL.' Walking into the arena with confident, swaggering strides is fourteen-year-old Babel von Agnus, wearing a tailored dueling tunic and an iron longsword! Babel out here smiling like a benevolent saint, declaring to the noble guests that as Joshua's loving older brother, he will personally guide his training! But beneath that fake smile, Babel's eyes are dripping with unhinged malice: *I treasure you so much, little brother... that I'm going to cripple your limbs on this stage.* Joshua's jaw tightens: 'Babel, you arrogant piece of trash...' This was the Duke's true plan all along—to force the two brothers into open combat! The viewing balcony erupts in anticipation. Princess Charles is leaning over the railing thrilled for drama, while Lady Isellin watches Joshua with intense curiosity. Count Chiffon can barely hide his grin. A fourteen-year-old C-class knight who can wield mana versus an unarmed nine-year-old with a broken core! Babel draws his blade with a ringing metallic chime, flexing his knuckles. He has been dreaming of washing away his hallway humiliation in Joshua's blood. Joshua turns toward the weapons rack. He walks past rows of finely balanced broadswords, rapiers, and noble sabers without a second glance. Instead, our boy reaches down into the bottom corner and pulls out a plain, heavy wooden spear! The audience gasps in disbelief! In the Avalon Empire, choosing a peasant's spear over a knight's sacred sword is considered an unforgivable insult to nobility! But the second Joshua grips the wood, his posture transforms completely. He twirls the shaft in a lightning figure-eight that whistles through the arena. From the balcony, Duke Aden's pupils dilate. In all his forty years of warfare, he has never seen a nine-year-old child handle a spear with such god-level poise! An arrogant C-rank heir wielding imperial sword arts versus an awakened reincarnator holding a simple wooden stick. The arena is about to become a slaughterhouse!
