======================================================================
CHAPTER 08 VOICEOVER
======================================================================
CONTINUOUS RECORDING TEXT:
High on the royal balcony, Duke Aden lays down the law: this is a formal evaluation spar, meaning blunted wooden weapons only. And more importantly, Aden explicitly forbids Babel from using mana—because pitting a mana-empowered C-rank knight against an unawakened kid is basic murder. Babel scoffs under his breath, thinking that wasting a single drop of mana on a manure-shoveling bastard is beneath his noble dignity anyway. But our boy Joshua reads the room instantly: Babel and Chiffon orchestrated this entire circus to humiliate him in front of continental royalty. From the balcony, Princess Charles laughs out loud, thoroughly amused that the concubine's son brought a literal walking stick to a sword duel! Count Chiffon mocks the boy openly, asking what on earth he expects to accomplish with a peasant's stick against an imperial heir's blade. Duke Aden locks eyes with his youngest son, asking with grave intensity: 'Are you being serious?' Our boy answers with a single word: 'Yes.' The Duke takes his seat and roars the command: 'NOW, LET THE SPAR BEGIN!' Babel doesn't hold back for a fraction of a second. He launches forward like a cannonball, swinging his practice sword in a vicious downward diagonal slash! To any normal spectator, Joshua looks frozen in terror. But our boy waits until the wood is inches from his collarbone before making his move. Clack, clack, clack! With three microscopic wrist rotations, Joshua redirects Babel's entire momentum into empty floor space! Babel stumbles off-balance, snarling that the stable rat must have learned some cheap parlor tricks from traveling peddlers. But Joshua is already dissecting him: *He has decent C-rank physical speed, but his tactical mind is as predictable as a spoiled toddler.* Sliding his hands along the wood, Joshua extends his reach by four feet, slipping past Babel's clumsy broadsword guard with surgical precision. Whack! He drives the blunt wooden tip straight into Babel's solar plexus, knocking the wind clean out of the older brother's lungs with a sickening 'OOFT!' Babel staggers backward five paces, coughing violently. The entire spectator gallery freezes in pure shock—the fourteen-year-old heir just got countered! Humiliated in front of Princess Charles, Lady Isellin, and his legendary father, Babel's fragile teenage sanity completely snaps. Screaming that a concubine's bastard has no right to challenge him, Babel breaks the Duke's rules and ignites raw mana along his wooden sword! 'You should rot in the stable where you belong!' Babel roars, lunging to decapitate his little brother! Duke Aden roars from the balcony: 'BABEL! STOP!' But Joshua doesn't flinch. At point-blank range, retreating is suicide. If Babel wants to play with mana, our boy will show him what real magic looks like. With a terrifying intake of breath, Joshua unleashes the ancient technique! Pure sapphire mana explodes around his wooden spear like a roaring tempest! Count Ponselle—the 5th-Class imperial archmage—leaps out of his seat in total shock, shouting that the supposed cripple is wielding high-density mana! Babel's arrogant expression instantly dissolves into horror. His tiny flame is swallowed whole by Joshua's roaring azure hurricane! Joshua executes Magic Spear Arts Stage 1! His wooden spear shears clean through Babel's sword, pulverizing the seasoned oak into flying splinters! The shockwave hurls Babel twenty feet across the arena, smashing his spine against the stone wall with a concussive boom: 'GUAAAHHH!' Our boy steps forward to finish the job—until a golden tidal wave of pure Master-level pressure crashes down from above! Duke Aden drops from the balcony like a meteor, releasing his colossal master aura and commanding them to freeze: 'STOP! I will not forgive another move!' The sonic pressure rattles the arena chandeliers. Princess Charles covers her ears in shock, while Lady Isellin stares at Joshua in complete awe. Up on the terrace, Count Chiffon is trembling so hard his teeth chatter: 'Mana at age nine?! This is unheard of in continental history!' Count Ponselle steps down, verifying that Joshua's mana density is vastly superior to Babel's! This isn't just an awakening—it's a generational anomaly! Sir Cain stands in the shadows with an ecstatic grin. His instincts were completely vindicated—Joshua is the true dragon of the Agnus bloodline! Duke Aden strides across the splintered floorboards, staring down at his nine-year-old son with an expression he has never shown another living soul. Chiffon desperately tries to bring up the curse again, but Duke Aden cuts him down with a single murderous look: 'Chiffon, watch your mouth.' Then Duke Aden drops the command that breaks the entire estate hierarchy: 'Joshua... come to my private study tonight. I have something to ask you.' Babel clutches his broken ribs in agony, his mind shattered: Duke Aden has never once invited anyone—not even his legitimate heir—to his private sanctum! Our boy lowers his practice spear with flawless military grace, bowing slightly: 'I understand. I will see you tonight, Duke Agnus.' Fast-forward to the Pontier family's private suite. Princess Charles is literally jumping up and down on the velvet mattress like a kid who just won the lottery! 'Cox! That was absolutely incredible!' she squeals. 'A nine-year-old commoner's bastard smashing the empire's greatest heir with a wooden stick?!' Her guardian Cox begs her to calm down, but Charles crosses her arms and announces: 'Alright, I've made my decision!' 'Forget Babel,' the merchant princess declares with hungry ambition. 'Joshua is the true diamond of this empire. I am going to make that boy mine!' Meanwhile, across the garden in the Rebreka guest wing, Lady Isellin sits silently by the window with an ancient grimoire open in her lap. She stares at the diagrams of the fatal Mana Curse, unable to reconcile the textbooks with the godlike sapphire tempest she witnessed with her own eyes. Her father, Count Ponselle, paces the room in agitation, realizing that Joshua's mana frequency wasn't imperial knight aura—it was ancient primordial energy! As twilight gives way to midnight, the ducal estate falls into an uneasy silence heavy with brewing conflict. Our boy Joshua walks down the executive corridor toward Duke Aden's private sanctum, carrying the dormant spirit of Lugia under his coat. He knows the Duke wants answers. But Joshua doesn't plan on giving away his secrets for free—he plans on using this meeting to secure ducal resources. Knock, knock. Joshua taps on the massive ironwood doors, and the gravelly voice of the empire's greatest warlord calls out: 'Enter.' The door swings open into a dimly lit study filled with war maps, ancient trophies, and the legendary two-meter greatsword resting against the mantle. Duke Aden swirls the amber liquid in his glass, gesturing toward the leather armchair opposite him: 'Take a seat, Joshua.' Our boy sits down with the relaxed composure of an equal monarch, meeting his father's icy blue gaze with complete stillness. The Duke doesn't waste time with small talk: 'Where on earth did an untrained nine-year-old learn master-tier spear arts that shattered an imperial knight?' Joshua smiles faintly into the crackling flames, preparing a reply that will secure his independence and shake the empire's foundation. While in the shadows of the east wing, Duchess Vanessa and Count Chiffon are already drafting a deadly retaliation to eliminate the awakened bastard. An illegitimate son playing chess against the greatest warlord alive, two princesses scheming to claim him, and the true imperial war about to begin.

======================================================================
CHAPTER 09 VOICEOVER
======================================================================
CONTINUOUS RECORDING TEXT:
High up in the ducal palace's east wing, a thunderous slap echoes across the marble floor: SMACK! Duchess Vanessa—sister to the Iron-Blooded Emperor himself—is practically breathing fire, glaring down at Count Chiffon who is kneeling on the floor! 'Tell me one more time,' she snarls. 'What did you just say about my Babel?!' Chiffon can barely choke out the words: Babel was defeated by Joshua. Another brutal slap cracks across Chiffon's cheek! Vanessa shrieks in pure hysteria: how on earth could a maid's child wield mana better than her prized heir?! Chiffon wipes blood from his mouth and drops the real nightmare: Duke Aden has decided to bring Joshua along to the imperial capital, Arcadia! In the Avalon Empire, registering as an official C-rank knight requires dueling an imperial royal knight in the capital under the emperor's gaze. If Duke Aden presents Joshua to the empire as a nine-year-old C-rank knight, Babel's succession as the sole heir of House Agnus is completely finished. Vanessa's venomous smirk returns. She mutters that if Joshua must face an imperial knight... she just needs to contact her royal uncle to rig the execution. Meanwhile, down the quiet executive hallway, our boy Joshua arrives at Duke Aden's private sanctum, knocking twice with calm precision. 'Come in,' a gravelly voice echoes. Joshua steps inside, closing the door behind him as Duke Aden stands silhouetted against the moonlight. Joshua takes a knee with the smooth grace of a seasoned commander: 'You summoned me, My Lord.' Duke Aden doesn't beat around the bush: 'Who are you?' Our boy meets his father's gaze dead-on: 'I am Joshua von Agnus.' No trembling, no hesitation—just absolute, sovereign certainty. Aden lets out a rare sigh, observing that his youngest has changed completely since their last encounter, asking softly: 'Do you resent me?' Joshua calmly replies that he holds no resentment. He understands the political reality: Aden had to tread carefully under the emperor's paranoid scrutiny. 'In a world as brutal as ours,' Joshua states, 'seeking an apology is completely useless. However, I do have one request.' Aden arches an eyebrow in surprise: 'Are you so sure I decided to take you to the capital?' 'Yes, I am certain,' our boy smirks. 'And there are exactly two reasons why you have no choice.' Reason number one: Babel was groomed to be the sole successor, but Joshua's sudden awakening completely shattered that balance. If Joshua remains at the estate, Vanessa's faction will inevitably try to assassinate him, sparking a bloody civil war inside House Agnus. 'And reason number two,' Joshua continues with razor-sharp insight, 'is your personal, bottomless greed for supreme martial talent.' 'You cannot bear losing a prodigy like me. Because if I reach heights beyond even your abilities, the Agnus family will rise above the imperial throne itself!' Duke Aden breaks into a wide, thunderous grin. This isn't just a kid who knows how to swing a stick—his strategic intellect is downright terrifying! 'Then you already know the solution,' Aden remarks. Joshua nods: 'Take me to the capital and enroll me in the Imperial Academy.' In the Avalon Empire, all noble children between ten and fifteen must attend the Imperial Academy—a mandate created by the crown to monitor provincial heirs. Aden agrees, offering something unprecedented: whenever he is in the capital, he will personally tutor Joshua in master-level swordsmanship! To any knight in the realm, learning the sword from Duke Aden is the ultimate honor. But our boy Joshua politely declines: 'I will be using a SPEAR.' 'WHAT?!' Aden roars, the study windows rattling in their frames. 'A SPEAR?! Why on earth would you choose a peasant's weapon over the sword?!' Duke Aden unleashes the terrifying mana pressure of a continental Master! A roaring vortex of blue aura fills the room, heavy enough to crush normal knights flat! The gravity is suffocating. Joshua's ten-year-old boots slide backward against the carpet, but our boy refuses to bow his head! Shouting right through the master's storm, Joshua declares: 'Just as Duke Aden takes pride in his swordsmanship... my pride in the spear is UNRIVALED!' 'I will show you that anyone standing in my path will be met with my spear—even if I have to face one of the Nine Stars of the continent!' 'You claim you will defeat even the Nine Stars?!' Duke Aden roars, thoroughly exhilarated by the audacity of his nine-year-old blood! Aden reels in his aura with a snap of his fingers, challenging the boy: 'Then prove the worth of the spear with your own hands—I will be testing you myself!' 'I accept,' Joshua counters smoothly, 'on one condition.' Aden chuckles in amusement: a nine-year-old daring to set conditions against a Great Duke! If Joshua passes the Duke's personal test, Aden must formally endorse the spear as his primary weapon and allow him full autonomy at the Academy. 'Deal,' Duke Aden promises. 'If you pass, you may wield whatever weapon you please. But if you fail... you will follow my orders and master the sword!' Joshua bows with a predator's smirk: 'Understood.' Failure isn't even in his vocabulary—he has forty years of grandmaster combat memory backing every strike. Meanwhile, in a soundproof cellar deep beneath the palace east wing, dark sorcery is being invoked in the shadows. Cloaked in velvet, Duchess Vanessa watches the crystal orb illuminate, connecting a secure visual line straight into the Imperial Palace in Arcadia. 'It has been a while, Uncle,' Vanessa purrs with aristocratic sweetness. The imperial elder chuckles back, asking how she is enjoying life with Duke Aden. But Vanessa's smile turns to cold poison: 'Actually, Uncle... an unexpected incident occurred here, and I need a favor from the imperial family.' She requests that when Joshua arrives in Arcadia for his knighthood exam, the imperial court assign their most ruthless Royal Knight to 'accidentally' butcher the boy! Morning arrives. The grand ducal courtyard is packed with armored carriages and imperial cavalry, ready for the journey to the capital. Down by the carriage, Joshua shares a warm embrace with his mother Lucia, promising to return as a legendary knight who will grant her everything. Sir Cain stands guard beside the carriage door, giving Joshua a subtle wink. The boy climbs inside, feeling Lugia humming warmly against his side. The carriage convoy rolls out past the blooming blue roses, embarking on the week-long trek toward Arcadia, the heart of the Avalon Empire. But traveling across imperial borders is never a peaceful scenic tour—especially when the carriage must navigate through the monster-infested mountain valleys. While the carriage rumbles, our boy spends every waking hour meditating, using Lugia's frequency to dissolve the temporal knots clogging his channels. On the fourth night of travel, the convoy enters a narrow ravine. Red Knight scouts suddenly signal an emergency halt from the front line! Bathed in blood-red moonlight is the mangled wreckage of an entire merchant caravan, torn to shreds by something far worse than common bandits. Inside his cabin, Joshua's eyes snap open. He recognizes that stench instantly—the sulfur and rot of a mutated Black Ogre! A massive four-meter beast drops from the high cliff with a ground-shaking thud, brandishing a tree-trunk club bathed in gore! An imperial ambush, a four-meter mutated monster, and a nine-year-old reaper about to test Magic Spear Stage 1 on live prey. The slaughter begins now!

======================================================================
CHAPTER 10 VOICEOVER
======================================================================
CONTINUOUS RECORDING TEXT:
Right after stepping out of Duke Aden's private office, our boy Joshua can still feel his fingertips tingling from the sheer pressure of facing an imperial Master. Even after regressing with decades of combat memory, standing before one of the Nine Stars in a nine-year-old body is enough to get anyone's blood pumping. But before Joshua can take three steps down the carpeted corridor, an obnoxious voice calls out from the shadows: Babel has been waiting for him. Joshua doesn't even break stride. He hits him with the ultimate disrespect, saying he has zero interest in wasting breath on a sore loser. Babel loses his mind, shouting for an immediate rematch. He claims their first fight was just child's play with wooden toys, and this time he wants real steel. Joshua notices something unexpected in Babel's glare. The petty arrogance is gone, replaced by a desperate, burning hunger to prove his martial worth. That genuine spark of warrior pride earns a slight smirk from our boy. He asks if Babel truly has the confidence to step onto the execution block again. Meanwhile, hundreds of leagues away in the heart of the continent, the camera pans over Galtika Palace, the towering seat of the Avalon Empire. Inside the cavernous courtroom, heavy silence reigns over the crimson carpets, broken only by the cold draft whistling through the high stained glass. On the throne sits Emperor Marcus von Britton—the Iron-Blooded Monarch whose overwhelming presence radiates the lethality of a true continental Master. Prostrated before him is the commander of the Black Wind—the sovereign's personal shadow network that watches every heartbeat across the empire. The spy delivers an urgent report: a monstrous new talent with the potential to become a continental star has suddenly emerged within the Agnus Duchy. Marcus assumes his sister Vanessa gave birth to another royal prodigy, but the spy clarifies that this boy has zero imperial blood in his veins. He was born to a common maid from the servant quarters—a despised bastard whose very existence was hidden away in the horse stables. Instead of anger, Emperor Marcus roars with laughter. Having Babel was already envious enough, but Duke Agnus has somehow pulled a second miracle out of thin air. The spy reminds him that his own sons possess unmatched royal blood, but Marcus dismisses them. Politically gifted they may be, but martially, they are mediocre at best. Seeing the Emperor's good mood, the spy drops the real bomb: verified reports confirm that Babel, the empire's golden child, was completely demolished by this boy. Marcus freezes on his throne. Babel is a recognized C-rank knight at fourteen. For an older teen to beat him is possible—so Marcus asks how old this bastard is. When the spy replies that Joshua only just turned nine, the temperature in the imperial throne room drops below freezing. Marcus stands tall, trembling with adrenaline. To beat a mana user, you must wield mana yourself—meaning a nine-year-old child is already operating at C-rank! The Duke has formally requested the Imperial Knight Order to conduct a certification exam. Marcus wants to ride out himself, but geopolitical tensions forbid it. Instead, Marcus issues an imperial mandate: dispatch one of his royal sons alongside the knights. An emperor must possess an eye for talent, and this is their test. The scene cuts back to the secluded training yard behind the ducal estate, where Babel has brought Joshua for their secret, unauthorized duel. Babel draws a razor-sharp steel longsword, completely discarding the rules. Thick mana wraps around the blade like a hungry viper! Babel arrogantly offers him a spare sword, but our boy Joshua raises his bare hands. For someone of Babel's caliber, flesh and bone are more than enough. Enraged beyond reason, Babel lunges with full C-rank velocity, aiming a lethal strike straight at Joshua's unprotected throat! With millimetric precision, Joshua slips under the steel edge like smoke, causing Babel's sword to whistle uselessly through empty air. A brutal barehanded punch smashes directly into the flat of the sword! The violent vibration tears Babel's grip and sends shockwaves up his arm. Joshua lectures him mid-clash: knights look down on hand-to-hand combat as commoner brawling, but real martial arts turn the entire human body into a lethal weapon. Concentrated sapphire mana suddenly coats Joshua's bare knuckles! Babel is horrified—infusing raw mana directly into flesh without a weapon is Master-tier control! Right before impact, Joshua detonates the compressed mana at point-blank range, unleashing his exclusive signature technique: Aura Explosion! The resulting shockwave obliterates the ground! The concentrated kinetic blast bends Babel's mastercrafted steel sword like a cheap tin fork! Babel is hurled across the arena, smashing into the stone wall and spitting a mouthful of blood as his weapon clatters uselessly to the flagstones. Lying in the dirt, Babel's reality collapses. He finally realizes how small he is—this illegitimate boy isn't just talented, he is an untouchable monster. Joshua towers over him with icy disdain. He echoes the Duke's exact words: if this humiliation eats away at your soul, then stop whining and get stronger. A terrifying wave of murderous aura washes over Babel. Joshua reminds him that for all the cruelty inflicted on his mother, he could easily butcher him right here. Because Babel showed genuine martial determination, Joshua spares his limbs—with one final warning: dare cross that line again, and he will permanently sever his sword hand! As our boy walks away, Babel clutches his bent sword in total defeat, realizing the chasm between them is too wide to ever cross. The next morning in his new high tower chambers, Joshua wakes up feeling completely drained after consecutively deploying high-level mana in an unconditioned body. Absorbing Lugia stabilized his spiritual core, but a nine-year-old vessel still suffers physical fatigue when channeling adult-tier mana bursts. A polite knock rattles the chamber door. Assuming Sir Cain has returned with his supply inventory, Joshua pulls on his vest and walks over to unlock it. He swings the door open saying he didn't expect Cain so early—only to freeze when he's met with a radiant girl dressed in an elegant lavender gown! It is none other than Princess Cheryl de Pontier, the sole heiress of the northern Pontier Duchy, smiling brightly like an older sister paying a friendly visit! Cheryl playfully suggests he can call her Noona since she's three years his senior, stepping right past him to invite herself into his room for tea! But our boy Joshua does not give a single care about high noble charm. He plants his hand flat on her shoulder and shoves her straight back into the hallway! He looks her straight in the eyes, tells her to get lost, and slams the heavy door right in the aristocratic princess's face with a thunderous BANG! Cheryl is left frozen in the hallway, completely flabbergasted. No noble in the entire empire has ever dared treat the Pontier jewel like an unwanted solicitor! Inside, Joshua shakes his head at how exhausting politicians are. But barely two seconds later, another insistent series of knocks rattles the door frame! Our boy snaps. He thinks this spoiled princess clearly cannot comprehend English and needs a physical demonstration to understand the concept of personal space! He rips the door open and roars at the top of his lungs to get lost—only to realize he's screaming directly into the stunned face of Sir Cain! Cain stammers in terror, wondering if he interrupted something scandalous. Joshua quickly clears his throat, awkwardly apologizing for the friendly fire. Shifting back to business, Joshua asks if the secret preparations are complete. Cain nods, reporting that every carriage, steed, and crate is locked and loaded. But Cain can't help asking the burning question on his mind: why is a nine-year-old boy plotting clandestine logistical movements like a seasoned grand strategist? Joshua turns toward the horizon with a confident grin. He tells Cain that blind trust is all he needs—because when the empire's wolves finally bare their fangs, everything will make terrifying sense!

[00:36 – 00:42] Shot 07: That genuine spark of warrior pride earns a slight smirk from our boy. He asks if Babel truly has the confidence to step onto the execution block again.

======================================================================
CHAPTER 11 VOICEOVER
======================================================================
CONTINUOUS RECORDING TEXT:
Over at Duke Agnus's central military training hall, dozens of elite centurions are huddled together, trading scandalous rumors in hushed whispers. Two veteran squad leaders corner Rols against the stone wall, demanding to know where his bully partners Gort and Roid suddenly disappeared to. Ever since watching his friends get executed like cattle, Rols has been completely mute, sweating bullets every time someone mentions Joshua's name. Suddenly, the massive oak doors swing wide open as a herald's voice booms across the arena: Red Knight Sir Cain has entered the building! Every soldier immediately snaps to attention, expecting a grueling inspection—only to blink in confusion at the small kid casually strolling beside him. Whispers break out across the ranks—that's the barn bastard Joshua! While the others scoff, Rols looks like he's about to faint on the spot. Sir Cain doesn't hesitate. With full ducal authority, he announces that today's entire combat evaluation will be run by none other than Joshua von Agnus! Our boy Joshua steps into the center of the hall, declaring with absolute authority that he is here to evaluate their combat readiness. The veteran centurions are completely insulted. They are battle-hardened squad leaders who command the vanguard, not babysitters for a toddler. Joshua cuts right through their murmurs, reminding them that leading troops into war requires sheer individual strength, not inflated egos. Then our boy drops the most disrespectful challenge imaginable: he tells the entire squad of centurions to draw their swords and jump him together! The centurions scoff, warning him that royal blood or not, swinging real blades against hardened soldiers will only end with him in the medical ward. Joshua doesn't even blink. He casually picks up a long training spear from the rack, spinning the heavy shaft like a feather between his fingers. He marches straight over and points the steel spearhead dead center at Rols's neck, asking if he also thinks this is a childish game. Rols collapses to his knees like a sack of potatoes, frantically begging for mercy in front of all his bewildered peers! Joshua's expression turns razor-sharp. He gave Rols one simple task—warn the barracks what happens to anyone who touches his mother—and Rols choked. The spearhead cracks the stone right between Rols's boots! Joshua asks him out loud if he wants to share the exact same fate as Gort and Roid. Hearing Gort and Roid's names makes the entire room go dead silent. The officers realize with mounting horror that the rumors were real. Joshua orders Rols to his feet and tells him to draw steel. If he can defeat a nine-year-old in single combat, his life will be spared. Cornered with no way out, Rols's survival instincts kick in. He reasons that no matter how prodigal Joshua is, a child's muscles cannot stop a grown warrior's full charge! Rols draws his broadsword with a frantic roar, throwing his entire body weight into a desperate, overhead execution slash! Our boy doesn't even bother dodging. Azure mana surges along the wooden spear shaft, crackling like living lightning around the tip! With a flick of his wrist, he unleashes the Magic Spear Arts First Form: Lightning Strike! The blunt butt of the spear catches Rols square in the chest before his blade even reaches the halfway mark, blowing clean through his reinforced cuirass! Rols is catapulted thirty meters through the air, embedding into the far stone wall and slumping unconscious without even landing a single blow! The centurions stand frozen like statues. That wasn't just physical strength—that was refined, condensed mana channeled through an ordinary spear! Joshua casually rests the spear over his shoulder, lecturing the room: judging an adversary by their size or age is the fastest way to end up in a body bag. He dares anyone who still doubts his authority to step into the ring. Not a single man moves an inch. The senior-most officer steps forward, drops his sword to the stone, and falls to one knee, loudly swearing fealty to Young Master Joshua! A glorious domino effect ripples through the hall! One by one, every squad commander drops to their knees, bowing their heads in complete surrender. By the doors, Sir Cain watches with starry eyes. In less than ten minutes, a nine-year-old has thoroughly subjugated the fiercest division in the ducal army. Meanwhile, over in the guest palace, Princess Cheryl is curled up in bed clutching her stuffed puppy, still replaying Joshua's brutal rejection in her head. 'Screw off... Joshua,' she mumbles into her pillow. She's supposed to be an untouchable high noble heiress, but that little demon boy made her feel completely helpless! Her guardian Cox bursts through the door with an urgent update: the imperial court has officially dispatched the Royal Knight Order to the Agnus estate! Cheryl groans, telling him to get lost. Cox reminds her that Duke Pontier sent them on a diplomatic mission, and slacking off will ruin everything. But political treaties are the last thing on her mind. Staring at the ceiling, she whispers that all she wants is to figure out who Joshua really is. The scene cuts to Duke Aden's study. The Master of the Nine Stars looks up from his ledgers as Joshua steps inside to report. Aden mentions that reports already reached his desk about Joshua violently demolishing a squad leader in the central hall. Joshua doesn't flinch. He explains that under imperial martial law, defaming nobility carries the death penalty—and Aden himself would have severed his head. Aden admits that's true, but wonders if Joshua acted out of reckless childish temper or calculated strategy. Our boy lays out the brutal truth: just a few days ago, he was a nameless barn bastard. Sudden respect cannot be bought with sweet smiles. The only way to guarantee his mother's safety while he's away at the capital is to plant absolute, overwhelming terror into every soldier's mind. Duke Aden lets out a satisfied chuckle. Joshua has the ruthless mind of an apex predator. He tells him to get ready—the imperial testing party has arrived! Down by the grand courtyard gates, Count Chiffon is pacing anxiously, grumbling about how the Agnus estate suddenly produced two rival C-rank prodigies. Other commanders mock Chiffon, asking if he's sweating because his favorite horse Babel was just put out to pasture by a nine-year-old child. The bickering instantly ceases as Duke Aden arrives at the courtyard with Joshua at his side, stepping out onto the grand promenade. The camera pulls back to reveal an astonishing spectacle: an enormous royal envoy stretches across the bridge, flanked by hundreds of elite imperial cavalry! Chiffon is flabbergasted—did the capital dispatch an entire knight battalion just to administer a routine rank test to a child?! The gilded door swings open with a heavy thud. A polished leather boot steps onto the stone pavement, commanding the presence of high royalty. Stepping into the sunlight is a handsome, golden-haired royal dressed in pristine military regalia, gazing across the estate with patronizing amusement. Duke Aden's jaw tightens. He instantly recognizes that face—it is none other than the Fourth Prince of the Avalon Empire! Kaiser von Britton—the treacherous schemer who orchestrated the second civil war, stole the throne, and had Joshua murdered in his previous life! The moment Joshua sees that deceitful face, centuries of bottled-up hatred explode inside his chest like a volcanic eruption! His sworn enemy has arrived on his doorstep years ahead of schedule. The architect of his death is standing right in front of him—and Joshua vows to make him taste true despair!

CHAPTER 12 VOICEOVER
======================================================================
CONTINUOUS RECORDING TEXT:
Before the imperial delegation even arrived at the ducal estate, dark machinations were already turning behind closed doors in the capital. Inside the Imperial Knight barracks, a junior recruit is violently beaten half to death, begging for mercy at the boots of a sadistic officer. Meet Joker, Captain of the 1st Imperial Battalion—a notoriously ruthless B-rank veteran who despises rookies and loves breaking bones for fun. A hooded agent emerges from the darkness with a discreet royal bounty directly from Duchess Vanessa: a classified hit job on the Agnus estate. Joker grins from ear to ear. His orders are simple: disguise the evaluation as an accident and permanently shatter Joshua's mana core and limbs! The camera cuts back to the present courtyard, where Prince Kaiser steps onto the promenade, locking eyes with our boy Joshua. Standing only five paces away, Joshua stares at the man who manipulated his loyalty, betrayed his army, and ordered his death in his previous life. An imperial retainer immediately scolds Joshua, barking that a low-born child must drop to both knees in the presence of royal blood. Joshua laughs right in his face. He points out imperial law: there cannot be two suns in the empire, and a knight answers only to the Emperor! He drops the ultimate insult, asking why anyone should bow to a powerless Fourth Prince whose claim to the throne isn't even guaranteed! Imperial Vice-Captain Valmont draws his sword in absolute fury, roaring that a kid who was shoveling horse manure yesterday has completely lost his mind! Even Count Chiffon steps forward with drawn steel, declaring that regardless of birth, an Agnus cannot be assaulted on their own front lawn! Suddenly, the entire courtyard shakes! A suffocating mountain of azure mana erupts as Duke Aden steps forward like an ancient god of war! Aden's voice chills everyone's marrow: 'Drawing blades before royalty... do you people wish to die right now?' Valmont freezes in absolute terror. Aden tells Joshua that disrespecting imperial guests is unacceptable and demands an apology to smooth over political protocol. But Prince Kaiser waves his hand with a saintly smile. He tells the Duke not to worry, admitting that as the fourth son, he holds no actual sovereignty. Kaiser walks right up to Joshua, gently resting a hand on his shoulder while delivering a veiled barb: a true knight speaks through steel, not empty words. Joshua recognizes that venomous, hypocritical smile. He vows internally that he won't assassinate Kaiser today—he will decapitate him at the height of his power! Our boy simply responds that he is always ready to dance, casually strolling toward the evaluation grounds like he owns the place. Sensing his golden opportunity, Captain Joker steps forward, humbly volunteering to personally oversee Joshua's certification duel. Kaiser readily agrees, praising Joker's B-rank pedigree and granting him full authority to conduct the formal combat test. Turning toward the arena, Joker's grin widens into a demonic sneer. He promises himself that this kid will leave the ring on a stretcher. The arena is set. High above on the marble balcony sit Duke Aden, Kaiser, Chief Wizard Evergrant, and the imperial commanders. Down in the ring, Joshua enters wielding a blunt steel training pole. Joker immediately bursts out laughing at the pathetic excuse for a weapon. Joker barks that bringing a rod to a knight's duel is an insult to the imperial crown. Joshua yawns, noting that the imperial order sure loves to talk. Our boy wonders aloud if imperial knights are selected for combat prowess or their ability to recite speeches. Joker's jaw practically snaps from rage! Thick cyan mana erupts around Joker's blade! He charges with true B-rank speed, determined to carve the insolent brat into ribbons! The blades collide with a bone-jarring crash! Shockwaves rip through the flagstones as Joshua blocks the B-rank strike dead in its tracks! Joker's eyes bulge in shock. Joshua's mana isn't just defending—it feels denser and heavier than a collapsing mountain! Joker tries to exploit the classic spear weakness by closing the gap to point-blank range, expecting an easy kill inside Joshua's guard. But Joshua laughs in his face. In his past life, he mastered close-quarters spear combat centuries ago, neutralizing the dead zone completely! He channels the Magic Spear Arts First Form: Lightning Strike, swinging the iron pole like a crescent bolt of thunder! The blow smashes into Joker's pauldron, sending the grown captain stumbling backward in front of the entire imperial entourage! Up on the balcony, Valmont can't believe what he's seeing: a nine-year-old kid is trading blows on equal footing with an imperial battalion captain! Valmont realizes the horrifying truth: Joshua is intentionally holding his mana back to C-rank, yet he is utterly outclassing Joker in pure martial technique! Five rapid-fire exchanges detonate across the tiles in less than two seconds, every spear parry deflecting Joker's sword with surgical precision. Panic sets in for Joker. If this match ends as an honorable draw, Duchess Vanessa will execute him for failing to cripple the target. Pushed to the brink, Joker decides to discard all knightly honor and gamble his entire career on an illegal, lethal strike! Joshua feels the strain in his young joints. His tactical mind is that of a grandmaster, but his prepubescent body cannot endure prolonged high-intensity exertion. Joker roars like a mad beast, forcibly unleashing forbidden internal energy to spike his aura far beyond normal B-rank regulations! Up on the gallery, Count Chiffon leaps to his feet, shouting that Joker's power has crossed the line into an outright assassination attempt! But Duke Aden holds up a hand, commanding everyone to stay put. The master wants to see how his son handles true mortal peril. Joker dives downward with full execution force, screaming that Joshua's flimsy spear tricks won't save him from being crushed into paste! Joshua anchors his heels into the floorboards. Deep cobalt electricity crackles along his arms as he prepares to meet the killing stroke head-on! Joker brings the massive broadsword down with decapitating malice, convinced the annoying little prodigy is about to be erased from existence! Valmont roars at Joker to stand down, but the rogue captain has already committed everything to the kill! An earth-shattering detonation engulfs the center of the ring! A tidal wave of primordial energy violently blows back the entire arena! Even Duke Aden grips the balcony railing so hard his fingers crack the solid marble, his eyes widening in total disbelief! Valmont and Prince Kaiser are forced to shield their eyes as divine pressure washes over the entire hall like an ancient awakening! When the dust begins to clear, a massive fifty-meter trench has been gouged straight through the reinforced stone arena! Skidding at the far end of the trench is Captain Joker, clutching his trembling sword with blood dripping from his lips, unable to comprehend what just hit him! 'I was pushed back?!' Joker stammers in horror. 'What kind of demonic monstrosity is standing before me?!' Through the parting smoke stands our boy Joshua, completely untouched. The plain iron rod has shattered its disguise! Blazing in his grip is the sacred divine spear Lugia, crackling with crimson and white lightning! The unrivaled spear knight has revealed his divine fang—and the imperial court will never be the same!

======================================================================
CHAPTER 13 VOICEOVER
======================================================================
CONTINUOUS RECORDING TEXT:
Right in the middle of Joker's illegal execution strike, our boy Joshua channels the deepest frequencies of his mana core, summoning the sacred name: Lugia! The disguise on the iron rod violently shatters! A blinding flash of divine aura detonates outward, blasting the imperial captain fifty paces across the arena! Through the dissipating smoke stands a nine-year-old child brandishing a legendary weapon—the divine spear Lugia, gleaming in pristine white and crimson steel. The entire spectator gallery loses its collective mind. High nobles whisper in shock: has a nine-year-old somehow bonded with a genuine ancient artifact?! Prince Kaiser breaks into a polite smile, leaning over to Vice-Captain Valmont and noting that Joshua has already proven far more than required for a C-rank test. But down in the ring, Joker is consumed by panic. If he doesn't cripple the boy now, Duchess Vanessa will execute him, so he prepares to charge again! Before Joker can take another step, Valmont drops from the balcony like a red thunderbolt, drawing his sword and commanding Joker to stand down immediately! Valmont stares at our boy in sheer disbelief. Even Babel's legendary genius pales into utter insignificance compared to what Joshua just displayed. With full formal dignity, Valmont raises the imperial decree, projecting his voice across the arena to deliver the empire's official verdict! 'Joshua von Agnus,' Valmont roars, 'by imperial decree, I officially declare your appointment as a C-Rank Knight! Kneel before the blade of the Emperor!' Joshua doesn't grovel or kneel. He stands tall with Lugia resting over his shoulder, calmly accepting the title like a sovereign accepting a token tribute. Prince Kaiser leads the applause with an elegant smile. But behind those glittering amber eyes, he is calculating just how dangerous this boy might become. Joshua stares straight back at Kaiser. In this lifetime, he won't be anyone's sacrificial pawn; he will make every betrayer suffer tenfold! A warm fur cloak suddenly drops over Joshua's shoulders. He looks up to find Duke Aden standing right behind him, softly calling his name. 'Are you hurt?' Aden asks quietly. Joshua is momentarily stunned—in fifty years of his previous life, he had never once seen his father look at him with genuine concern. Shaking off the sentimentality, Joshua replies that he is perfectly fine, reaching out to grasp the ceremonial knight's sword presented by the empire. He hoists the blade high into the air, formally cementing history: at nine years old, Joshua von Agnus has become the youngest certified knight on the continent! But the celebration is short-lived. That very night, emergency torches line the corridor outside the ducal grand council chamber. Duke Aden sits at the head of the colossal mahogany council table, surrounded by his vassal lords and knights for a mandatory high-level assembly. Count Rannel rises to deliver the opening report: the Knights of the Golden Mane, sent on a northern border pacification campaign, have returned early. Aden questions why an elite expedition retreated prematurely. Rannel explains that monster activity along the northern frontier hasn't just increased—it has multiplied exponentially! Entire outposts are being wiped off the map overnight, with veteran squads torn to shreds before they can even send out distress flares! The nightmare culprit is the apex predator of the northern woods: the Black Ogre, a mutated titan whose skin is tougher than tempered steel! Even high-tier Fourth Circle battle mages and veteran B-rank knights struggle to bring down a single Black Ogre without heavy casualties. While the lords panic, Duke Aden shuts them down with one cold sentence: he will personally step into the field and eliminate the beasts himself. Rannel moves to the second agenda item regarding the Pontier family's visit, but Aden instructs him to hold that briefing for a private meeting later. Before the meeting can adjourn, Count Chiffon raises his hand, announcing that he has an urgent concern regarding Joshua's sudden appointment! Several lords remark that having the continent's youngest knight brings unprecedented glory to Agnus, but Chiffon quickly rains on their parade. Chiffon reminds everyone of the cold facts: Joshua lived in a barn with a known mana disability. How does a disabled child suddenly become a C-rank overnight?! He argues passionately that natural genius cannot defy biology, claiming there must be a sinister, external explanation behind this miracle. He points to the weapon: how could a nine-year-old wield a shapeshifting spear that radiates alien power unless he contracted with a demonic entity?! Dark magic is an absolute continent-wide taboo. If the imperial court suspects an Agnus son of dabbling in demonic power, the entire ducal bloodline could face holy crusade! Chiffon smugly suggests exiling Joshua to a remote northern outpost until an investigation is concluded, effectively burying him out of sight. SLAM! The council chamber doors burst wide open as every lord snaps their head around to see our boy Joshua standing on the threshold! With hands tucked into his pockets, Joshua strolls right down the center of the hall, repeating Chiffon's words with dripping venom: 'Demonic energy?' He marches right up to Chiffon's desk, daring the grown commander to draw his rapier and test whether his power is demonic with a duel right now! Chiffon is paralyzed. A nine-year-old child is openly threatening to cross blades with a ducal commander in the middle of a high council session! Chiffon breaks into a cold sweat. If Joshua truly fought on par with Joker, accepting this challenge could end with Chiffon getting butchered in front of his peers! Joshua doesn't let him breathe, roasting him in front of every lord: shouldn't one of the family's finest commanders have the spine to back up his slander?! Pushed past his limit, Chiffon violently kicks his chair back, unleashing his aura and screaming that Joshua has crossed every line of decency! The surrounding lords scramble out of their seats, shouting for Chiffon to stand down before he triggers a bloodbath in the council chambers! Duke Aden taps his index finger on the mahogany table. The single sound rings like a thunderclap, freezing both combatants dead in their tracks! Aden fixes Chiffon with a chilling stare, pointing out that in his frantic rush to slander Joshua, he overlooked one undeniable fact. Chief Magician Evergrant of the Imperial Order personally oversaw the duel. If Joshua had used even a microgram of demonic energy, Evergrant would have purged him instantly! Chiffon slumps back into his chair, completely defeated. With no counterargument left, Aden declares the slander closed for good. But Duke Aden isn't done. He turns his heavy gaze back onto Joshua, noting that clearing demonic charges doesn't explain his impossible rate of growth. Aden admits that seeing a nine-year-old child wield Master-tier spear mechanics is genuinely unprecedented, even for an apex warrior of the Nine Stars. To truly measure what his son is capable of when pushed to the absolute brink, Duke Aden drops a shocking proposal that silences the entire room. 'We will be heading to the Forest of Dark Beings,' Aden declares—taking Joshua straight into the terrifying hunting ground of the Black Ogres! The council lords gasp in horror. Throwing a nine-year-old into the deadliest monster-infested wilderness on the continent sounds like a suicide mission! But our boy Joshua doesn't flinch for a second. A slow, dangerous smile curls across his lips as he welcomes the trial with open arms. Facing apex monsters is exactly what he needs to temper his nine-year-old vessel and unlock the higher stages of the Magic Spear Arts! Father and son lock eyes across the council hall, both understanding that words are meaningless—only blood and steel will reveal the truth. The stage is set. An apex ducal expedition, an army of mutated Black Ogres, and a nine-year-old reaper ready to paint the forest red. The hunt begins now!

[04:48 – 04:54] Shot 49: 'We will be heading to the Forest of Dark Beings,' Aden declares—taking Joshua straight into the terrifying hunting ground of the Black Ogres!
[04:54 – 05:00] Shot 50: The council lords gasp in horror. Throwing a nine-year-old into the deadliest monster-infested wilderness on the continent sounds like a suicide mission!
[05:00 – 05:06] Shot 51: But our boy Joshua doesn't flinch for a second. A slow, dangerous smile curls across his lips as he welcomes the trial with open arms.
[05:06 – 05:12] Shot 52: Facing apex monsters is exactly what he needs to temper his nine-year-old vessel and unlock the higher stages of the Magic Spear Arts!
[05:12 – 05:18] Shot 53: Father and son lock eyes across the council hall, both understanding that words are meaningless—only blood and steel will reveal the truth.
[05:18 – 05:24] Shot 54: The stage is set. An apex ducal expedition, an army of mutated Black Ogres, and a nine-year-old reaper ready to paint the forest red. The hunt begins now!

======================================================================
CHAPTER 14 VOICEOVER
======================================================================
CONTINUOUS RECORDING TEXT:
The grand council chamber instantly erupts into chaos as vassal lords wave their hands, begging Duke Aden not to risk his life in the monster-infested north. Duke Aden shuts them down with one cold glare, reminding the table that he is one of the Nine Stars—apex predators do not fear overgrown beasts! Aden then drops an official decree: once the Black Ogres are purged, Joshua will be formally enrolled into the prestigious Imperial Academy in Arcadia! Under the ironclad laws of House Agnus, undeniable talent commands respect—and Joshua has earned his right to compete on the grandest stage. Chiffon panics internally. If this monster boy spends five years honing his skills at the Imperial Academy under royal protection, Babel is completely finished! Before the council can move on, our boy Joshua steps forward with perfect composure, announcing that he has one non-negotiable condition. Aden asks what he wants. Joshua looks his father dead in the eye and requests to bring his mother, Lady Lucia, along with him to Arcadia! Chiffon jumps out of his chair like he sat on a cactus, barking that Duchess Vanessa would never tolerate a lowly concubine living in the capital! Joshua turns slowly, smirking like a grandmaster who just spotted checkmate in one move: 'And what exactly happens if she doesn't stand for it?' He drops the hammer: is Chiffon publicly claiming that Duke Aden, Master of the Nine Stars, needs his wife's permission to move his own family?! Chiffon chokes. If he says yes, he's committing treason by implying the Duke is whipped by his wife. If he says no, Joshua gets exactly what he wants! Cornered and humiliated, Chiffon stammers a pathetic excuse about court gossip. Aden waves his hand, completely ignoring the flustered count. 'Forget it,' Aden decrees. 'I grant your request. You may bring Lucia with you.' Joshua bows politely, securing his mother's safety in one brilliant move! Aden looks at his nine-year-old son with growing fascination. Even Babel at his sharpest never brought this level of strategic thrill to the table. The entire table bows in unison, endorsing the Duke's command. Chiffon has no choice but to bend the knee, stewing in utter defeat. Aden stands up, instructing Count Armstrong to deliver the confidential Pontier family dossiers to his private office, motioning Joshua to follow. But just as the meeting adjourns, the double doors swing open once again. Standing in the entrance is none other than Babel von Agnus! Babel drops to one knee with impeccable manners, apologizing for his late arrival and stating that he needed time alone to reflect on his defeat. Aden observes a dramatic transformation in his heir. The childish vanity is gone, replaced by the tempered focus of a warrior who tasted true humility. Babel stands up and looks directly at Joshua, addressing him formally as Lord Joshua—a title of genuine peer respect he had never used before. Chiffon instantly butts in, scolding Joshua that Babel is the senior heir and demanding that the younger brother bow down in deference! Joshua roasts him on the spot: is Chiffon Babel's personal lapdog or a commander of the ducal army?! Our boy reminds him that in House Agnus, rank is earned through combat prowess. If Chiffon wants to dictate hierarchy, he should draw his sword! Surprisingly, Babel steps in and tells Chiffon to stand down. He admits openly that Joshua's words are completely correct! Babel locks eyes with his younger brother, declaring that the humiliation of defeat only lit a fire in his belly. He will climb higher than ever before! Joshua smirks with quiet approval. His bare-knuckle lesson did exactly what was needed: it broke the spoiled brat and birthed a true warrior. Then Babel drops the biggest bombshell in the history of the duchy: he formally requests to renounce his position as the official designated heir! The entire room gasps in disbelief! Chiffon looks like his heart just stopped beating—the imperial bloodline heir is voluntarily surrendering his birthright! Duke Aden asks if he is running away because his monstrous younger brother crushed his confidence. Babel immediately denies it. He wants to strip away the unearned privilege of birth and compete for the ducal throne fair and square through pure, unadulterated strength! Aden lets out a proud, rare smile. He promises to consider the petition, formally bringing the historic council to a close. As Duke Aden walks down the colonnade, he reflects on how fast his family dynamics are shifting—and for the first time in years, he feels genuine optimism. Behind him, two brothers who were once mortal enemies now share an unspoken pact: an honorable war of talent where only the strongest will survive! The scene cuts to Duchess Vanessa's private quarters, where an atmosphere of pure, suffocating fury fills the candlelit room. Count Chiffon kneels before the Duchess, trembling as he delivers the twin disasters: Babel renounced his heir seat, and Joshua is taking Lucia to the capital! CRASH! Vanessa sweeps her arm across the mahogany table, shattering her crystal glass against the wall in a blind, shrieking rage! She clutches her temples, screaming that she was already on the verge of madness after hearing that Captain Joker failed his assassination mission! She glares down at Chiffon like a cornered cobra, hissing that Babel's succession is her entire reason for living, and failure is not an option. Chiffon grovels frantically on the floor, stammering apologies and admitting that he should have physically restrained Babel from opening his mouth. Vanessa sighs, acknowledging that Babel inherited his father's infuriating stubbornness—once Duke Aden's blood makes up its mind, no words can move it. But then she leans down and delivers a chilling ultimatum straight into Chiffon's ear that makes the blood drain from his face! 'If that servant's brat ever threatens my Babel's position,' she whispers, 'I will tell my husband every single dark secret between us!' If Duke Aden ever discovers their treasonous collusion, Chiffon's entire noble line will be eradicated overnight. He swears on his life to fix this! Composing herself, Vanessa asks about Joshua's schedule—where is the little prodigy heading before his departure for the capital? Chiffon reports that Duke Aden is personally escorting Joshua north to the barren Loch Fief for a two-day subjugation campaign in the Forest of Dark Beings. 'The Loch Fief?' Vanessa muses. That means the Duke and Joshua will be hundreds of leagues away for at least four to five days! And while Joshua is fighting monsters in the freezing north, his precious mother Lucia will be left completely alone and defenseless in the ducal mansion! A venomous, spider-like smirk spreads across Vanessa's face. The perfect window of vulnerability has just fallen right into her lap! The camera closes in on the Duchess's flawless aristocratic features, masking a black heart capable of unspeakable cruelty. She issues a formal command: before Joshua and the Duke set out for the hunt, send an official invitation summoning Lady Lucia to join her for private dining! Chiffon shivers down to his bones. A private luncheon hosted by Duchess Vanessa for the maid who stole the Duke's heart is nothing less than a banquet of death! 'Invite her,' Vanessa purrs with venomous delight, 'since it will be our very first... and our absolute last meeting!' While Joshua prepares his gear for the northern monster hunt, deadly poison is already being prepared in the ducal kitchen for the only woman he loves. A deadly trap is set. A mother's life hangs by a thread. And the unrivaled spear knight is about to show Duchess Vanessa the terrifying cost of touching his family!

======================================================================
CHAPTER 15 VOICEOVER
======================================================================
CONTINUOUS RECORDING TEXT:
With the mana evaluation officially concluded, the imperial knight battalion packs their banners and begins their march back to Arcadia. The ducal courtyard is buzzing with hectic activity as stable hands load heavy baggage carts for Duke Aden's sudden northern campaign. Walking calmly through the chaos is our boy Joshua, wearing an elegant travel cloak and admiring a brilliant silver ring on his finger: the Don Ring. A flashback reveals Duke Aden summoning Joshua for a private briefing, sliding an ornate velvet box across his desk as an unexpected gift. Aden reveals that decades ago, he assisted the Master of the Magic Tower, who gifted him this sacred artifact crafted by his own hands. The Don Ring holds spatial enchantment: with a single injection of mana, full-body enchanted plate armor materializes over the wearer in milliseconds! Joshua teases his father, pointing out that an artifact of this caliber was strictly reserved for the designated heir of the duchy. Aden awkwardly clears his throat, barking that it was merely gathering dust in the armory and Joshua is only borrowing it for protection. Aden explains the real strategy: to navigate the treacherous vipers of Arcadia, Joshua will enter the capital under an alias, posing as a nameless viscount's heir. The armor carries hidden ducal runes—a silent warning to imperial royals that behind this mysterious child stands the wrath of Duke Aden himself! Then Duke Aden hesitates. In a rare moment of vulnerability, the iron-blooded warrior asks quietly: 'Joshua... do you hold resentment toward me?' Joshua falls silent. In his past life, Duke Aden neglected him and his mother, leading directly to Lucia's agonizing death in the servant quarters. But Joshua also remembers the brutal tragedy ten years later: Duke Aden falling in the imperial civil war, pierced by dozens of treacherous arrows in the mud. Time washes away old bitterness. In this new life, a bond that never existed before has begun to take root between father and son. Back in the present courtyard, a smooth, honeyed voice interrupts his thoughts: Prince Kaiser von Britton is walking over with a radiant smile. Flanked by Valmont and Evergrant, Kaiser playfully asks whether he should address him as Sir Joshua now that he holds royal knight status. Our boy hits him with an unenthusiastic deadpan glare, asking what business the fourth prince could possibly have with him. Kaiser extends an official invitation, urging Joshua to visit his palace the moment he arrives in the capital after clearing the northern monsters. Kaiser dangles massive bait: royal sponsorship can bypass the brutal annual entrance exams and exempt Joshua from ten years of mandatory military servitude! He frames the corrupt offer as innocent admiration, claiming that his political greed for Joshua's talent is simply too great to resist. Joshua feels sick to his stomach. That exact fake smile and honeyed tongue lured him into twenty years of thankless bloodshed in his previous life. If the imperial archmage weren't watching, Joshua would have summoned Lugia and driven thirty inches of divine steel straight through Kaiser's lying teeth! But Joshua needs imperial clearance for a greater purpose: deep inside the secret vaults of Galtika Palace sleeps his most cherished companion from his past life! Joshua waves his hand dismissively, casually telling the prince he'll think about it before turning on his heel and walking away! Valmont fumes at the boy's breathtaking disrespect, but Kaiser stops him with a dark, confident smirk: Joshua will fall into his net eventually. Joshua smirks back into the sun. The game has begun, and this time, the prince will be the one dancing like a puppet on strings! The expedition sets forth! Duke Aden leads an elite strike force of one hundred seasoned knights, thundering north toward the treacherous Loch Fief. Riding alongside the Duke are Count Chiffon and Master Magician Roman—the legendary Fourth Circle archmage who commands the dukedom's arcane corps. Beside Joshua rides Babel, keeping his eyes locked onto his brother the entire journey, burning with silent determination to prove his worth. Our boy laughs internally at Babel's intense staring contest before the lead scout halts the column outside the gates of Loch Castle! Loch Fief is a desolate frontier territory, bordering the abyssal wilderness known as the Forest of Dark Beings. Greeting them at the portcullis is Roben, a grizzled veteran of the Cloven Knights who dedicated decades of blood and sweat to the Agnus bloodline. Duke Aden dismounts and warmly greets the old veteran, expressing genuine remorse for having an honorable loyalist stationed in this desolate outpost. Roben places a hand over his breastplate, replying that being allowed to serve House Agnus even in retirement is his greatest lifelong privilege. Suddenly, heavy waddling footsteps shake the flagstones as a morbidly obese noble dressed in garish velvet bursts through the castle doors! This is Viscount Beeg, Lord of Loch Fief and a close maternal relative of Duchess Vanessa, sweating profusely with his young daughter Anna in tow. Duke Aden rubs his temples in immediate regret, thinking he should have bypassed the castle entirely and ridden straight into the monster woods. Beeg instantly turns on old knight Roben, loudly screeching that the veteran has gone senile for failing to announce the Duke's arrival with fanfares! As Roben endures the insults, Duke Aden steps in and roasts Beeg, remarking that the viscount seems to grow rounder and fatter every passing month. Beeg shamelessly giggles, rubbing his plump palms together and claiming that his expanding waistline is proof of the Duke's generous governance. In the background, Joshua and Babel share a rare moment of mutual agreement: this corrupt viscount is either completely brainless or impossibly shameless! Beeg turns to Babel with theatrical groveling, showering his high-born nephew in sickening praise about his burgeoning swordsmanship spirit. Babel brushes him off with a cool greeting. Beeg's eyes then wander over to Joshua, wondering who this unfamiliar child in fine traveling clothes could be. Beeg beams, assuming this is another high-ranking noble ward. Count Chiffon steps forward, awkwardly announcing that this is Lord Joshua. 'The second son of Duke Agnus,' Chiffon announces. Beeg blinks dumbly—when on earth did his cousin Vanessa conceive another child?! Chiffon pulls Beeg aside, frantically whispering the scandalous truth: Joshua has zero imperial royal blood—he was born to a common maid from the servant quarters! The jovial smile on Beeg's face instantly turns to stone. The warmth completely vanishes from his piggish eyes. Beeg nervously asks if the mother at least belonged to a fallen aristocratic family. Chiffon confirms she was nothing more than an illiterate commoner. Beeg turns back to face Joshua. The boy stands in front of him with tranquil, unwavering dignity, completely unbothered by the whispers. But in Beeg's poisoned mind, blood is everything. The sight of a commoner's bastard wearing fine ducal silks fills him with sickening revulsion. Looking down at the continent's youngest knight, the corrupt viscount sneers internally: 'Ugh... what a filthy, disgusting pig!' Joshua reads the fat noble's sneer instantly. Our boy has put hundreds of haughty aristocrats in early graves, and this corrupt pig is making the list! With treacherous nobles behind him and monstrous Black Ogres ahead in the dark woods, the stage is set for a brutal baptism of fire! An arrogant corrupt noble, a forest teeming with apex predators, and an unrivaled spear knight about to demonstrate who the real monster is. The bloodbath begins now!

[03:48 – 03:54] Shot 39: As Roben endures the insults, Duke Aden steps in and roasts Beeg, remarking that the viscount seems to grow rounder and fatter every passing month.
[03:54 – 04:00] Shot 40: Beeg shamelessly giggles, rubbing his plump palms together and claiming that his expanding waistline is proof of the Duke's generous governance.
[04:00 – 04:06] Shot 41: In the background, Joshua and Babel share a rare moment of mutual agreement: this corrupt viscount is either completely brainless or impossibly shameless!
[04:06 – 04:12] Shot 42: Beeg turns to Babel with theatrical groveling, showering his high-born nephew in sickening praise about his burgeoning swordsmanship spirit.
[04:12 – 04:18] Shot 43: Babel brushes him off with a cool greeting. Beeg's eyes then wander over to Joshua, wondering who this unfamiliar child in fine traveling clothes could be.
[04:18 – 04:24] Shot 44: Beeg beams, assuming this is another high-ranking noble ward. Count Chiffon steps forward, awkwardly announcing that this is Lord Joshua.
[04:24 – 04:30] Shot 45: 'The second son of Duke Agnus,' Chiffon announces. Beeg blinks dumbly—when on earth did his cousin Vanessa conceive another child?!
[04:30 – 04:36] Shot 46: Chiffon pulls Beeg aside, frantically whispering the scandalous truth: Joshua has zero imperial royal blood—he was born to a common maid from the servant quarters!
[04:36 – 04:42] Shot 47: The jovial smile on Beeg's face instantly turns to stone. The warmth completely vanishes from his piggish eyes.
[04:42 – 04:48] Shot 48: Beeg nervously asks if the mother at least belonged to a fallen aristocratic family. Chiffon confirms she was nothing more than an illiterate commoner.
[04:48 – 04:54] Shot 49: Beeg turns back to face Joshua. The boy stands in front of him with tranquil, unwavering dignity, completely unbothered by the whispers.
[04:54 – 05:00] Shot 50: But in Beeg's poisoned mind, blood is everything. The sight of a commoner's bastard wearing fine ducal silks fills him with sickening revulsion.
[05:00 – 05:06] Shot 51: Looking down at the continent's youngest knight, the corrupt viscount sneers internally: 'Ugh... what a filthy, disgusting pig!'
[05:06 – 05:12] Shot 52: Joshua reads the fat noble's sneer instantly. Our boy has put hundreds of haughty aristocrats in early graves, and this corrupt pig is making the list!
[05:12 – 05:18] Shot 53: With treacherous nobles behind him and monstrous Black Ogres ahead in the dark woods, the stage is set for a brutal baptism of fire!
[05:18 – 05:24] Shot 54: An arrogant corrupt noble, a forest teeming with apex predators, and an unrivaled spear knight about to demonstrate who the real monster is. The bloodbath begins now!

======================================================================
CHAPTER 16 VOICEOVER
======================================================================
CONTINUOUS RECORDING TEXT:
Outside Castle Loch, Viscount Beeg sneers down at Joshua like a peasant stepping on mud, muttering vile insults under his breath about commoner filth. Quickly pivoting back to groveling mode, Beeg tries to delay the Duke by proposing an opulent welcoming banquet with wine and roast meats. Duke Aden dismisses him instantly. His elite knights don't need nap times and feasts; they came to the frontier to hunt monsters, not lounge in luxury! Aden barks at his regiment, asking if a single soldier needs rest before entering the dark forest. One hundred swords rise with a deafening roar: 'NO SIR!' Seeing the Duke's resolve, Beeg hastily offers his local garrison to march alongside the ducal army, claiming he wishes to share in the glorious campaign. Aden asks what he plans to do with his young daughter Anna, pointing out that leaving a small child unguarded near a monster-infested border is reckless. Beeg beams with theatrical pride, nudging Anna forward and claiming that his little girl will actually be one of the expedition's greatest tactical assets! Anna curtsies politely, closing her eyes as glowing teal runes and concentric arcane circles manifest beneath her small boots! Swirling gusts of wind coalesce into a radiant, sparkling wind spirit! Anna whispers the sacred invocation: 'Sylph!' Even Archmage Roman, commander of the ducal arcane forces, gasps in utter astonishment: this little girl has successfully contracted with an authentic spirit! Spirit elementalists are exceedingly rare across the continent. While thousands study circle magic, an entire empire might only produce a hundred low-tier spirit summoners! Joshua studies the girl intently. In his past life, he knew every major elementalist on the continent, yet Anna's name never appeared in imperial history books. Yet her face triggers a nagging sense of familiarity. Joshua decides to observe her closely; if she poses any threat to his timeline, he'll deal with it accordingly. Recognizing the scouting value of wind spirits, Duke Aden permits Anna to join the ranks, assigning the local Loch infantry to guard the rear. As the column prepares to depart, Viscount Beeg steps directly in Joshua's path, whispering a venomous warning that he will be watching every step our boy takes! Joshua doesn't even blink. He treats the fat noble like a minor annoyance, knowing that frontier accidents happen very easily in monster woods. The camera cuts hundreds of leagues south to the imperial capital, where Emperor Marcus sits on his golden throne, receiving an urgent debrief. Evergrant confirms the shocking reports: Joshua didn't just pass the C-rank test; he blocked an expert B-rank captain twice and launched a devastating counterattack! Marcus demands Evergrant's unvarnished opinion as the greatest archmage in the realm: what is this nine-year-old child's true combat ceiling?! Evergrant frames his analysis through comparison. He states that if he fought Vice-Captain Valmont in a life-or-death duel, he is certain he would emerge victorious. 'However,' Evergrant whispers with chills, 'what I felt radiating from that nine-year-old boy was something fundamentally alien to ordinary human progression.' Marcus asks if the archmage fears losing to a toddler. Evergrant clarifies: right now he would win, but within ten years—or even five—the boy may stand above everyone! Marcus grins like an apex predator, dropping a lethal hypothetical: if Evergrant and the Emperor himself were to fight to the death, who would survive?! Evergrant freezes in utter terror. In the presence of a true Master, speaking the wrong answer means decapitation. He wisely keeps his mouth shut! Marcus bursts into roaring laughter! The sheer unpredictability of this Agnus prodigy delights him—he regrets not riding out to see the boy with his own eyes! SNAP! Marcus snaps his fingers, summoning his shadow spymaster Jacken to inquire about the Fourth Prince's movements in the capital. Jacken reports that Prince Kaiser has already submitted a formal royal recommendation to recruit Joshua directly into the Imperial Knights! Marcus considers the move. While he dislikes commoner-heavy mercenary battalions, having Kaiser aggressively recruit Joshua opens up fascinating political leverage. As his retainers withdraw, Emperor Marcus mutters to himself in the dim courtroom, plotting a far grander destiny for the young Agnus knight. 'Joshua von Agnus,' Marcus muses with a dark smile. 'With monstrous talent like that, he might be the perfect sacrifice for THAT secret project!' The camera cuts to the northern wilderness under a blood-red full moon, where thick crimson mist weaves through the ancient, gnarled trees. Demonic howls shatter the silence! Dozens of mutated orcs and goblins burst from the undergrowth, their bloodshot eyes locked onto fresh meat! In a narrow ravine, our boy Joshua finds himself completely encircled by an entire horde of frenzied monsters! These aren't common wilderness pests; these are mutated beasts empowered by the blood moon's corrupting mana, hungry for warm flesh! Despite muttering that things look bad, Joshua's combat instincts take over instantly, calculating distances, attack angles, and escape paths in fractions of a second. The lead orc lunges with a guttural roar, bringing a jagged iron axe down in a savage, cleaving strike aimed straight at Joshua's shoulder! With millimetric timing, our boy ducks beneath the rusted steel, driving a condensed mana palm strike right through the orc's solar plexus! CRACK! The kinetic blast shatters the monster's spine, sending the two-meter brute crashing backward into his screeching goblin pack! Three screeching goblins leap from the shrubbery to exploit his landing, but Joshua sweeps his boot in a spinning crescent wave of sapphire mana! The razor-sharp aura crescent decapitates all three scavengers in mid-air before their poisoned blades can even graze his cloak! Landing lightly on the frozen soil, Joshua realizes something is terribly wrong: wild beasts don't coordinate pincer traps unless an alpha is commanding them. Deep tremors shake the forest floor—THUD... THUD... The surrounding goblins screech and scatter in sheer terror, parting before a colossal shadow! Crashing through the towering pines is a terrifying four-meter nightmare: the apex mutated Black Ogre, bathed in blood-red moonlight! Dragging an iron-spiked timber club drenched in ancient gore, sulfuric steam billows from its fangs as it locks its murderous gaze on the small boy. Where ordinary knights would wet themselves, our boy Joshua breaks into a slow, dangerous grin. The real training session has finally arrived! He channels mana into the Don Ring! In a flash of sapphire light, enchanted battle armor materializes over his chest and arms, shielding his vital organs! Lugia leaps into his grip, crackling with divine lightning! Tonight, the sacred spear will feast on the blood of the forest's apex predator! The Black Ogre roars with deafening fury, swinging the colossal tree trunk downward with enough force to pulverize a fortress gate! Joshua launches diagonally like an azure streak, dodging by mere inches as the strike obliterates the stone floor into a smoking crater! Using the embedded club as a ramp, our boy sprints up the timber shaft with terrifying velocity, closing the distance to the beast's throat! He channels the Magic Spear Arts First Form: Lightning Strike, driving the twin prongs straight at the monster's jugular! The divine spear pierces the impenetrable obsidian hide like wet paper, detonating concentrated lightning directly inside the monster's chest! The four-meter behemoth crashes to the earth with a thunderous thud! The surrounding monsters drop their weapons, fleeing in blind, shrieking terror! Under the crimson moon, Joshua flicks the gore from his spear. The northern wolves came to hunt an easy prey, but they just awakened an unrivaled reaper!
