======================================================================
CHAPTER 17 VOICEOVER
======================================================================
CONTINUOUS RECORDING TEXT:
As twilight bathes the northern peaks in fiery amber light, Duke Aden orders his forces to halt and pitch camp on the open plain. Viscount Beeg waddles over in a panic, whining about why they are pitching tents in an exposed field instead of marching straight into the trees. Old knight Roben scoffs under his breath. The corrupt viscount knows everything about taxing peasants and zero about basic military strategy. Duke Aden shuts the coward down, educating him on the deadly reality of the northern woods when the sun dips below the horizon. Under the rising Red Moon, monster aggression spikes fivefold! Marching into dense foliage in the dark is an invitation to be slaughtered in an ambush. Realizing he just looked like an idiot, Beeg immediately switches to shameless flattery, praising the Duke's infinite strategic genius. Aden orders Babel and Joshua to pitch their own tents, treating them not as coddled high nobles, but as disciplined soldiers in the field. Under the clever excuse of gathering dry firewood for the camp, our boy Joshua steps into the dense tree line accompanied by a local guard. Joshua casually dismisses the guard, telling him to take the first bundle back to camp while he gathers a few more thick logs. The second the soldier disappears behind the brush, Joshua drops the twigs, turning and sprinting like a shadow into the forbidden heart of the forest! But Joshua wasn't entirely unobserved. Lurking behind an oak tree is Viscount Beeg, watching the young boy vanish into the darkness with a sinister sneer! Why is our boy risking his neck alone? Lore reveals the answer: he is hunting for an ancient relic known as the Primordial Stone! Born of pure primordial mana, these five legendary stones can instantly elevate a novice magician's spells by two full circles without strain! But for Joshua, its value is life-saving: only a Primordial Stone can stabilize his overflowing adult-tier mana and prevent his young core from bursting! In his previous life, explorers discovered one of the stones buried in the heart of this exact forest years later. Joshua is here to claim it first! After an hour of traversing treacherous terrain, the canopy thickens into near-total darkness, blotting out the stars completely. Heavy rhythmic tremors vibrate through the earth—THUD... THUD... Joshua freezes, instantly dropping flat behind a mossy log. Lumbering directly onto the central trail is a massive mutated Black Ogre, blocking the only direct route to the forest's sacred heart! Black Ogres shrug off low-grade magic like rain and require B-rank knights to bring down. Fighting one head-on right now will blow his cover and waste precious stamina. Joshua agilely scales a towering pine, deciding to deploy the secret stealth art of his past-life companion: the legendary Assassin King! Unlike combat arts that concentrate mana into sharp focal points, assassin stealth diffuses mana into a thin, imperceptible membrane around the skin. Joshua slows his heartbeat to one beat per minute, extinguishing his breath and matching his body temperature perfectly to the winter breeze. The Black Ogre stops inches beneath his perch, sniffing the air with confused, snorting grunts—smelling nothing but pine needles and damp earth! Finding no scent of prey, the beast lumbers away into the ravine. Joshua exhales softly—the Assassin King's technique still works like a charm! Joshua leaps across the high branches like an ethereal cat, bypassing the monster-infested forest floor entirely! But continuously maintaining full-body mana diffusion while performing high-speed gymnastics places enormous stress on a nine-year-old cardiovascular system. Disaster strikes! Landing on an ancient rotten bough, the wood fractures under his weight with a loud, echoing CRACK! Joshua plummets twelve meters through the canopy, crashing onto the hard frozen ground and bruising his ribs and spine! The loud crash instantly shatters the forest's silence. Hungry rustling sounds erupt from every bush surrounding the clearing! Over twenty berserk goblins and feral orcs step into the moonlight, brandishing rusted cleavers and baring blood-soaked fangs! Encircled with his back against a boulder, our boy sighs, muttering that an unconditioned body really makes basic mobility annoying. The first goblin lunges with saliva spraying from its jaws, thrusting a jagged poisoned dagger straight toward Joshua's neck! But instead of panicking, Joshua's eyes light up with pure exhilaration. He has been dying for a target dummy to test out his upgraded spear forms! He drops into a rock-solid horse stance, gripping the spear shaft horizontally with both hands as azure mana begins to vortex around his waist! Gale-force winds violently whip up around his boots! The air pressure drops drastically as compressed mana spirals into a howling vortex! Joshua whispers the ancient invocation: 'Magic Spear Arts, Second Phase, Second Form: Violent Gale!' He spins the spear in a full three-hundred-and-sixty-degree arc! A colossal cyclone of razor-sharp mana blades detonates thirty paces in every direction! The hurricane of energy slices through reinforced armor, iron bucklers, and monster bones like hot wire through butter, detonating the entire pack into red mist! The shockwave is so violent that twenty massive pine trees are cleanly sheared in half, crashing to the forest floor around the clearing! When the storm settles, not a single enemy remains breathing. The entire twenty-monster ambush was wiped from existence in a single heartbeat! Joshua checks his core: the Second Phase consumed barely twenty percent of his reserves. His temporal knot is loosening, unlocking greater efficiency! But as the ringing in his ears fades, Joshua picks up the faint sound of panicked human breathing hiding behind the shattered tree trunks. Crouched behind a boulder is Viscount Beeg, his round face white as a sheet, trembling violently after witnessing a nine-year-old butcher an army in seconds! The fat noble is practically wetting his trousers. If that demon boy realizes he was stalking him, Joshua could erase him right here and blame it on the monsters! Joshua deliberately plays along, pretending he didn't notice the fat lord trembling behind the rock. Let the corrupt coward stew in his own terror! Ahead in the mist, a soft, ethereal luminescence pulses from a hidden cavern—the undeniable aura of the Primordial Stone! Stepping inside the subterranean grotto, ancient crystalline formations glow along the cavern ceiling like a subterranean galaxy. Hovering above an underground spring is the legendary Primordial Stone of Storms, crackling with pure primal lightning! Joshua reaches out his hand. The moment Lugia's frequency harmonizes with the stone, the subterranean chamber illuminates in glorious sapphire light! The stone dissolves into liquid lightning, pouring straight through Joshua's palm into his spiritual core to permanently stabilize his power! A golden chime echoes in his soul as the temporal blockage completely unravels! His meridians expand, permanently cementing his core stability! Dawn approaches as military horns echo from the camp perimeter, signaling that Duke Aden's vanguard is preparing to enter the forest. Joshua steps out of the cavern with crystal-clear vision. His vessel has ascended, and his spear is ready to challenge even the Nine Stars! An unraveled seal, a petrified corrupt lord, and an unrivaled spear master about to show Duke Aden what true genius looks like. The campaign marches on!

[02:48 – 02:54] Shot 29: The loud crash instantly shatters the forest's silence. Hungry rustling sounds erupt from every bush surrounding the clearing!
[02:54 – 03:00] Shot 30: Over twenty berserk goblins and feral orcs step into the moonlight, brandishing rusted cleavers and baring blood-soaked fangs!
[03:00 – 03:06] Shot 31: Encircled with his back against a boulder, our boy sighs, muttering that an unconditioned body really makes basic mobility annoying.
[03:06 – 03:12] Shot 32: The first goblin lunges with saliva spraying from its jaws, thrusting a jagged poisoned dagger straight toward Joshua's neck!
[03:12 – 03:18] Shot 33: But instead of panicking, Joshua's eyes light up with pure exhilaration. He has been dying for a target dummy to test out his upgraded spear forms!
[03:18 – 03:24] Shot 34: He drops into a rock-solid horse stance, gripping the spear shaft horizontally with both hands as azure mana begins to vortex around his waist!
[03:24 – 03:30] Shot 35: Gale-force winds violently whip up around his boots! The air pressure drops drastically as compressed mana spirals into a howling vortex!
[03:30 – 03:36] Shot 36: Joshua whispers the ancient invocation: 'Magic Spear Arts, Second Phase, Second Form: Violent Gale!'
[03:36 – 03:42] Shot 37: He spins the spear in a full three-hundred-and-sixty-degree arc! A colossal cyclone of razor-sharp mana blades detonates thirty paces in every direction!
[03:42 – 03:48] Shot 38: The hurricane of energy slices through reinforced armor, iron bucklers, and monster bones like hot wire through butter, detonating the entire pack into red mist!
[03:48 – 03:54] Shot 39: The shockwave is so violent that twenty massive pine trees are cleanly sheared in half, crashing to the forest floor around the clearing!
[03:54 – 04:00] Shot 40: When the storm settles, not a single enemy remains breathing. The entire twenty-monster ambush was wiped from existence in a single heartbeat!
[04:00 – 04:06] Shot 41: Joshua checks his core: the Second Phase consumed barely twenty percent of his reserves. His temporal knot is loosening, unlocking greater efficiency!
[04:06 – 04:12] Shot 42: But as the ringing in his ears fades, Joshua picks up the faint sound of panicked human breathing hiding behind the shattered tree trunks.
[04:12 – 04:18] Shot 43: Crouched behind a boulder is Viscount Beeg, his round face white as a sheet, trembling violently after witnessing a nine-year-old butcher an army in seconds!
[04:18 – 04:24] Shot 44: The fat noble is practically wetting his trousers. If that demon boy realizes he was stalking him, Joshua could erase him right here and blame it on the monsters!
[04:24 – 04:30] Shot 45: Joshua deliberately plays along, pretending he didn't notice the fat lord trembling behind the rock. Let the corrupt coward stew in his own terror!
[04:30 – 04:36] Shot 46: Ahead in the mist, a soft, ethereal luminescence pulses from a hidden cavern—the undeniable aura of the Primordial Stone!
[04:36 – 04:42] Shot 47: Stepping inside the subterranean grotto, ancient crystalline formations glow along the cavern ceiling like a subterranean galaxy.
[04:42 – 04:48] Shot 48: Hovering above an underground spring is the legendary Primordial Stone of Storms, crackling with pure primal lightning!
[04:48 – 04:54] Shot 49: Joshua reaches out his hand. The moment Lugia's frequency harmonizes with the stone, the subterranean chamber illuminates in glorious sapphire light!
[04:54 – 05:00] Shot 50: The stone dissolves into liquid lightning, pouring straight through Joshua's palm into his spiritual core to permanently stabilize his power!
[05:00 – 05:06] Shot 51: A golden chime echoes in his soul as the temporal blockage completely unravels! His meridians expand, permanently cementing his core stability!
[05:06 – 05:12] Shot 52: Dawn approaches as military horns echo from the camp perimeter, signaling that Duke Aden's vanguard is preparing to enter the forest.
[05:12 – 05:18] Shot 53: Joshua steps out of the cavern with crystal-clear vision. His vessel has ascended, and his spear is ready to challenge even the Nine Stars!
[05:18 – 05:24] Shot 54: An unraveled seal, a petrified corrupt lord, and an unrivaled spear master about to show Duke Aden what true genius looks like. The campaign marches on!

======================================================================
CHAPTER 18 VOICEOVER
======================================================================
CONTINUOUS RECORDING TEXT:
Right after the gale-force detonation settles, dozens of shredded monsters crash onto the frozen forest loam like broken twigs. Leaning heavily against his spear, our boy Joshua gasps for breath as acute fatigue washes over his prepubescent muscles. He shakes his head in mild disappointment: that gale strike wasn't even twenty-five percent of its full potential in his previous life! But catching his breath will have to wait. Looking at the trampled trails, he realizes the primary monster horde is marching straight toward camp! Time is of the essence. Gritting his teeth, Joshua sprints across the rocky ridgeline, desperate to warn the knights before slaughter strikes! Down at the encampment, pitch-black night settles over the valley under the blood-red moon, making sentries grip their spears with sweaty palms. RUSTLE! A sudden tremor shakes the wooden fences as dozens of glowing red eyes ignite in the darkness like demonic embers! 'AMBUSH!' the sentry roars as a feral goblin lunges over the palisade, slitting a watchman's throat before the alarm horn can finish sounding! Chaos erupts across the campsite! Over a thousand demonic beasts flood the clearing, setting tents ablaze and overrunning the southern perimeter! Shouts of 'FORM A LINE!' and 'MEDIC!' echo through the flames as the vanguard fights a desperate retreat against wave after wave of claw and steel! Suddenly, a brilliant violet mana blade cuts through the smoke! Three goblins are sliced cleanly in half by textbook imperial swordplay! It is Babel von Agnus! Free of his former vanity, the young prodigy stands on the frontline like a rock, shielding wounded squires behind him! Beside Babel fights a mysterious cloaked warrior in dark leather, severing orc throats with lethal, fluid precision that leaves the ground littered with corpses! Babel is stunned. This cloaked fighter is operating far above C-rank based on pure swordsmanship alone—a hidden elite guarding the ducal line! But the relief is brief. An unending tide of over a thousand snarling beasts surges over the northern hill, threatening to swallow the entire battalion whole! Panic grips the junior lines as men cry out to abandon camp and flee toward Castle Loch before they are completely encircled! 'SHUT UP!' Count Chiffon roars, drawing his steel with crackling purple lightning and executing the first deserter on the spot to hold the line! 'Red Knights, lock shields!' Chiffon commands. The elite ducal vanguard interlocks heavy iron pavises, bracing for the impact of a charging tsunami! The monstrous wave crashes into the shield wall with bone-jarring violence! Spears thrust outward in disciplined rhythm, impaling dozens of beasts! As an expert B-rank commander, Chiffon proves his pedigree, darting through the breach like a purple phantom to decapitate three orc warriors in one breath! But just as Chiffon clears a perimeter, the temperature plummets as a suffocating demonic pressure bears down directly on his neck! Chiffon curses in disbelief: the lunar cycle shifted, meaning the Red Moon rose an entire day earlier than astrological forecasts predicted! Emerging through the burning debris is the true nightmare: an apex Black Ogre, mutated and supercharged by the premature lunar peak! The titan beats its chest with a ground-shaking roar! Sonic shockwaves send three squads of armored knights tumbling across the mud! Chiffon realizes he is the highest-ranked knight on the frontline. If he breaks, the entire camp—including Babel—will be slaughtered! Burning his remaining mana to the absolute brim, Chiffon lunges forward with a desperate cry: 'I'll split you in two!!' His blade aims straight for the creature's eye—but supercharged by the moon, the Black Ogre swats the steel away like an annoying mosquito! CRASH! A boulder-sized fist connects square with Chiffon's ribs, sending the B-rank commander flying fifty meters to crash into the dirt! Babel rushes to his side, but Chiffon can barely choke out words through bloody lips: the beast is too fast, and the camp is doomed! Towering above them, the Black Ogre raises its massive spiked club, preparing to smash both noble heirs into red paste! 'Knights must never lose their composure in any situation.' A deep, resonant voice cuts through the hurricane like ringing bronze. Stepping out from the command pavilion is Duke Aden von Agnus, wearing his white fur mantle and stepping calmly toward the front! He gently rests his gloved palm on the pommel of his colossal broadsword, murmuring six simple words: 'I'll take care of it from here.' The heavy steel glides from its scabbard! Blood-red mana instantly wraps around the two-meter blade, burning hotter than molten iron! The entire horde screeches as one, hundreds of bloodthirsty beasts and the Black Ogre charging straight at the lone master! Duke Aden exhales frosted air into the night. His piercing blue pupils lock onto the epicenter of the charging stampede. With a single, unhurried forward step, he sweeps the broadsword horizontally across the valley in one clean, effortless arc! BOOOOOM! An earth-shattering wave of scarlet mana erupts from the steel, splitting the horizon in two like a descending blade of heaven! The sheer atmospheric pressure vaporizes boulders, soil, and century-old pines into fine dust before the wave even touches flesh! When the blinding flash dissipates, the entire geography has been rewritten: a hundred-meter canyon stretches across the valley floor! The charging vanguard of several hundred monsters—including the mutated Black Ogre—has been completely deleted from the mortal realm! Surviving goblins on the edge of the crater stare into the smoking abyss with dilated, terrified pupils, utterly paralyzed! Standing at the edge of the ravine, Duke Aden gently flicks a single drop of dark blood from his sword, not a single hair out of place. Chiffon grovels in the dirt, completely breathless: 'In a single swing... hundreds of monsters were wiped off the face of the earth?!' Babel stares at his father's back in awe. This is the summit he must climb—an insurmountable peak of pure martial omnipotence! 'Is this the power of Duke Aden von Agnus?' Chiffon whispers in cold sweat. 'One of the legendary Nine Stars who rule the continent?!' The remaining horde breaks into full retreat, dropping weapons and scattering into the crags, knowing that human presence means absolute death! CLICK! Duke Aden sheathes his greatsword. But instead of returning to his tent, his piercing gaze snaps toward the eastern woods. Stepping out from the tree line is our boy Joshua, holding his spear and watching his father's handiwork with a knowing, appreciative smirk. Across the smoking valley, father and son exchange a silent look. With a Master's intuition, Aden senses that Joshua's mana has evolved yet again. Old knight Roben arrives with water and bandages, reporting that Castle Loch held firm and the outer perimeter is secured. The blood moon finally sinks below the horizon as golden morning rays wash over the northern crags, bringing an end to the night of terror. Aden declares the northern subjugation concluded. The order is given to strike camp: it's time to ride south to the imperial capital Arcadia! The forest is conquered, a Master's might revealed, and the road to Arcadia wide open. But with Duchess Vanessa waiting in the shadows, the deadlier battle begins now!

======================================================================
CHAPTER 19 VOICEOVER
======================================================================
CONTINUOUS RECORDING TEXT:
Right after obliterating the ambushing monster pack, our boy Joshua treks deeper into a jagged subterranean gorge, following a faint electromagnetic hum. Standing at the cliff edge, his hair flutters in the charged static air, guided by the memories of his previous life's northern campaigns. Deep down in the fissure, a colossal crystalline spire pulses with untamed celestial lightning—the legendary Primordial Stone of Storms, Bronto! This is the divine anchor he desperately needed. His nine-year-old mana circuits were on the verge of collapsing from explosive technique backlash. Breathing in rhythm with Lugia's hidden hum, Joshua siphons a refined strand of Bronto's frequency directly into his core, harmonizing his unstable energy. But miles away at the expedition encampment, the blood moon burns with sinister crimson light, amplifying the madness of every northern beast. Even after Duke Aden's catastrophic sword strike cleaved a hundred-meter trench, the atmosphere in the forward camp remains suffocatingly tense. Meanwhile, Babel is still shivering from his near-death encounter, but Count Rebrecca immediately swoops in with sickening noble flattery. The fat count laughs that only the legitimate heir could ever carry Duke Aden's invincible legacy, inflating Babel's battered ego back to maximum levels. But glancing around the perimeter, Rebrecca realizes someone critical is missing from the muster ranks—our boy Joshua is nowhere to be seen! Marching over to Chiffon, Rebrecca sneers loudly, asking where that insolent concubine's brat disappeared to during the chaotic assault. Nursing his cracked ribs, Chiffon admits he lost track of the boy, instantly sparking malicious rumors across the tired guard battalions. Rebrecca openly laughs, declaring that despite all Joshua's arrogant posturing, when real monsters showed up, the commoner's brat deserted to save his own skin! The camp soldiers immediately jump on the gossip bandwagon, claiming Joshua's lowborn upbringing finally proved he has zero warrior backbone. But before their slanders can spread another inch, Duke Aden turns with an icy glare that freezes the blood of every gossiping noble in their tracks! Aden roars at his men to drop the petty court politics—a seasoned commander knows when the forest's blood lust is reaching its boiling point! Right on cue, the earth beneath their boots begins to tremble violently, tossing iron kettles and weapons across the frozen mud! Massive pine trunks splinter and snap along the ridge, as dozens of crimson eyes ignite through the swirling nocturnal mist! An entire vanguard of mutated Black Ogres bursts through the outer barricades, each towering titan supercharged by the lunar curse! Frontline knights are swatted across the dirt like dry leaves, their enchanted shields folding like tin foil beneath catastrophic club strikes! Chiffon turns ghost-white: a single Black Ogre nearly snapped his spine in two, but facing three blood-frenzied behemoths at once is certain death! Young Babel's legs turn to jelly as he drops behind his escort, while fat Count Rebrecca trips over his cape, squealing in undiluted dread. The alpha ogre raises a massive tree-trunk club to crush the command tent, when suddenly, the atmospheric pressure plummets to absolute zero! A deafening thunderclap splits the clouds as a piercing azure lightning spear arcs from the heavens at hypersonic velocity! Clad in crackling celestial plasma, our boy Joshua drops out of the sky like an enraged deity, his wooden spear radiating pure divine judgment! Piercing straight through the lead ogre's skull, tens of thousands of volts explode through the titan's nervous system, instantly cooking its brain! The five-ton behemoth drops like a lead weight, dead before its massive body even hits the ground, plunging the entire clearing into stunned silence! Standing proudly atop the monster's smoldering carcass, Joshua wipes a speck of ash from his cheek, looking completely bored by the spectacle. Chiffon rubs his eyes in complete existential crisis: killing a lunar-buffed Black Ogre in one single blow should require an entire B-rank knight company! Duke Aden watches quietly from the back, recognizing the pristine signature of elemental lightning infused directly into physical spear craft! Unable to accept that a lowborn child achieved what he couldn't, Rebrecca shrieks that it had to be a trick—Duke Aden must have struck from the shadows! Joshua shoots the fat count a sideways glance that could curdle milk, utterly disgusted by aristocrats who bark loudest when danger is furthest. But two more enraged ogres charge from the flanks, roaring for revenge as their iron morningstars whistle through the nocturnal gloom! Our boy calmly warns the trembling guards to fall back twenty paces unless they want their steel breastplates permanently welded to their flesh! Closing his eyes for a split second, Joshua synchronizes his heartbeat with the refined frequency of the Primordial Stone Bronto. He knows his nine-year-old vessel can only sustain one full-output burst, so he decides to eradicate every attacking beast in a single blast! Planting his boots into the soil, Joshua spins his weapon in a wide circle, unleashing the forbidden form: Magic Spear Technique, Thunderstorm! A cataclysmic dome of electrical scythes explodes across the camp, carving through ogre armor, flesh, and stone with surgical brutality! Both attacking behemoths are instantly vaporized into smoldering charcoal, sending shockwaves that blow the cloaks clean off the watching knights! Count Rebrecca hits his knees in the mud like a sack of grain, his jaw trembling as his entire worldview of noble superiority shatters to pieces. Veteran knights whisper in terror: wielding physical spear arts while freely manipulating elemental lightning is the mythical trademark of a Magic Swordsman! In the center of the crater, Joshua forces his chin up high. He knows that in the cutthroat Agnus family, showing even a hint of weakness is fatal. Watching from ten paces away, Duke Aden notices the micro-tremors in Joshua's hands, quietly respecting the iron will of his youngest boy. Court Archmage Arman steps forward, adjusting his spectacles with trembling fingers as he assesses the magical mechanics behind the devastation. Duke Aden quietly asks his court mage for an unfiltered assessment: how does Joshua compare against the prodigies of the continent? Arman gasps that in magic circles, Joshua is already performing above a third-class wizard, effortlessly pulling off the impossible miracle of double casting! Arman proclaims that Joshua isn't just talented—he is a generational monster who could reshape imperial history and fulfill Duke Aden's deepest ambitions! Aden nods slowly. For the first time in nine years, the iron duke sees the true future sovereign of House Agnus standing before him. But back in the clearing, the extreme backlash hits all at once. Joshua's vision blurs into darkness as his small body reaches its absolute physical limit. Seeing the boy stagger, a wounded Black Ogre rears up from the ash, lunging with a brutal downward hammer fist to crush him into paste! Before the beast's fist can travel an inch, a sonic boom shatters the air as Duke Aden blurs across the field, cleaving the ogre cleanly in two! Showered in evaporating monster blood, Duke Aden effortlessly catches his falling son, wrapping him securely against his armored chest. Feeling the warmth of his father's protective embrace, Joshua lets his weary eyes close, drifting to sleep with a confident, victorious smirk. With the northern horrors conquered and a prodigy awakened, the stage is set for the journey home. But what dark premonitions await Joshua in his dreams?

[02:00 – 02:06] Shot 21: Chiffon turns ghost-white: a single Black Ogre nearly snapped his spine in two, but facing three blood-frenzied behemoths at once is certain death!
[02:06 – 02:12] Shot 22: Young Babel's legs turn to jelly as he drops behind his escort, while fat Count Rebrecca trips over his cape, squealing in undiluted dread.
[02:12 – 02:18] Shot 23: The alpha ogre raises a massive tree-trunk club to crush the command tent, when suddenly, the atmospheric pressure plummets to absolute zero!
[02:18 – 02:24] Shot 24: A deafening thunderclap splits the clouds as a piercing azure lightning spear arcs from the heavens at hypersonic velocity!
[02:24 – 02:30] Shot 25: Clad in crackling celestial plasma, our boy Joshua drops out of the sky like an enraged deity, his wooden spear radiating pure divine judgment!
[02:30 – 02:36] Shot 26: Piercing straight through the lead ogre's skull, tens of thousands of volts explode through the titan's nervous system, instantly cooking its brain!
[02:36 – 02:42] Shot 27: The five-ton behemoth drops like a lead weight, dead before its massive body even hits the ground, plunging the entire clearing into stunned silence!
[02:42 – 02:48] Shot 28: Standing proudly atop the monster's smoldering carcass, Joshua wipes a speck of ash from his cheek, looking completely bored by the spectacle.
[02:48 – 02:54] Shot 29: Chiffon rubs his eyes in complete existential crisis: killing a lunar-buffed Black Ogre in one single blow should require an entire B-rank knight company!
[02:54 – 03:00] Shot 30: Duke Aden watches quietly from the back, recognizing the pristine signature of elemental lightning infused directly into physical spear craft!
[03:00 – 03:06] Shot 31: Unable to accept that a lowborn child achieved what he couldn't, Rebrecca shrieks that it had to be a trick—Duke Aden must have struck from the shadows!
[03:06 – 03:12] Shot 32: Joshua shoots the fat count a sideways glance that could curdle milk, utterly disgusted by aristocrats who bark loudest when danger is furthest.
[03:12 – 03:18] Shot 33: But two more enraged ogres charge from the flanks, roaring for revenge as their iron morningstars whistle through the nocturnal gloom!
[03:18 – 03:24] Shot 34: Our boy calmly warns the trembling guards to fall back twenty paces unless they want their steel breastplates permanently welded to their flesh!
[03:24 – 03:30] Shot 35: Closing his eyes for a split second, Joshua synchronizes his heartbeat with the refined frequency of the Primordial Stone Bronto.
[03:30 – 03:36] Shot 36: He knows his nine-year-old vessel can only sustain one full-output burst, so he decides to eradicate every attacking beast in a single blast!
[03:36 – 03:42] Shot 37: Planting his boots into the soil, Joshua spins his weapon in a wide circle, unleashing the forbidden form: Magic Spear Technique, Thunderstorm!
[03:42 – 03:48] Shot 38: A cataclysmic dome of electrical scythes explodes across the camp, carving through ogre armor, flesh, and stone with surgical brutality!
[03:48 – 03:54] Shot 39: Both attacking behemoths are instantly vaporized into smoldering charcoal, sending shockwaves that blow the cloaks clean off the watching knights!
[03:54 – 04:00] Shot 40: Count Rebrecca hits his knees in the mud like a sack of grain, his jaw trembling as his entire worldview of noble superiority shatters to pieces.
[04:00 – 04:06] Shot 41: Veteran knights whisper in terror: wielding physical spear arts while freely manipulating elemental lightning is the mythical trademark of a Magic Swordsman!
[04:06 – 04:12] Shot 42: In the center of the crater, Joshua forces his chin up high. He knows that in the cutthroat Agnus family, showing even a hint of weakness is fatal.
[04:12 – 04:18] Shot 43: Watching from ten paces away, Duke Aden notices the micro-tremors in Joshua's hands, quietly respecting the iron will of his youngest boy.
[04:18 – 04:24] Shot 44: Court Archmage Arman steps forward, adjusting his spectacles with trembling fingers as he assesses the magical mechanics behind the devastation.
[04:24 – 04:30] Shot 45: Duke Aden quietly asks his court mage for an unfiltered assessment: how does Joshua compare against the prodigies of the continent?
[04:30 – 04:36] Shot 46: Arman gasps that in magic circles, Joshua is already performing above a third-class wizard, effortlessly pulling off the impossible miracle of double casting!
[04:36 – 04:42] Shot 47: Arman proclaims that Joshua isn't just talented—he is a generational monster who could reshape imperial history and fulfill Duke Aden's deepest ambitions!
[04:42 – 04:48] Shot 48: Aden nods slowly. For the first time in nine years, the iron duke sees the true future sovereign of House Agnus standing before him.
[04:48 – 04:54] Shot 49: But back in the clearing, the extreme backlash hits all at once. Joshua's vision blurs into darkness as his small body reaches its absolute physical limit.
[04:54 – 05:00] Shot 50: Seeing the boy stagger, a wounded Black Ogre rears up from the ash, lunging with a brutal downward hammer fist to crush him into paste!
[05:00 – 05:06] Shot 51: Before the beast's fist can travel an inch, a sonic boom shatters the air as Duke Aden blurs across the field, cleaving the ogre cleanly in two!
[05:06 – 05:12] Shot 52: Showered in evaporating monster blood, Duke Aden effortlessly catches his falling son, wrapping him securely against his armored chest.
[05:12 – 05:18] Shot 53: Feeling the warmth of his father's protective embrace, Joshua lets his weary eyes close, drifting to sleep with a confident, victorious smirk.
[05:18 – 05:24] Shot 54: With the northern horrors conquered and a prodigy awakened, the stage is set for the journey home. But what dark premonitions await Joshua in his dreams?

======================================================================
CHAPTER 20 VOICEOVER
======================================================================
CONTINUOUS RECORDING TEXT:
Carried through the smoking embers in his father's sturdy arms, our boy Joshua feels the heavy weight of exhaustion drag him down. For an old warrior who spent his entire previous life betrayed, unloved, and fighting alone, this rare feeling of safety feels almost surreal. He lets his guard down and slips into unconsciousness, but what begins as peaceful rest instantly twists into a waking apocalyptic nightmare! Desperate screams shatter the void as colossal stone spires topple into ash, trapped inhabitants pleading for help through choking plumes of black smoke! Floating above the devastation as a spirit, Joshua rubs his spectral eyes in shock—this isn't some random castle, it is the ancestral seat of House Agnus! He tries to convince himself it is just a nightmare, but the choking heat, the smell of burnt flesh, and the spiritual mana are terrifyingly real. Heavy metallic footsteps drag through the courtyard embers as a battered warrior in scorched red officer regalia stumbles through the inferno. When the firelight illuminates the knight's scarred face, Joshua's heart skips a beat: trudging through the ruins is an adult, battle-hardened Babel! The haughty young master of today is gone, replaced by a broken commander weeping helpless tears as his ducal legacy crumbles into smoking rubble. Doing the math on Babel's age, Joshua realizes this apocalyptic purge occurred roughly ten years in the future, during the climax of the imperial civil war! Absorbing the Primordial Stone of Storms has resonated with his regressed soul, ripping open a psychic portal into the unwritten future timeline! Out of nowhere, razor-sharp wind scythes slice through the smoke, shredding Babel's breastplate and hurling him violently against a marble pillar! Parting the storm clouds, a magnificent winged wind serpent glides into the courtyard, radiating supreme pressure that crushes the oxygen from the air! Joshua's phantom recoils in horror: that divine beast is Sylion, the legendary High-Rank Wind Spirit spoken of only in ancient summoner myths! Stepping off the spirit's crown is a strikingly gorgeous woman with braided amber hair, draped in embroidered robes that billow like autumn leaves. Joshua freezes in disbelief: that ruthless conqueror is none other than Anna Bell Grace, the adult persona of little Iceline from Count Rebrecca's estate! She looks down at the bleeding Babel and speaks, but her voice is completely masked by impenetrable silence magic, scrambling her words into ancient runes! Unable to hear the exchange, Joshua grits his teeth in frustration, desperately trying to read her lips to uncover the motive behind the dukedom's downfall. But a horrifying realization dawns upon him: Anna never acted independently—if she is here with a High Spirit, her enigmatic master must be lurking nearby! Clinging to his family pride, Babel staggers to his feet, screaming that a pack of traitors can never extinguish the eternal glory of House Agnus! But a spectral chimera lunges from the shadows with bone-crushing speed, snapping Babel's enchanted broadsword in half like a dry matchstick! From behind the blazing colonnade steps a tall, cloaked figure, exuding an aura of pure continental authority that suffocates the entire courtyard. Pulling down his hood with graceful ease, the man reveals a handsome face crowned by a sickening, mockingly superior smirk! Joshua's spirit violently shakes with pure hatred as he recognizes the continent's most ruthless mastermind: Draxia Bell Grace! His overwhelming surge of emotional shock fractures the psychic connection, detonating the prophetic vision into blinding white shards! Gasping for oxygen, our boy Joshua violently bolts upright in bed, clutching his chest as cold sweat drenching his bandages! Looking down, he realizes he is resting inside a VIP recovery chamber at Castle Loch, meticulously wrapped in high-grade healing salves. He clenches his fists to check his mana circuit—and what he feels brings a fierce smirk to his bruised lips. The chaotic lightning from Bronto didn't cripple him—it completely expanded his meridians, pushing his Magic Spear Arts directly into the second stage! While still officially a C-Rank knight, his elemental output and perfected technique allow him to easily dismantle veteran B-Rank masters with ease. A quiet voice suddenly cuts through the silence, as a familiar armored silhouette gazes out at the tranquil night sky from the window. Duke Aden sits motionless in an armchair, watching over his recovering son with the quiet vigilance of an apex predator. Aden remarks on how peacefully the stars shine now that the blood moon has passed, offering rare praise for Joshua's heroic stand. Shifting into business mode, the Duke lays out their immediate schedule: as soon as they reach the dukedom, Joshua will ride straight to the capital! Aden warns that walking into Arcadia as an official Agnus heir would paint a massive bullseye on his forehead for every scheming imperial faction. Between Fourth Prince Kaiser's secret police and rival ducal families, an acknowledged Agnus prodigy would be targeted for poison or blackmail immediately. To shield him from political sabotage, Aden has forged a complete false pedigree: Joshua will enroll as the sole orphan of the fallen Viscount Frederick! A ruined countryside noble striving to revive his dead estate will be dismissed as harmless fodder, giving Joshua total freedom to train undisturbed. Joshua nods without hesitation. Having lived through decades of palace treachery, he knows a low profile is the greatest tactical weapon of all. Operating under the radar means he can plunder ancient dungeons, recruit forgotten prodigies, and master Lugia without Emperor Marcus interfering! Duke Aden pushes himself up from his chair, preparing to let his son rest, but suddenly halts in his tracks, freezing the room's temperature once more. Turning back with an unreadable expression, the Duke's piercing eyes lock onto Joshua with the intensity of an interrogator hunting treason. Joshua feels a chill run down his spine, immediately realizing he must have muttered something catastrophic while trapped in his psychic nightmare. Aden steps closer to the bed, murmuring in a low, thunderous tone that while delirious with fever, Joshua uttered a very interesting name. Leaning over the mattress, Duke Aden speaks the forbidden name that once bathed half the empire in blood: Draxia Bell Grace! Joshua's pupils contract into needlepoints: in his fury during the vision, he shouted the mastermind's name at the top of his lungs! Aden's voice drops to a deadly whisper, demanding to know how a child raised in an isolated ducal barn could possibly know that classified imperial taboo! Pin-drop silence falls over the chamber. One wrong syllable could expose Joshua's regression, while a clumsy lie will insult the Duke's intellect. Forcing his breathing to stay even, our boy Joshua prepares to navigate the deadliest interrogation of his life against the iron duke himself! Because beyond this bedroom and past the northern mountains, the imperial capital of Arcadia awaits—a city of gold, glory, and ruthless assassinations! Back at the Agnus estate, Duchess Vanessa is already pouring venomous wine, believing Joshua will return home only to find his mother in a grave! In the palace, Fourth Prince Kaiser plots his ascension, completely unaware that his former weapon of slaughter has returned as his executioner! Wrapped safely within his spatial ring, the divine spear Lugia hums with anticipation, ready to taste the blood of empire traitors once more! Twenty chapters conquered, the first major trial overcome, and the path to total continental supremacy carved in stone. The legend of the Spear Knight has officially begun!

======================================================================
CHAPTER 21 VOICEOVER
======================================================================
CONTINUOUS RECORDING TEXT:
Right after dropping the most radioactive name in imperial history, the air in the bedroom freezes into pure liquid nitrogen. Our boy Joshua stares back with wide eyes, his mind frantically calculating how a nine-year-old stable boy could explain knowing a classified continental war criminal. For five agonizing seconds, father and son engage in a battle of wills, before Duke Aden exhales a heavy breath and decides to drop the interrogation. The Duke tells him to rest up: whatever secrets the boy holds can wait, because tomorrow morning the entire army marches back home to House Agnus. Collapsing back onto his pillows, Joshua lets out the longest sigh of his life. That was way too close—one slip of the tongue nearly blew his entire cover! But while our hero rests, beneath the silent stone towers of Castle Loch, a nest of vipers is already cooking up a toxic new scheme. Deep inside a secluded basement vault, fat Viscount Beeg is practically sweating through his velvet doublet, pacing back and forth in pure panic. Beeg demands to know if his sister, Duchess Vanessa, is aware that the concubine's son just stole the entire northern campaign right out from under Babel's nose! Nursing his bruised ribs, Chiffon confirms the Duchess already received the report—and to say she is losing her aristocratic mind would be an understatement. Beeg stammers in complete denial: Joshua spent nine years shoveling dung in the stables! How on earth does a peasant brat awaken world-class martial arts alone?! Even young Lord Babel, hailed across the empire as a generational prodigy, required royal instructors, legendary elixirs, and personal tutoring from the Duke to reach his level. Yet Joshua received zero pennies, zero tutors, and zero resources—and still managed to one-shot an enraged Black Ogre that nearly turned Chiffon into horse paste! Chiffon admits the entire garrison is in awe, with veteran centurions swearing the concubine's son is the second coming of an ancient Magic Swordsman. But Beeg sneers with theatrical disgust: Magic Swordsmen are fairy tales! In the real world, sudden explosive power only comes from one forbidden source. The corrupt viscount claims Joshua must have made an illicit blood contract with a high-ranking Demon from the nether realms to steal dark power! Chiffon scoffs at the theory: Duke Aden inspected Joshua's mana directly, and if there were even a speck of demonic taint, the Duke would have cleaved the boy in two on the spot. Beeg laughs at Chiffon's naivety: in high-stakes imperial politics, truth is irrelevant! All they need is a believable rumor to paint a target on the boy's head! The viscount smirks that Joshua is still just an immature child who wanted to show off. That flashy lightning display will become the very noose that hangs him! Beeg reveals his trump card: his own daughter, little Anna Bell Grace, is attending the Imperial Academy in Arcadia this term! With Anna serving as their inside spy, they can monitor Joshua's every move in the capital, making sure the concubine's son never threatens Babel's succession. Meanwhile, hundreds of miles to the south, warm sunshine glints off the majestic marble terraces of the main Agnus Ducal estate. Up on the royal balcony, Princess Cheryl—Lady Charles of the Pontier family—is leaning against the railing, looking thoroughly bored out of her mind. Her loyal retainer, old knight Cox, comes charging out onto the terrace like an excited bulldog, shouting that urgent news has arrived from the northern frontier! Panting for air, Cox reports that young Joshua successfully crushed the monster horde, and the entire dukedom is preparing an enormous victory banquet in his honor! But Cheryl barely reacts, letting out a sluggish sigh that leaves Cox scratching his bearded chin in utter bewilderment. Cox is shocked: usually, the princess is obsessing over Joshua! Why is she acting like a deflated balloon right when her favorite person returns? Cheryl's gaze turns somber as she reminds Cox of the brutal political reality that brought her to House Agnus in the first place. The legendary Mercenary King unexpectedly formed an alliance with the rival Cromwell faction, putting House Pontier under imminent threat of financial and military ruin! Her father sent her here as a political sacrificial pawn—her sole mission was to charm and marry Babel to drag Duke Aden's armies into their civil dispute! Cox softens his voice, admitting he knows Cheryl despises Babel's arrogant personality and cannot bear the thought of becoming a political trophy wife. Cox looks her in the eye: whenever she interacts with Joshua, her genuine smile returns. He urges her not to surrender her happiness for an ungrateful family! Cheryl flushes red down to her neck, stammering that Joshua was an insolent brat who told her to shut up and slammed a door directly in her face! But Cox merely chuckles, gently proposing that before Joshua departs for the capital, she should invite him to a private meal and speak from the heart. Pretending to be reluctant, Cheryl agrees to the meeting, secretly pleased that she will get a chance to see her mysterious spear knight once more! That very evening, night falls as dazzling fireworks detonate across the sky, painting the city in vibrant showers of ruby, gold, and sapphire! The central banquet hall is bursting at the seams with feast platters, roaring laughter, and endless casks of northern ale celebrating the expedition's victory! Frontline knights drink until their faces turn red, telling wild stories of young Lord Joshua descending from the sky to obliterate colossal monsters in a single blow! But while the party rages downstairs, the man of the hour is already slipping away into the quiet upper corridors, accompanied by a loyal knight officer. The knight chuckles that the guest of honor shouldn't sneak out so early, casually addressing our boy as "my liege" without missing a single beat. Joshua gives him a deadpan look, warning him that throwing around titles like "my liege" inside the ducal estate will have Duchess Vanessa's inquisitors hunting him by midnight! But the knight doesn't care. Having witnessed Joshua's peerless courage on the frozen frontline, he has already sworn his eternal sword to the young master! Joshua rubs his temple with a smirk: "Do I not get a vote in who pledges fealty to me?" The knight merely laughs, bowing with unshakable noble devotion. Wishing him a peaceful recovery, the loyal retainer departs, leaving Joshua alone in the grand hallway beneath the cold glow of the arched stained glass. Watching him leave, bitter memories from thirty years ago surge to the surface of Joshua's mind like acid, reopening scars that never truly healed. In his previous life, dozens of powerful knights and lords begged to serve under him. But our boy rejected every single offer without hesitation. He had vowed to be Kaiser's singular sword. He intentionally avoided building a personal faction, terrified that gathering followers would make Kaiser suspect him of treason! He sacrificed fame, power, and comrades just to protect Kaiser's fragile ego—and in the end, Kaiser still poisoned him like a stray dog in the dark! A sudden jolt of PTSD strikes his skull like a hammer. Draxia Bell Grace... that treacherous snake was standing right beside Kaiser, smiling as Joshua bled out! Gasping for air against a stone pillar, Joshua is suddenly interrupted by the quiet footfalls of the ducal head butler stepping out of the dark. The old servant inquires if the young master requires a doctor. Joshua shakes his head dismissively, telling the butler to go about his business. But the butler refuses to move, announcing in a flat, unyielding tone that a private dining table has already been prepared and everyone is waiting. Joshua frowns with annoyance, reminding the servant that he already declined the public banquet because of his battle fatigue and injuries. Leaning forward with a chilling glint in his eye, the butler drops the hammer: this isn't a public party—Duchess Vanessa has personally set a private table just for him! And the final poisoned hook? "Your mother Lucia has already arrived at the table." In a split second, Joshua's world turns to ice—Vanessa has taken his mother hostage!

[00:48 – 00:54] Shot 09: Nursing his bruised ribs, Chiffon confirms the Duchess already received the report—and to say she is losing her aristocratic mind would be an understatement.
[00:54 – 01:00] Shot 10: Beeg stammers in complete denial: Joshua spent nine years shoveling dung in the stables! How on earth does a peasant brat awaken world-class martial arts alone?!
[01:00 – 01:06] Shot 11: Even young Lord Babel, hailed across the empire as a generational prodigy, required royal instructors, legendary elixirs, and personal tutoring from the Duke to reach his level.
[01:06 – 01:12] Shot 12: Yet Joshua received zero pennies, zero tutors, and zero resources—and still managed to one-shot an enraged Black Ogre that nearly turned Chiffon into horse paste!
[01:12 – 01:18] Shot 13: Chiffon admits the entire garrison is in awe, with veteran centurions swearing the concubine's son is the second coming of an ancient Magic Swordsman.
[01:18 – 01:24] Shot 14: But Beeg sneers with theatrical disgust: Magic Swordsmen are fairy tales! In the real world, sudden explosive power only comes from one forbidden source.
[01:24 – 01:30] Shot 15: The corrupt viscount claims Joshua must have made an illicit blood contract with a high-ranking Demon from the nether realms to steal dark power!
[01:30 – 01:36] Shot 16: Chiffon scoffs at the theory: Duke Aden inspected Joshua's mana directly, and if there were even a speck of demonic taint, the Duke would have cleaved the boy in two on the spot.
[01:36 – 01:42] Shot 17: Beeg laughs at Chiffon's naivety: in high-stakes imperial politics, truth is irrelevant! All they need is a believable rumor to paint a target on the boy's head!
[01:42 – 01:48] Shot 18: The viscount smirks that Joshua is still just an immature child who wanted to show off. That flashy lightning display will become the very noose that hangs him!
[01:48 – 01:54] Shot 19: Beeg reveals his trump card: his own daughter, little Anna Bell Grace, is attending the Imperial Academy in Arcadia this term!
[01:54 – 02:00] Shot 20: With Anna serving as their inside spy, they can monitor Joshua's every move in the capital, making sure the concubine's son never threatens Babel's succession.
[02:00 – 02:06] Shot 21: Meanwhile, hundreds of miles to the south, warm sunshine glints off the majestic marble terraces of the main Agnus Ducal estate.
[02:06 – 02:12] Shot 22: Up on the royal balcony, Princess Cheryl—Lady Charles of the Pontier family—is leaning against the railing, looking thoroughly bored out of her mind.
[02:12 – 02:18] Shot 23: Her loyal retainer, old knight Cox, comes charging out onto the terrace like an excited bulldog, shouting that urgent news has arrived from the northern frontier!
[02:18 – 02:24] Shot 24: Panting for air, Cox reports that young Joshua successfully crushed the monster horde, and the entire dukedom is preparing an enormous victory banquet in his honor!
[02:24 – 02:30] Shot 25: But Cheryl barely reacts, letting out a sluggish sigh that leaves Cox scratching his bearded chin in utter bewilderment.
[02:30 – 02:36] Shot 26: Cox is shocked: usually, the princess is obsessing over Joshua! Why is she acting like a deflated balloon right when her favorite person returns?
[02:36 – 02:42] Shot 27: Cheryl's gaze turns somber as she reminds Cox of the brutal political reality that brought her to House Agnus in the first place.
[02:42 – 02:48] Shot 28: The legendary Mercenary King unexpectedly formed an alliance with the rival Cromwell faction, putting House Pontier under imminent threat of financial and military ruin!
[02:48 – 02:54] Shot 29: Her father sent her here as a political sacrificial pawn—her sole mission was to charm and marry Babel to drag Duke Aden's armies into their civil dispute!
[02:54 – 03:00] Shot 30: Cox softens his voice, admitting he knows Cheryl despises Babel's arrogant personality and cannot bear the thought of becoming a political trophy wife.
[03:00 – 03:06] Shot 31: Cox looks her in the eye: whenever she interacts with Joshua, her genuine smile returns. He urges her not to surrender her happiness for an ungrateful family!
[03:06 – 03:12] Shot 32: Cheryl flushes red down to her neck, stammering that Joshua was an insolent brat who told her to shut up and slammed a door directly in her face!
[03:12 – 03:18] Shot 33: But Cox merely chuckles, gently proposing that before Joshua departs for the capital, she should invite him to a private meal and speak from the heart.
[03:18 – 03:24] Shot 34: Pretending to be reluctant, Cheryl agrees to the meeting, secretly pleased that she will get a chance to see her mysterious spear knight once more!
[03:24 – 03:30] Shot 35: That very evening, night falls as dazzling fireworks detonate across the sky, painting the city in vibrant showers of ruby, gold, and sapphire!
[03:30 – 03:36] Shot 36: The central banquet hall is bursting at the seams with feast platters, roaring laughter, and endless casks of northern ale celebrating the expedition's victory!
[03:36 – 03:42] Shot 37: Frontline knights drink until their faces turn red, telling wild stories of young Lord Joshua descending from the sky to obliterate colossal monsters in a single blow!
[03:42 – 03:48] Shot 38: But while the party rages downstairs, the man of the hour is already slipping away into the quiet upper corridors, accompanied by a loyal knight officer.
[03:48 – 03:54] Shot 39: The knight chuckles that the guest of honor shouldn't sneak out so early, casually addressing our boy as "my liege" without missing a single beat.
[03:54 – 04:00] Shot 40: Joshua gives him a deadpan look, warning him that throwing around titles like "my liege" inside the ducal estate will have Duchess Vanessa's inquisitors hunting him by midnight!
[04:00 – 04:06] Shot 41: But the knight doesn't care. Having witnessed Joshua's peerless courage on the frozen frontline, he has already sworn his eternal sword to the young master!
[04:06 – 04:12] Shot 42: Joshua rubs his temple with a smirk: "Do I not get a vote in who pledges fealty to me?" The knight merely laughs, bowing with unshakable noble devotion.
[04:12 – 04:18] Shot 43: Wishing him a peaceful recovery, the loyal retainer departs, leaving Joshua alone in the grand hallway beneath the cold glow of the arched stained glass.
[04:18 – 04:24] Shot 44: Watching him leave, bitter memories from thirty years ago surge to the surface of Joshua's mind like acid, reopening scars that never truly healed.
[04:24 – 04:30] Shot 45: In his previous life, dozens of powerful knights and lords begged to serve under him. But our boy rejected every single offer without hesitation.
[04:30 – 04:36] Shot 46: He had vowed to be Kaiser's singular sword. He intentionally avoided building a personal faction, terrified that gathering followers would make Kaiser suspect him of treason!
[04:36 – 04:42] Shot 47: He sacrificed fame, power, and comrades just to protect Kaiser's fragile ego—and in the end, Kaiser still poisoned him like a stray dog in the dark!
[04:42 – 04:48] Shot 48: A sudden jolt of PTSD strikes his skull like a hammer. Draxia Bell Grace... that treacherous snake was standing right beside Kaiser, smiling as Joshua bled out!
[04:48 – 04:54] Shot 49: Gasping for air against a stone pillar, Joshua is suddenly interrupted by the quiet footfalls of the ducal head butler stepping out of the dark.
[04:54 – 05:00] Shot 50: The old servant inquires if the young master requires a doctor. Joshua shakes his head dismissively, telling the butler to go about his business.
[05:00 – 05:06] Shot 51: But the butler refuses to move, announcing in a flat, unyielding tone that a private dining table has already been prepared and everyone is waiting.
[05:06 – 05:12] Shot 52: Joshua frowns with annoyance, reminding the servant that he already declined the public banquet because of his battle fatigue and injuries.
[05:12 – 05:18] Shot 53: Leaning forward with a chilling glint in his eye, the butler drops the hammer: this isn't a public party—Duchess Vanessa has personally set a private table just for him!
[05:18 – 05:24] Shot 54: And the final poisoned hook? "Your mother Lucia has already arrived at the table." In a split second, Joshua's world turns to ice—Vanessa has taken his mother hostage!

======================================================================
CHAPTER 22 VOICEOVER
======================================================================
CONTINUOUS RECORDING TEXT:
Right after the head butler drops the hostage bombshell, our boy Joshua kicks the dining room doors wide open, his eyes burning with pure ice! Seated across the private table is Duchess Vanessa, flanked by Chiffon, her noble cronies, and right in the crosshairs—Joshua's sweet mother Lucia. Seeing her nine-year-old boy covered in bandages, Lucia's face lights up with sheer relief, completely unaware of the venomous trap surrounding her. For a fraction of a second, the battlefield demon vanishes from Joshua's eyes, replaced by genuine childlike warmth as he greets his mother. But Vanessa cuts in with sickening aristocratic sweetness, purring that this is their very first formal introduction as one big happy family. Chiffon immediately tries to assert dominance, barking at Joshua to mind his manners and grovel before the Duchess like a good concubine's bastard. Joshua shoots Chiffon a sideways glare so heavy with killing intent that the veteran knight literally chokes on his own saliva, stumbling back a full step! Chiffon is sweating bullets inside: a nine-year-old child just made a seasoned B-Rank commander's survival instincts scream for mercy! Sensing the lethal tension, Lucia softly intervenes, gently reminding her son to offer proper etiquette to the terrifying mistress of the house. Lowering his head in a picture-perfect imperial salute, our boy delivers his introduction: "I am Joshua Agnus." Clear, loud, and proud. The word "Agnus" hits Vanessa like a physical slap across the face! A visible vein pops on her forehead—how dare a lowborn maid's son claim her sacred house surname?! Clenching her manicured nails under the table, Vanessa forces herself to breathe: just endure the disrespect, because after tonight, this brat will be out of her sight forever. The Duchess purrs that she organized this farewell dinner because Joshua is riding south to Arcadia, lamenting that they haven't had time to bond. Lucia's jaw drops: this is the first time she is hearing about it! Her nine-year-old baby is being shipped off to the capital's ruthless academy?! Chiffon confirms that Joshua earned his official C-Rank knighthood in the north, making his enrollment at the Imperial Academy a mandatory ducal decree. Right on cue, ducal servants roll in silver platters piled high with butter-glazed venison steaks, roasted root vegetables, and rare vintage wine. An exceptionally tender cut is placed directly in front of Lucia, glazed in rich red gravy that smells heavenly—with a barely perceptible metallic undertone. Having lived in grinding poverty for a decade, Lucia is touched by the feast, picking up her silver knife to slice into the steak with genuine gratitude. Slicing off a tender bite, Lucia raises the fork toward her lips, happily preparing to enjoy the meal prepared by the generous Duchess. Across the table, Vanessa's eyes narrow into sadistic crescents. She leans forward, practically drooling at the thought of the concubine swallowing lethal venom! BANG! Joshua violently slams both hands onto the table, sending silver goblets and crystal carafes rattling across the white tablecloth! The entire dining hall freezes. Standing tall, our boy smiles like an executioner: "One moment. Before we dine, I have a tiny question to clear up." Joshua tilts his head, locking eyes with Vanessa: "It's not like Her Grace slipped deadly poison into our dishes... right?" Absolute silence suffocates the room. Dropping a poison accusation directly to the Duchess's face in her own dining room is pure imperial suicide! Chiffon erupts in theater-level outrage, screaming that accusing the Duchess of poisoning food is treason worthy of being beheaded where he stands! Spitting venom, Chiffon yells that a loyal matriarch would never stoop to such filthy peasant tricks, demanding an immediate apology on his knees! But Joshua doesn't flinch. Instead, oppressive sovereign aura spills from his small frame, suffocating the oxygen in the room like a physical blanket of lead! In a voice that could freeze hell itself, our boy murmurs: "Disrespectful? Raising your dog's voice at an Agnus knight is true disrespect." "WHO DO YOU THINK YOU'RE TALKING TO?!" The authority of an undefeated Grand Duke echoes through the hall, reducing Chiffon to a paralyzed mute! Smoothly stepping forward, Joshua slides the poisoned meat away from his mother, declaring with a cold grin that Lucia will take only fresh peeled fruit tonight. Vanessa's knuckles turn chalk-white, bending her silver chalice out of shape. She has been completely outmaneuvered, unable to utter a single word of protest! Sitting back down, Joshua takes a sip of spring water, smiling politely: "Now then, Duchess, let us not let your wonderful feast go to waste." Pure psychological dominance! Meanwhile, hundreds of leagues to the north in the imperial capital Arcadia, golden sunshine washes over the manicured royal gardens of the Imperial Palace. Lounging on an ornate stone bench is a handsome young man with shimmering golden locks—Fourth Prince Kaiser von Britten in his younger royal days! Suddenly, the calm afternoon air ripples like water as dark purple mist coalesces behind the hedge, tearing open a silent spatial gate. Stepping through the shadows is Archmage Evergrant himself, holding a thick parchment scroll sealed with the crimson wax of House Agnus. Evergrant bows low, reporting that the long-awaited reply from the Agnus Dukedom has finally arrived via emergency courier bird. Setting his book aside, Kaiser offers an intrigued smile, asking if the concubine's son has finally responded to the imperial summons. Evergrant presents the letter, noting that young Joshua has penned his personal decision regarding Kaiser's offer of royal knighthood sponsorship. Taking the envelope, Kaiser wonders aloud if the concubine's son proved smart enough to grab the golden lifeline offered by the imperial throne. Evergrant smirks with absolute noble arrogance: of course he accepted! The Imperial Knight Order is every swordsman's wet dream—only an idiot would say no! The archmage chuckles that a neglected bastard would crawl on broken glass just to kiss the Fourth Prince's boots for a shot at noble status. Kaiser breaks the wax seal, unfolding the parchment to read Joshua's personal response with relaxed, amused expectation. But as his eyes track across the ink, Kaiser's easy smile suddenly freezes solid. The relaxation vanishes from his posture like a pulled trigger. Joshua didn't just decline—he roasted the Fourth Prince in print! Our boy bluntly told the future emperor that his "generous offer" wasn't worth the parchment it was written on! For three full seconds, complete silence blankets the garden. Then, a low, guttural chuckle begins rumbling from the prince's chest. "AHAHAHAHA! HAHAHAHA!" Kaiser throws his head back, bursting into wild, psychotic laughter that sends birds scattering from the palace trees! Evergrant is thoroughly spooked, stammering in bewilderment: "Y-Your Highness? What on earth did the insolent brat dare write to you?!" RIIIP! Kaiser violently tears the letter in half with trembling, manic hands, shredding the ducal seal into fluttering confetti! His handsome face warps into a mask of pure demonic madness, his golden eyes glowing with the psychotic cruelty of a born tyrant! "HOW INTERESTING!! JOSHUA!!" Kaiser barks into the empty garden, intoxicated by the sheer audacity of a peasant daring to defy imperial royalty! Even Evergrant feels a chill of genuine dread. Kaiser doesn't just want Joshua as a knight anymore—he wants to break the spearman's soul into tiny pieces! But far to the south, our boy Joshua steps into his departure carriage, having successfully protected his mother from Duchess Vanessa's deadly feast. The poison feast is thwarted, the prince publicly insulted, and the stage set for war. Next stop: Arcadia, where the true hunt for Emperor Kaiser begins!

======================================================================
CHAPTER 23 VOICEOVER
======================================================================
CONTINUOUS RECORDING TEXT:
Right after Joshua asks if the Duchess put poison in the steak, Chiffon completely snaps, unsheathing his sword with a manic screech! Realizing this nine-year-old child will soon surpass him, Chiffon decides to execute him right on the carpet before he becomes unstoppable! "WHAT THE HELL ARE YOU DOING?!" A thunderous roar shatters the dining room as Duke Aden steps through the double doors like a hurricane! Standing at the threshold, Duke Aden radiates raw continental Master pressure that instantly suffocates every single spark of mana in the room! Chiffon turns whiter than a bedsheet, his drawn sword rattling in his gauntlets like a dry twig as he chokes out the Duke's name in horror. Marching forward with deliberate, heavy steps, Aden repeats his question in a low growl that makes the crystal wine goblets vibrate off the table. Vanessa quickly jumps into damage-control mode, purring with rehearsed innocence that she merely arranged a friendly dinner to bid the boy farewell. Aden glances down at the untouched steaks, then shifts his icy gaze back to Chiffon's naked blade with quiet, lethal skepticism. "How long do you plan to hold that steel?" Aden barks. Trembling like a scolded puppy, Chiffon slams the sword back into its sheath in shame. Joshua dispels the hidden mana coalescing around his fingers with a bored snort, thoroughly unimpressed by Chiffon's cowardly retreat. Aden turns to Joshua with a surprisingly gentle command: we march south at dawn, so take your mother and return to your quarters immediately. Taking his mother's trembling hand, our boy leads her toward the double doors, without giving Duchess Vanessa so much as a parting glance. Lucia looks back at Duke Aden with a mixture of sadness and gratitude, before stepping through the threshold under her son's protection. The heavy oak doors seal shut, and Duchess Vanessa drops her fake smile instantly, demanding why Aden has suddenly become so attached to a bastard! She whines that hosting dinner was merely basic courtesy. But Aden turns around with an expression that freezes the air inside her lungs! "Basic courtesy?" Aden's baritone shakes the stone arches. "Does an honorable duchess serve poison to a mother and her child under the guise of hospitality?!" Vanessa's face drains of all color! Chiffon clutches his chest in panic—how on earth did the Duke find out about the secret banquet poison?! "DID YOU TRULY THINK I DIDN'T KNOW?!" Aden roars, unleashing a shockwave of crimson mana that cracks the plaster along the ceiling! Both conspirators cower in terrified silence. The iron duke isn't a passive figurehead; he monitors every single rat crawling through his estate! Meanwhile, out in the quiet gardens, Joshua tucks his mother into bed and steps outside her cottage, letting out a heavy, contemplative breath. Thinking about Lucia's quiet tears, our boy grits his teeth. His mother suffered ten years of noble contempt just to keep him alive. But his thoughts are interrupted by the faint rustle of leather boots. Waiting by the courtyard steps is an armored figure kneeling in the grass. It is the brown-haired knight officer from the victory banquet, waiting patiently in the cold midnight dew to speak with our hero! Lowering his head in solemn feudal tradition, the knight asks for the supreme honor of pledging his life and sword to Joshua as his liege! Joshua studies him closely: having talented subordinates is vital for rebuilding his army, but a returned war god cannot afford half-hearted followers. "Do you understand what kind of path I will walk?" Joshua asks in a chilling whisper. "If you are looking for glory and chivalric games, turn back now." "The road ahead of me is soaked in endless blood and universal hatred. Knowing that, do you still dare to walk behind me?!" The knight doesn't waver for even a millisecond: "I will follow only you, my liege. Even if that road leads through the depths of hell itself!" Presenting his steel blade horizontally on outstretched palms, he declares that a warrior wagers his life only once—and his soul belongs to Joshua! Seeing such authentic fire, Joshua smiles warmly: "Of course." Reaching out, his small hands accept the heavy sword hilt to seal the sacred oath! Dawn arrives in an explosion of golden sunlight. Warhorses snort and paw at the gravel as the departure expedition readies for the trek to Arcadia! Duke Aden sits tall atop his massive white warhorse, draped in his signature fur mantle, ready to personally escort his son to the capital. Light footsteps draw everyone's attention as Princess Cheryl—Lady Charles of House Pontier—strides gracefully into the courtyard in a stunning gown! Lifting the hem of her dress, Cheryl offers a refined noble curtsy, politely thanking the Duke for his hospitality before their departure. Aden chuckles with amusement: is he seeing things, or did the hot-headed tomboy princess suddenly transform into a demure imperial lady overnight? Cheryl immediately breaks character, putting her hands on her hips and complaining: "Then should I kick your shins and yell like before, Uncle Aden?!" Old knight Cox nearly has a stroke on the spot, waving his hands frantically: "Ah! Young Lady, please remember you are speaking to the Supreme Duke!" Aden roars with genuine laughter: that is the Cheryl he knows and loves! He invites her to join their convoy to Arcadia, since her father is currently there. Cheryl politely declines, explaining she promised to visit Cox's family in the countryside first, but promises to catch up in the capital soon. Excusing herself, Cheryl lifts her silken skirts and marches straight past the royal guards toward a shaded tree where our boy Joshua is waiting on horseback. "Young Lord Joshua!" she calls out. Sitting in the saddle, our boy looks down with mild exasperation: what is this reckless girl scheming now? Standing beside the horse's flank, Cheryl's cheeks flush a delicate rose pink as she twirls her fingers, looking uncharacteristically nervous. "I have something important to say to you," she murmurs softly. Joshua raises an eyebrow, wondering if she is about to demand another sparring match. "Please don't freak out," she whispers, reaching inside her bodice to pull out an object wrapped in a heavy golden chain. Joshua blinks in confusion as pure golden light glints through her delicate fingers, radiating refined noble enchantment. Resting on her palms is a heavy, solid-gold pocket medallion intricately engraved with a crowned owl—the sacred supreme seal of House Pontier! Looking up with bright, earnest ruby eyes, Cheryl announces: "This is the ancestral symbol of my family." Extending the precious heirloom toward him, she softly requests: "Once you reach Arcadia... please use this to visit my family estate." Joshua is stunned: that token grants VIP access to Pontier territory, command over their merchant banks, and direct audience with the Duke himself! "WHAAAAAAAT?!" A bloodcurdling shriek tears across the courtyard as old knight Cox clutches his beard in total existential catastrophe! Cox is on the verge of fainting: "Y-Y-Young Lady! You can't just hand out our thousand-year family heirloom to a boy like free party favors!" Cheryl simply giggles with impish defiance, winking at our boy as Joshua securely closes his fingers around the warm golden crest. Horns sound across the estate as Duke Aden signals the march! Carriages roll, hooves clatter, and the journey toward the imperial capital officially commences! With a sworn knight at his side, a ducal seal in his pocket, and a Master's fury backing his name, Joshua rides toward Arcadia to take everything that was stolen from him!

[02:24 – 02:30] Shot 25: Joshua studies him closely: having talented subordinates is vital for rebuilding his army, but a returned war god cannot afford half-hearted followers.

[02:36 – 02:42] Shot 27: "The road ahead of me is soaked in endless blood and universal hatred. Knowing that, do you still dare to walk behind me?!"
[02:42 – 02:48] Shot 28: The knight doesn't waver for even a millisecond: "I will follow only you, my liege. Even if that road leads through the depths of hell itself!"
[02:48 – 02:54] Shot 29: Presenting his steel blade horizontally on outstretched palms, he declares that a warrior wagers his life only once—and his soul belongs to Joshua!
