CHAPTER 24 VOICEOVER
======================================================================
CONTINUOUS RECORDING TEXT:
Right after Princess Cheryl thrusts her ducal family heirloom into his hands, our boy Joshua looks down from the saddle with total bewilderment. Cheryl blushes, playing it off casually: just consider it a personalized VIP pass to visit the Pontier estate whenever he reaches the imperial capital. The surrounding knights instantly start gossiping like schoolgirls: why on earth is the Empire's most eligible highborn lady handing her family crest to Joshua?! "Is that basically a royal love letter?!" one soldier whispers. "Wait, wasn't she supposed to marry Lord Babel?! Talk about an imperial scandal!" Joshua feels a massive migraine coming on: if he rejects the princess in front of chivalric knights, rumors will spread like wildfire and destroy his low profile! To make matters ten times worse, Cheryl starts crocodile crying: "Are you really going to humiliate me in front of everyone after I came to apologize?!" Our boy panics: "She's crying?! She planned this trap down to the millimeter!" Having zero defense against noble tears, Joshua hurriedly snatches the token! Duke Aden laughs heartily from his stallion, cutting through the awkward tension by declaring it's time to ride before the morning heat catches up to them. Cheryl beams with triumph, waving goodbye to Aden and secretly flashing Joshua an impish wink that says "You owe me a visit." "SALUTE THE DEPARTURE! RAISE YOUR WEAPONS!" Hundreds of Agnus knights draw their steel in a thunderous salute, sending the convoy rolling south! Trotting along the tree-lined highway, Duke Aden shoots Joshua an amused side-eye, noticing how carefully the boy tucked the golden token into his coat. "You are still too young to understand," Aden chuckles under his breath. "What it truly means when the sole daughter of a Great House surrenders her ancestral token." Meanwhile, deep within the colossal imperial palace in Arcadia, sunlight streams through soaring stained-glass arches into the supreme throne room. Kneeling on the polished marble steps is an imperial intelligence officer, reciting the exact contents of Joshua's scandalous reply to Fourth Prince Kaiser. The officer quotes Joshua's exact words: "I do not require a prince's favors. I will abide by imperial law and follow only the supreme will of our Emperor!" Seated upon the golden dais is the supreme ruler of the continent—Emperor Marcus von Britten, the iron-fisted monarch of the Avalon Empire! "KEKEKEKE! He'll follow MY will?!" Marcus breaks into a rumbling, menacing laugh that echoes off the soaring vaulted ceilings like rolling thunder! Marcus smiles like a hungry tiger: that means the concubine's brat plans to smash his way into the Imperial Order using pure, unadulterated combat skill! "He intends to crush my proudest imperial knights in open combat? Now THAT is a spectacle I cannot wait to witness with my own eyes!" Four days of hard travel pass, as the glowing crimson sunset illuminates the colossal stone battlements of the imperial capital, Arcadia! The carriage turns down a pristine cobblestone avenue, halting before an enormous neoclassical palace—House Agnus's private capital residence! Stepping out onto the marble driveway, Lucia gasps in pure disbelief: having lived in a shack for ten years, this luxurious palace feels like a dream! Lucia nervously whispers if lowborn folks like them are truly allowed inside. Joshua squeezes her hand with a gentle smile: "Of course, Mother. You deserve this." Flocks of uniformed servants bow to the floor, greeting young Joshua with total reverence under Duke Aden's strict personal orders. Escorting them through the gilded foyer is Sir Cain, his golden eyes scanning every window and corner like an elite royal bodyguard on active duty. Walking through the echoing halls, our boy calculates the security flaws: the mansion is huge, making it nearly impossible for Cain to guard Lucia alone if assassins strike! He cannot stay glued to the mansion 24/7 once Academy classes begin. To guarantee his mother's survival, he needs his own private intelligence network! Joshua turns to his sworn knight: "Cain, I have personal business in town. I will be heading out for a couple of hours." Cain is surprised: "Right now?! But Young Lord, you've never set foot in Arcadia! The capital's alleys are a labyrinth—you could easily get lost!" Joshua smiles calmly: "Don't worry about me. Your only assignment tonight is to guard my mother's door with your life." "Understood, my liege." Cain bows with absolute knightly devotion, taking his post as Joshua slips out into the falling nocturnal mist. Leaving the noble sector behind, our boy vanishes into the bustling labyrinth of downtown Akardi, where neon-lit taverns and criminal syndicates rule the night. Draped in a simple travel cloak, Joshua navigates the dark alleys with the eerie familiarity of someone who spent twenty years memorizing every rat hole in this city. Lingering beneath a street lamp is a man with the most painfully generic, forgettable face imaginable—the exact trademark of an elite professional spy. Joshua notes that in this industry, looking completely average is the greatest armor. Nobody suspects the tavern server or the quiet courier. The spy gives a microscopic nod, turning toward a battered wooden tavern door. Joshua follows two paces behind without making a single sound. Inside, the tavern is strangely quiet. Only a handful of scarred mercenaries sit nursing ale mugs, minding their own business in the shadows. The guide casually murmurs to the barkeep: "Rynn, watch the front counter." A practiced code signaling an official client has arrived. Though surprised to see a child client, the barkeep asks zero questions, proving this underground outfit takes client confidentiality with dead seriousness. Unlocking a heavy door behind the wine racks, the guide motions Joshua forward into a secret back corridor shielded from outside eavesdropping. Leading him into a dusty barrel storage, the guide kneels and presses a concealed latch hidden between the oak floor planks. With a muted click, a massive trapdoor slides back, revealing a steep stone flight of stairs descending deep into the city's ancient underbelly! Joshua is impressed: "Trust is this industry's lifeline. With security this paranoid, even if I offered to sell a dragon's heart, they wouldn't leak a word." Raising a hooded brass lantern, the guide leads the way down the slick stone stairs, cautioning the young lord about the uneven steps. Passing through iron-reinforced subterranean corridors, Joshua spots heavily armed sentries stationed outside each vault door like miniature fortresses. Stationed sentries bow low when the guide passes, revealing this "ordinary-looking man" actually holds considerable rank within the shadow guild! "As expected," Joshua muses. "Never judge a book by its cover—the guy looking like a stable sweeper is actually a high-ranking director." Stopping before a reinforced steel door bearing intricate dwarven lock mechanisms, the guide turns: "We have arrived at the inner consultation room." Joshua chuckles under his breath at the excessive paranoia, stepping inside as the heavy door swings open with a muffled hydraulic groan. Inside is a cozy, soundproof consultation room furnished with mahogany desks, warm velvet chairs, and shelves lined with coded leather ledgers. Sliding into the leather chair, our boy crosses his fingers over the polished wood, looking far more like an imperial warlord than a nine-year-old child! Dipping an iron quill in black ink, the informant asks what kind of secret knowledge a highborn child could possibly seek in the underworld. Joshua smirks under the flickering candle flames. He isn't here to buy cheap court gossip—he is here to buy the names of forgotten war gods! The imperial capital has no idea what is about to hit it. Behind the gilded walls of the Academy, Joshua is building an army that will topple the continent!

======================================================================
CHAPTER 25 VOICEOVER
======================================================================
CONTINUOUS RECORDING TEXT:
Right inside the subterranean consultation chamber, our boy Joshua leans back, noting that the informant recognized him the second he walked through the door. The spy chuckles: a nine-year-old child who one-shotted a Black Ogre in the frozen north is the biggest headline in the empire—they'd be terrible at their jobs if they didn't know him! Joshua’s eyes narrow: having an efficient spy network is great, but it also means his own classified movements could be auctioned off to Duke Aden's enemies at any moment. Reading the young lord's suspicion, the man bows formally, introducing himself as Zero—the supreme director of the legendary Moon Door intelligence guild! Zero reassures him that the Moon Door treats client identity as a sacred oath: only his two most trusted lieutenants know the young master stepped inside this room. Testing the waters, our boy smirks: "Did you lead me to this third-grade room because only basic third-tier gossip is available to someone my age?" Zero breaks into a nervous sweat: how does a child know about the Moon Door's secret tier rooms?! Flustered, he immediately offers top-tier first-grade dossiers! Joshua presses him: "Why offer the family silver to a stranger you just met? Free favors in the underworld are always the most expensive." Zero admits the truth: building an early bridge with a nine-year-old knight who commands Duke Aden's favor is worth a fortune in future influence! Joshua casually drops another bomb: "With a codename like Zero, I assumed you held the mythical 0th-Grade clearance. I guess I overestimated you." Zero's soul nearly exits his body: 0th-Grade clearance is a legendary guild secret known only to the supreme grandmasters of the underworld! Swallowing his dry throat, the master spy bows humbly: "I apologize for prying, Young Lord. Please name the target you wish us to track." Joshua cuts straight to the chase: "I am looking for the ancestral heirloom of the Orvis Count family, which was mysteriously eradicated decades ago." Zero strokes his chin: after the tragic fall of House Orvis, their family treasury was looted and funneled into the black market by corrupt fences. Zero promises that tracing the serial numbers through the underworld will take one week at most—his agents will pinpoint the current holder without a flaw. Joshua smiles internally: seeking the Orvis heirloom isn't just about recovering ancient power—it is the perfect piece of bait to distract the Emperor's hounds! Standing up from his chair, our boy fixes Zero with an intimidating smirk: "How about it, Director? Am I someone worth investing your future into?" Zero stands speechless as Joshua walks out into the night, realizing this nine-year-old boy possesses the calculated ferocity of an ancient emperor. Two entire months slip by in a flash, as the golden summer heat blankets the vast training grounds of the Agnus capital residence. Day and night, Joshua pushes his physical stamina to the absolute brink, sparring relentlessly against Sir Cain to polish his second-stage Magic Spear Arts! But more importantly, Joshua worked tirelessly to relocate his mother Lucia to a heavily fortified countryside estate owned exclusively by Duke Aden. Lucia wept at the thought of being separated from her son, but Joshua gently convinced her: knowing she is beyond Duchess Vanessa's reach gives him total freedom to fight! With his mother safely settled in the fortified sanctuary, the greatest vulnerability of Joshua's life is permanently sealed away! Back in his private capital study, Joshua is reviewing military documents when a sudden gust of wind snuffs out the candles. Out of the shadows steps a cloaked infiltrator, but before the intruder can take a single breath, Sir Cain bursts in with his broadsword bare! Cain's sword tip hovers half an inch from the intruder's throat, barking at the spy to surrender before he bisects him on the Persian rug! The spy raises his hands in surrender, pulling down his hood to reveal Zero's painfully ordinary face: "Peace, Sir Cain! I am an ally!" Joshua casually sips his tea: "Stand down, Cain. He is an associate on official business." Cain sheathes his steel, but keeps his hawk eyes locked on the spy. Setting his cup down, our boy gets straight to the point: "So, Director Zero... did you find the Orvis family heirloom?" Zero smirks with professional pride: "Naturally. When the Moon Door takes a contract, we deliver with mathematical precision." Sliding a silver scroll case across the mahogany, Zero explains that while the artifact was fenced across five provinces, they tracked down its current owner. Joshua reaches for his purse: "And the payment fee?" Zero waves his hands dismissively, declaring this initial service is on the house! Zero tries to pry: to their appraisers, the Orvis relic looks like mundane antique junk. Does it possess some hidden ancient magical property? "Stop prying," Joshua cuts him off with an icy look that makes Zero cough awkwardly: "Understood! I will check in periodically as promised!" Zero melts back into the night shadows, leaving Joshua to unfurl the classified report under the warm glow of the desk lantern. Reading the dossier, Joshua recalls the dark imperial history: House Orvis was eradicated in a single night of fire by Emperor Marcus's secret assassins. Tracking the black market chain of custody, his finger stops on the name of the final buyer: a nobleman named Baron Provallum! Standing nearby, Sir Cain raises his eyebrows: "Baron Provallum? Are you referring to the 'Great Merchant of the Night', Accent Provallum?" Cain explains that Provallum is an infamous underworld mogul: virtually every stolen relic and black-market antique in Arcadia passes through his vaults! And here comes the punchline: "Baron Provallum is also the younger biological brother of Viscount Beeg!" Talk about a tiny, corrupt world! Joshua’s lips curl into a vicious grin: fat Beeg's own brother holds the relic! Striking two birds with one stone just got ridiculously easy. A polite knock interrupts their conversation, as the study door swings open to reveal two very familiar faces standing in the entryway! It is Count Rebrecca, accompanied by his bashful little daughter Iceline, having traveled to the capital mansion to pay their formal respects! Meanwhile, on the wealthy outskirts of Arcadia, night blankets a private estate that is so disgustingly opulent it rivals the imperial palace itself. Pacing inside is Baron Accent Provallum—a bald, muscular titan of a merchant who controls the dark underbelly of the capital's finances. Despite his underworld power, Provallum is sweating bullets, rubbing his hands together in pathetic flattery before an uninvited royal guest. Lounging comfortably in the velvet armchair is a cold, silver-blue-haired youth draped in purple silk—Young Lord Veron Shen Villas! Flanked by two elite imperial bodyguards, Young Lord Veron belongs to House Villas—one of the foundational pillars of the empire's high nobility. Veron takes a slow sip of vintage wine, his glacial eyes fixing Provallum with the lethal detachment of a loan shark collecting blood debts. "The deadline for our agreement has already passed, Provallum," Veron murmurs, his voice dripping with unspoken violent promises. The mighty underworld baron trembles like an autumn leaf: cross House Villas, and not even the dark alleys of Arcadia can protect his neck! Unknown to Veron, the very treasure Provallum owes his creditors is the Orvis family relic that our boy Joshua has just marked for plunder! With Sir Cain at his flank and Count Rebrecca in his parlor, Joshua rolls up the blueprint of Provallum's compound with a wolfish grin. The black market overlord is about to learn the hard way that when the Unrivaled Spear Knight comes collecting, nobody leaves the table with their debts unpaid!

======================================================================
CHAPTER 26 VOICEOVER
======================================================================
CONTINUOUS RECORDING TEXT:
Deep inside the lavish private parlor, black market kingpin Baron Provallum is sweating bullets as a cold voice reminds him his deadline just expired. Provallum stammers that procuring living elves is nearly impossible on short notice—they are legendary woodland recluses who don't just walk into slave cages! Lounging on the velvet sofa is Young Lord Veron Shen Villas, sneering that he pays exorbitant fees precisely so he never has to hear cheap whining. Veron rises to his feet, casually offering Provallum a single week to produce the captive elf, his icy tone hiding a razor-sharp threat. Swallowing his terror, Provallum forces a greasy smile, swearing on his life that his underground smugglers will deliver the specimen without fail. Veron strolls out with a parting warning: fail again, and Provallum won't live long enough to enjoy a single silver coin in this empire. The second the door clicks shut, the underground boss collapses into his chair, groaning that every meeting with Veron shaves years off his life. His clerk points out the terrifying truth: seven days isn't enough time to comb the southern frontiers, much less smuggle an elf past imperial checkpoints! Provallum grits his teeth: they have to make it happen, because if they fail, Veron will tear down his entire syndicate brick by bloody brick! Veron isn't just another spoiled brat; he is the sole heir of Marquis Villas, the supreme military commander holding Emperor Marcus’s absolute personal trust. With the imperial army in his family’s pocket, Veron has spent his entire fourteen years learning how to break people until they kiss his boots. As his luxury carriage rolls through downtown Arcadia, Veron's personal knight officer, Muker, asks if Provallum can truly deliver such a prize. Veron chuckles dismissively: of course the fat merchant will fail, but that was the entire point of setting an impossible deadline from the start! Muker looks puzzled: why demand an impossible task if you already know the contractor can't fulfill the contract in time? Veron leans forward, asking his knight what single weapon a monarch must master to keep commoners, nobles, and scoundrels thoroughly tamed. Muker tentatively suggests the classic philosophy of the carrot and the stick—rewarding loyalty while punishing disobedience. Veron laughs: carrots only work on starving animals, but Provallum is already a fat, greedy pig gorged on black market gold! To domesticate a fat pig, you break its legs with the whip; once it's paralyzed by mortal terror, you offer a tiny crumb of mercy. By the time the deadline runs out, Provallum will be so desperate that he will happily hand over his entire black market empire just to stay breathing. Muker shudders with dark admiration: a fourteen-year-old child manipulating veteran underworld barons like marionettes is truly terrifying. Feeling thoroughly satisfied with his scheming, Veron decides he's built up an appetite and orders the driver to find the most exclusive restaurant nearby. The carriage halts before an elite establishment boasting authentic cuisine prepared by retired master chefs from the imperial palace itself. Meanwhile, inside that very same dining hall, our boy Joshua and his loyal guard Sir Cain have been enjoying a quiet afternoon meal. Suddenly, a soft, familiar voice calls out across the tables: Count Poncel Jean Rebrecca and his lovely daughter have spotted them! Count Rebrecca bows with genuine modesty, apologizing if their sudden greeting interrupted Joshua and Cain's peaceful lunch. Sir Cain returns the bow immediately; among the cutthroat imperial nobility, Count Rebrecca is famous for treating even common footmen with honor. Joshua watches the Count quietly, recalling from his past life that Poncel was born with a sickly constitution and a heart far too kind for politics. High-ranking aristocrats openly mocked Poncel for refusing to exploit his peasants, calling him weak-willed and unworthy of his count's coronet. But Poncel never wavered; while his peers wallowed in corrupt luxury, his compassion made him the most revered nobleman among the empire's common people. Next to him, young Lady Iceline steps forward, her flowing sapphire hair shimmering as she offers our boy Joshua a delicate, shy curtsy. She softly admits that she recognized his midnight-blue hair the moment they walked in, fidgeting slightly as she struggles to find the right words. Joshua raises an eyebrow in amused surprise: in his past life, the legendary Spirit Master Anna Bell Grace was notorious for being colder than an iceberg! But their peaceful reunion is shattered as heavy armored footsteps echo through the archway, accompanied by a guard loudly demanding the hall be cleared. Veron Shen Villas steps inside, but freezes mid-sentence as his eyes lock straight onto Iceline, his arrogant scowl morphing into raw fascination. Veron looks her up and down with greasy entitlement, astonished that a maiden of such ethereal beauty was hiding out in a public restaurant. Striding directly into her personal space, Veron demands to know which provincial family she belongs to, his tone dripping with condescension. Iceline visibly recoils, her skin crawling from his shameless stare, while knight Muker steps forward barking that she should show proper manners! Muker loudly proclaims that the boy before them is Veron Shen Villas, the direct heir to the highest military authority outside the imperial family itself! Sir Cain's face pales: Marquis Villas is one of the empire's legendary Twelve Pillars—why on earth is his notorious son parading around down here?! Seeing his daughter cornered, Count Rebrecca steps between them like a shield, bowing smoothly to diffuse the dangerous tension before blood is drawn. Veron doesn't even bother returning the greeting, glancing down at the frail count with blatant contempt as if an insect just blocked his view. With unflinching dignity, Poncel introduces himself as the head of the Rebrecca family, hoping his status as a sitting count will restore order. But Veron just scoffs: he doesn't give a damn about the father; he claims the daughter committed an unforgivable insult by daring to look away from him! Cain grits his teeth in silent fury: etiquette doesn't require formal obeisance in a tavern, but Veron is flexing his father's rank purely to break the girl. Veron shoves past the Count, reaching out toward Iceline while screaming that she will kneel and apologize before he loses his patience completely! Just as Veron’s hand reaches out, our boy Joshua steps out with hands in his pockets, his calm voice cutting through the hall like a winter frost. Joshua casually drops the ultimate verbal nuke: Marquis Villas must be having sleepless nights knowing his sole heir is an absolute, irredeemable idiot! The entire restaurant goes dead silent before Veron shrieks in disbelief, his aristocratic pride shattering into unhinged homicidal rage! Muker draws his steel blade with a deafening screech, lightning mana crackling down the edge as he screams that the insolent brat has a death wish! Facing a drawn sword, Joshua doesn't even blink, calmly reminding the knight that drawing steel on a noble carries lethal consequences. Joshua’s eyes flare with suffocating red aura: since the knight drew his weapon first, whatever happens next is entirely on his own head! Muker freezes, his sword arm trembling violently as a mountain of ancient slaughterhouse pressure bears down on his skull—this isn't a child, it's a monster! As Veron screams for his paralyzed guard to strike, Joshua smoothly reveals his covert alias: he is Ash von Frederick of the fallen southern nobility! With a predatory smile, our boy Joshua steps forward, promising that starting right now, he's going to engrave that name into their souls forever!

======================================================================
CHAPTER 27 VOICEOVER
======================================================================
CONTINUOUS RECORDING TEXT:
While our boy Joshua prepares to throw hands in the capital, hundreds of miles away, Castle Loch rests under a cold, foreboding moonlight. Inside Viscount Vig's locked private chamber, the fat lord stares blankly while a hooded shadow agent named Ju-ang bows to the room. Seated across the table is young Anna Bell Grace, smiling sweetly as if discussing the afternoon weather rather than high-treason shadow plots. Ju-ang asks what emergency forced an urgent summons, noting that keeping deep-cover operatives moving in broad daylight risks imperial exposure. Anna cheerfully informs him that an unprecedented monster has emerged inside House Agnus, threatening to flip the continent's balance upside down. Ju-ang assumes she means Babel, the ducal heir who became a mana master at fourteen and was hailed as the empire's greatest prodigy. Anna shakes her head: Duke Aden had a secret second son whose terrifying combat genius makes Babel look like a clumsy amateur! Ju-ang recoils in sheer disbelief: Babel was pampered with sovereign elixirs from infancy; how could some hidden child surpass him before age ten?! Anna explains the harsh political reality: the masses celebrate decades of peace, but beneath the surface, rival empires have amassed colossal armies. When military power reaches its absolute saturation point, rulers inevitably look for an excuse to unleash it—meaning continental war is inevitable. Ju-ang warns that an unpredicted genius under Duke Aden could completely shatter their master plan before the first battle even starts. Anna smirks, revealing her master countermove: she will enroll directly in the Imperial Academy of Avalon to seduce or bend Joshua to their cause! But right now in Arcadia, our boy Joshua couldn't care less about distant academy plots—he has an arrogant marquis heir to humble! Veron mocks the name Frederick, sneering that Joshua must be the spawn of some ruined provincial hicks trying to play aristocrat in the big city. Joshua calmly asks if a surname really matters when one of them is about to get their teeth rearranged across the dining room floor. Veron loses his mind: how dare a backwater insect mock House Villas? He vows to eradicate Joshua's entire lineage down to the pets! Veron demands Joshua drop to his knees and beg, claiming he might show mercy and only chop off a single arm or leg! Our boy Joshua doesn't even bother with noble banter—he simply looks the spoiled brat in the eye and calls complete and utter bullshit! Veron chokes on his own spit in absolute outrage, shrieking for knight Muker to shatter the brat's kneecaps and force him into the dirt! Shaking off the brief chill of Joshua's killing intent, Muker roars his obedience, raising his broadsword to execute the impudent child. Muker launches forward with lethal speed, igniting his blade with torrents of cyan lightning mana to end the fight in a single strike! The veteran knight barks that Joshua signed his own death warrant the moment he dared insult the bloodline of the Imperial Marquis! But Joshua doesn't draw a spear or evade—he casually raises his bare arm, condensing pure primordial mana around his knuckles like iron plate! Clang! The lightning-wrapped blade crashes into Joshua's bare fist, grinding to a dead halt with the deafening ringing of an anvil! Muker's jaw hits the floor: a nine-year-old child just caught a full-power mana blade with his bare knuckles without a single scratch! Before the knight can pull back, Joshua slips inside his guard and buries a thunderous, mana-packed straight punch directly into his solar plexus! An explosive shockwave erupts through Muker's spine, caving in his armor and rupturing his internal mana channels in a split second! The certified knight folds like a cheap lawn chair, crashing face-down onto the tiles in a pool of his own blood, completely out cold! Veron stumbles backward into a table, his mind short-circuiting: how on earth could a pint-sized kid flatten a mana-wielding knight in one punch?! Joshua casually dusts off his sleeve and turns toward Veron with a sinister smile, asking how the young master would like his lesson delivered. Shaking with terror, Veron tries to hide behind his usual arrogance, shrieking that touching a single hair on his head will bring down the imperial army! Step by steady step, Joshua closes the distance, calmly repeating that his name is Ash von Frederick—and he doesn't like repeating himself. Veron screams at the top of his lungs, reminding everyone that he is the sole heir of House Villas and that nobodies like Frederick are mere worms— BAM! Joshua doesn't let him finish the sentence, detonating a devastating left hook straight across Veron's obnoxious jawline! Veron goes airborne in slow motion, spitting broken teeth and blood before crashing through mahogany tables and slamming hard into the back wall! Joshua casually strolls over, standing above the whimpering heir and telling him to wake up, because class has only just begun! Joshua lifts Veron by his silk collar with one hand, mocking him for crying like a baby the second his family guards aren't around to protect him. As Veron’s eyes flutter shut in terror, Joshua winds up a second punch, cheerily telling the brat he's not allowed to faint until the debt is paid! Just before Joshua’s fist caves in Veron's skull, a steel blade slams into the floor tiles between them as Muker screams for him to stop! Bleeding from his mouth, Muker drags his shattered body onto one knee, begging for mercy and admitting they committed unforgivable disrespect! Muker slams his forehead directly against the marble floor, offering his own sword arm as a sacrifice if Joshua will only spare his young master! Joshua pauses, recognizing the profound weight of a knight throwing away his sacred pride and risking permanent mutilation just to shield his lord. The sight triggers a painful memory of his past life—a loyal subordinate who threw away his own future and bled out just to buy Joshua time. Joshua sighs, his killing intent receding: he refuses to become a monster who ignores genuine, selfless knightly loyalty. But our boy doesn't let them walk away for free—he smoothly slips the ornate Villas family signet ring right off Veron's limp finger! Tossing the signet ring in his hand, Joshua tells Muker that if Veron ever wants his ducal ring back, he will have to come find him in person! And if the spoiled marquis heir is too cowardly to face him, Joshua will happily auction the sacred Villas crest on the black market for pure gold! Turning on his heel, Joshua walks back to his table where Sir Cain is massaging his temples, wondering how a simple lunch turned into grand larceny! Cain mutters that while Joshua's charisma is unmatched, extorting the sole heir of the empire's greatest military titan is borderline suicidal! Joshua smirks, warning Cain never to copy his style unless he wants Duke Aden to personally throw him into a dungeon for reckless behavior! Watching Joshua stride away, Count Poncel Rebrecca shakes his head in absolute reverence: that boy carries the aura of a sovereign emperor. But young Lady Iceline isn't about to let him slip away—she gathers up her dark blue gown and runs down the marble staircase right after him! The poor count reaches out in utter confusion, left behind with a shattered restaurant and two battered imperial aristocrats on the floor! Sighing with his trademark gentle heart, Count Rebrecca awkwardly offers to escort the groaning Villas retinue to the nearest hospital!

[00:12 – 00:18] Shot 03: Seated across the table is young Anna Bell Grace, smiling sweetly as if discussing the afternoon weather rather than high-treason shadow plots.
[00:18 – 00:24] Shot 04: Ju-ang asks what emergency forced an urgent summons, noting that keeping deep-cover operatives moving in broad daylight risks imperial exposure.

[00:36 – 00:42] Shot 07: Anna shakes her head: Duke Aden had a secret second son whose terrifying combat genius makes Babel look like a clumsy amateur!

======================================================================
CHAPTER 28 VOICEOVER
======================================================================
CONTINUOUS RECORDING TEXT:
High inside the opulent recovery hall of Shani Temple, golden sunlight streams past stained glass as divine priests perform emergency holy healing. Young Lord Veron groggily opens his eyes, finding himself in the empire's most expensive temple clinic where holy magic costs a literal fortune. Stumbling over to the gilded mirror, Veron catches his reflection and lets out a blood-curdling screech that rattles the temple rafters! His prized handsome smile is completely ruined—our boy Joshua's fist knocked his front teeth clean out of his gums! Veins bulging on his forehead, Veron barks for Muker to drag the brat back immediately so he can personally carve him to pieces! But Muker bows his head in grim humiliation, stating bluntly that with his current abilities, defeating that demonic child is completely impossible. Veron violently kicks his knight's shins, screaming that the entire Villas escort retinue will face brutal military torture once they return home! Veron vows to deploy private legions to locate House Frederick and slit the throats of Joshua's parents, siblings, and servants right in front of him! Muker stops him cold: they have zero justification. If they deploy troops over a restaurant brawl, the political fallout will destroy them! No noble in the capital will believe the elite guard of House Villas was crushed by a ten-year-old child—and if they do, their prestige collapses instantly! Muker warns that for great houses, public honor is worth more than life itself; if Marquis Villas finds out, his wrath won't stop at simple discipline! Remembering his ruthless father, Veron shivers, then suggests their usual cleanup: massacre everyone present in the restaurant and bury the bodies! Muker drops the devastating truth: they can't cover it up, because undeniable physical evidence was stolen from the crime scene! Ash von Frederick didn't just walk away victorious—he confiscated the supreme signet token of House Villas right off Veron's hand! Veron frantically pats his coat and fingers, gasping in horror as he realizes the sacred family heirloom is completely missing! That ring wasn't just a signet—it was the Deon Ring, a B-Class national treasure forged by the Magic Tower worth more than an entire castle! Muker explains that Joshua told Veron to come beg in person if he ever wants his family's sacred artifact returned in one piece. Realizing Joshua calculated every single move in advance, Muker advises sending a reliable proxy who won't leak their humiliating defeat. Swallowing his shattered pride, Veron agrees: they need someone influential enough to speak for them, but expendable enough to keep quiet. Back at the dining table, our boy Joshua casually twirls the heavy golden Deon Ring, admiring its intricate sword-and-shield engravings. Sir Cain leans forward nervously, asking if Joshua is truly certain the marquis's spoiled heir will come looking for his stolen treasure. Joshua smirks: Veron can't afford to lose a Magic Tower artifact, but his fragile pride will prevent him from coming in person! He predicts Veron will dispatch his personal shadow proxy—someone of high underground status who answers directly to House Villas! In Arcadia's underworld, Baron Provallum runs the black market night, but House Villas controls Provallum—meaning the baron himself will soon arrive! Cain's mind reels: he assumed Joshua picked a fight out of childish temper, but our boy orchestrated the entire brawl to reel in Provallum! Cain grips the table in silent awe, realizing that the nine-year-old before him possesses political foresight that rivals veteran imperial grand dukes! Just as they finish their tactical analysis, a soft, nervous voice interrupts: Lady Iceline has returned, fidgeting shyly with her dark blue gown. Bowing with pure sincerity, she thanks him for defending her father's honor and shielding their family from Veron's vicious humiliation. Joshua casually waves it off, telling her not to overthink it; he had his own tactical reasons for rearranging that spoiled brat's face. Joshua studies her with genuine curiosity: how on earth does this gentle, sweet-natured girl eventually become the cold-blooded Grand Sorceress of Ice? In his previous life, Iceline was an emotionless queen of frost, wielding unique ice magic that slaughtered armies without shedding a single tear. Snapping back to the present, Iceline vows that if House Villas seeks revenge, House Rebrecca will testify before the Emperor to clear his name! Joshua chuckles: imperial politics isn't solved by honest testimony. But if she truly feels indebted, he has a much better proposition! Joshua asks her for a single personal favor in the future—locking in a future debt from the continent's greatest upcoming archmage! Delighted by the simple request, Iceline beams and promises on her life that she will do anything he asks, blushing as she takes her leave! As she disappears into the drizzling rain, Joshua watches through the window, his smile turning sharp as his sixth sense tingles. Outside in the pouring downpour, a cloaked figure crouches motionless on the rooftop across the avenue, monitoring every breath inside the restaurant. It is Zero, the supreme branch director of the Moon Door, sweating profusely as he recalls Joshua looking straight into his eyes through the rain! Zero’s stealth arts were perfected to fool Master-rank swordsmen, yet Joshua spotted him instantly and smirked like a predator toying with prey! Zero realizes Joshua's knowledge of the Moon Door isn't lucky gossip—this boy is an unfathomable entity who must be reported to guild leadership! Right as the shadow informant vanishes, a private carriage pulls up, and a stout, bald aristocrat steps out into the rain. It is Baron Accent Provallum, the ruler of Arcadia's black market, arriving in person as predicted by our boy Joshua! Joshua greets him smoothly by name, noting that the great underworld baron arrived far faster than expected! Though Provallum commands wealth that rivals high dukes, he bows with deep courtesy before the supposed heir of House Frederick. Sir Cain is dumbfounded: Provallum has the military backing of Marquis Villas, yet he is groveling before a child from the countryside! Provallum gets straight to the point: he is here as Veron's representative to reclaim the precious Deon Ring and settle the dispute peacefully. Joshua coldly rejects him: he told Veron to come in person, and if the coward refuses, Joshua will simply melt the priceless artifact into scrap! Provallum’s temples throb with fury, stammering that Veron cannot walk because our boy literally knocked his front teeth into the back of his throat! Joshua sighs at the heir's pathetic fragility, conceding that if Veron is too frail to walk, Provallum must bring knight Muker along next time. Relieved, Provallum asks if that means Joshua will finally negotiate the return of the ring once Muker arrives to represent the family. Joshua nods, but adds that business is a two-way street—since he agreed to entertain their request, the baron must grant him a small favor first! Joshua points directly at Provallum's fat fingers, cheerfully remarking that his diamond-encrusted gold rings look exceptionally valuable! The black market boss freezes in utter disbelief: this nine-year-old kid is shamelessly extorting the underworld emperor of Arcadia! Beckoning with a cheeky smile, our boy Joshua orders the underworld tycoon to cough up his jewelry right now—talk about peak daylight robbery!

======================================================================
CHAPTER 29 VOICEOVER
======================================================================
CONTINUOUS RECORDING TEXT:
Walking back beneath the capital streetlamps, Sir Cain chuckles that his young master has officially transformed into a ruthless demon! Cain can't wrap his head around how a nine-year-old child just casually stripped the supreme ruler of Arcadia's underworld of his personal jewelry! Joshua shrugs: he didn't rob anyone—giving that corrupt baron a second chance to breathe is a luxury that demands a hefty consultation fee! Raising the ring into the moonlight, Joshua reveals the truth: this unassuming piece of jewelry is the legendary heirloom known as the Elf's Tears. Centuries ago, greedy human nobles hunted the ethereal elven race for their beauty, capturing them into chains and slaughtering their sacred forests. Driven to the southern edge of the world, high elven mages combined their lifeforce to weave a colossal, impenetrable continental barrier around their forest. Dozens of High Elves perished maintaining the barrier, leaving behind a single surviving sovereign: Elunbug, the immortal High King of the Elves! For hundreds of years, only one single human was ever permitted to enter the sacred forest: Xerath Dick Orvis, the sworn friend of King Elunbug. But human greed knew no bounds—imperial nobles destroyed House Orvis and tortured Xerath to his dying breath, yet he took the secret to his grave. Arriving too late, King Elunbug found Xerath's orphaned daughter living as a common servant; weeping in sorrow, his divine tears crystallized inside this emerald ring. That ring became known as the Elf's Tears, an ancient artifact that holds dormant powers far beyond anything modern magicians can replicate. Snapping out of his historical musings, Joshua halts outside the Agnus capital estate, turning toward Sir Cain with a solemn expression. Joshua addresses him directly: he needs to ask a serious favor that concerns not just his own future, but Cain's entire path as a knight. Cain salutes enthusiastically, ready to tear through dragons, but Joshua clarifies that this mission requires absolute discipline and patience. Joshua states coldly that he has zero use for comrades who get slaughtered before him; he needs sworn warriors he can trust with his back! Our boy reveals his plan: he is enrolling in the Avalon Imperial Academy alone, meaning Cain must relocate to protect his mother, Lady Lucia. Cain protests frantically: without an attending knight, Joshua will enter the academy as a penniless country noble with zero protection! In that viper's nest of high aristocracy, nobles will openly torment a lone commoner—and Veron Shen Villas will be waiting with his private academy gang! Joshua offers a bittersweet smile: after fighting through forty years of bloody civil war in his past life, surviving alone is his absolute specialty! Then Joshua lowers his head with total sincerity, asking Cain not as a liege, but as a brother, to master Duke Aden's legendary sword arts! Cain's heart shatters: seeing the sovereign he worships bow before him is unbearable—he rushes forward, begging Joshua to lift his head! Cain accepts the duty under one condition: if business is an equal exchange, then Joshua must grant his knight a personal vow in return. With fierce tears in his eyes, Cain asks Joshua to become an emperor so absolute that Cain will never have to kneel before another soul in existence! Joshua smirks with absolute majesty, swearing upon his very soul that Sir Cain will never bow his head to anyone other than him! Meanwhile, thousands of leagues away at the geographical core of the continent, an ivory spire pierces the heavens—the legendary Magic Tower! Buffered by three rival superpowers, the Magic Tower stands as an untouchable sovereign nation, holding the supreme pride and lore of all mages. At the pinnacle of the citadel sits the Grand Council Chamber, where the Seven Magicians convene to decide the fate of continental magic. Presiding over the council is Iain Jean Perry, the world's first living 9th-Class Archmage and supreme Master of the Magic Tower! Iain scowls at three empty seats: Flame and Void wander the world doing whatever they please, while Thunder abruptly vanished yesterday! Marcus of the Earth Seat takes a calm sip of tea, explaining that Thunder rushed off to investigate an unprecedented emergency in Avalon. Iain cuts through the chatter: today's 27th regular conference has only one urgent agenda—the awakening of the legendary Primordial Stones! Theta of the Storm Seat smirks, remarking that it's unlike the old man to skip diplomatic formalities and dive straight to the punchline. Marcus reprimands the young archmage to show proper decorum, while Theta playfully mocks him as "Mister Earth" before falling silent. Dropping his playful facade, Theta asks if the chilling rumors are true—has someone actually discovered and claimed Magma, the Stone of Fire?! Marcus drops an even bigger shocker: not only has Magma been taken, but Bronto, the Primordial Stone of Storms, has completely vanished! Theta is dumbfounded: Primordial Stones are mythological entities sealed since the creation of the world—how could two vanish simultaneously?! Speaking for the first time, Lady Elisha of the Ice Seat warns that only the Magic Tower possessed the ancient coordinates of the stones! If an outsider stumbled upon them blindly, it's a minor nuisance; but if someone deliberately hunted them down, the continent faces catastrophe! Iain admits he already knows who took Magma, but Bronto vanished without a trace—no one knows whether it dissolved or was absorbed! Theta connects the dots: the Thunder Seat bolted for the Avalon Empire precisely to chase down the lingering lightning fallout of Bronto! Marcus drops the critical lead: right around the frozen crater where Bronto manifested, witnesses reported the appearance of a Magic Swordsman! Hearing the words "Magic Swordsman", Elisha kicks her throne back in absolute hysteria, gasping whether THAT legendary being has returned! Marcus shakes his head: it's not the ancient mythical founder; the magic swordsman reported in Avalon is far, far more unbelievable! Marcus drops the age that shatters all magical sanity: the monster who unleashed dual-cast lightning magic is a child barely nine years old! The council room explodes: forming a single circle before puberty is impossible, let alone dual-casting 3rd-class offensive magic into a blade! Elisha insists the boy must be the sole direct inheritor of the legendary Magic Swordsman, demanding the tower deploy investigators immediately! Marcus calmly adds the final piece of the puzzle: the child is unrelated to their founder—he is Joshua, the second son of Duke Aden von Agnus! Theta screams that Marcus should have opened the meeting with that headline instead of letting them stew in existential dread! BOOM! The 9th-Class Master slams the table with mana, commanding total silence and ordering every archmage to refrain from rash interference! Iain orders them to observe the Agnus Duchy strictly from the shadows; until the truth of Bronto is verified, no mage makes an open move! Grudgingly accepting the Master's decree, the archmages bow and exit, their minds spinning with the impossible name: Joshua von Agnus. Left alone in the cavernous council hall, the continent's strongest living wizard stares out into the moonlit night sky with heavy eyes. Iain murmurs to the empty room: the world is shifting with dizzying speed—and whether this child brings salvation or doom remains to be seen! As the Seven Magicians turn their gaze toward Avalon, our boy Joshua has just ignited the fuse of a global storm that will shake the empire to its core!

======================================================================
CHAPTER 30 VOICEOVER
======================================================================
CONTINUOUS RECORDING TEXT:
Early the next morning inside the private courtyard, dawn breaks across the marble tiles as our boy Joshua prepares to awaken the Orvis relic. The emerald ring hovers between his fingertips, vibrating violently as ancient golden glyphs peel away from its polished surface! Closing his eyes, Joshua recites forgotten primordial runes—not modern magic circles, but the original lost language of the world! Sir Cain covers his eyes in absolute shock: runes are the sacred domain of 5th-Class archmages—how is a nine-year-old chanting them fluently?! With a final flash of golden stardust, the seal shatters and the ring drops into Joshua's palm, pulsing with tranquil elven energy. Cain rushes forward to check on him, gasping to know what kind of divine blessing was engraved into that defiled black market treasure. Joshua explains that the ring automatically deploys a sovereign 7th-Class barrier in life-threatening danger, while boosting natural affinity with spirits! Dropping to one knee, Cain asks if he can bear the ring into battle, hoping its immense protection will let him shield Joshua from harm. Joshua chuckles: the ring was woven exclusively for Xerath's daughter, meaning men cannot channel its magical matrix even if they try! Our boy’s eyes soften: he hunted down this relic for one reason only—to slip it onto his beloved mother Lucia's finger, keeping her safe forever. Meanwhile, deep within an underground sanctum in Arcadia, Moon Door branch director Zero activates a classified communications orb. A shimmering projection materializes: the legendary Grandmaster of the Moon Door, the supreme phantom who commands the continent's underworld! Zero bows low, reporting that Joshua von Agnus isn't just an anomaly—he is hiding combat abilities and classified knowledge that defy all reason. The Grandmaster responds with chilling calm: he already knows the child is extraordinary, but the Magic Tower has officially mobilized to find him! Zero’s jaw drops: the isolationist Magic Tower never interferes in empire politics—why would continental archmages deploy over a ducal bastard?! The Grandmaster explains: the Seven Magicians believe Joshua is the secret blood descendant of Ainslott, the legendary founder of magic swordsmanship! The Primordial Stones are merely a pretext—the Imperial Knights, the Magic Tower, and their own guild's sacred Red Coin are all converging on Joshua! The Grandmaster orders Zero to secure Joshua's trust at all costs: whoever controls this boy will control the destiny of the entire continent! Zero salutes as the transmission terminates, his resolve hardening to tether his shadow network to our boy Joshua's meteoric rise. But hundreds of miles to the north, a violent tempest tears through the ancient wilderness as roaring thunder splits the dark night sky! Tied to a dead tree trunk in the freezing rain is a bruised, bound aristocrat—gasping and squirming in pure, blind terror! Approaching him through the mud is a hooded mage crackling with raw electrical discharge, ordering his captive to shut up and watch! Crashing through the treeline comes a feral horde of giant ogres, their blood-red eyes locked onto the solitary wizard standing in their path! Without taking a step back, the mage spreads his arms wide, chanting an ancient high-tier incantation that vibrates the entire forest! The Roman numeral VII glows beneath his eye, confirming his identity as a legendary pillar of the Magic Tower: the Thunder Seat! Calling down the wrath of the heavens, he unleashes his signature destruction spell: **Lightning Rain**! A biblical deluge of lightning shafts smites the forest floor, obliterating the ogre horde into smoldering ash in less than two seconds! The forest falls dead silent, save for the hissing of vaporized monster flesh as the archmage strolls calmly back toward his captive. Ripping the gag away, the mage demands to know how his lightning magic stacks up against the combat power of young Joshua von Agnus! Gasping for breath, the captive is revealed: it's Viscount Vig, the cowardly uncle who tried to assassinate Joshua at the banquet dinner! Vig shrieks that he is an imperial peer and that Duke Aden and the Emperor will hunt the mage down to the ends of the earth! The archmage chuckles: if he feared the Avalon Empire, he never would have crossed the border in the first place—so start talking! Sweating bullets, Vig flatters him frantically, swearing that the archmage's lightning is a thousand times stronger than that brat's parlor tricks! The mage nods: Ainslott never wielded lightning, meaning Joshua must have absorbed Bronto, the Primordial Stone of Storms, just as he suspected! He resolves to confront Joshua personally, but sighs that breaking into Duke Aden's ancestral fortress is nearly impossible even for him. Seeing a chance to save his miserable skin, Vig interrupts: if he wants Joshua, searching Castle Loch is a complete waste of time! Vig eagerly sells out his nephew: Joshua went to Arcadia with Duke Aden and has already entered the prestigious Imperial Academy! The archmage scowls: Arcadia is crawling with Emperor Marcus's elite guard and monster masters, making a quiet assassination tricky. Desperate to crush Joshua, Vig offers his underground noble connections, promising to smuggle the archmage right into the heart of Arcadia! The archmage chuckles condescendingly: what could an overweight, cowardly provincial noble possibly offer a supreme magician? Vig boasts his aristocratic knowledge: pointing out the Steropes brand, he correctly identifies the 6th-Class Thunder Seat of the Magic Tower! He screams out the legendary name: **Jack Steropes**, one of the Seven Magicians who reign over the mortal realm! Jack Steropes blinks in mild surprise—a backwater swine actually recognized his name without having to be told! Convinced he's saved, Vig smirks, claiming that with his political network and Jack's magical firepower, Joshua will be crushed into paste! But Jack merely shrugs: no matter how urgent the task, an archmage of the Magic Tower doesn't hold hands with a corrupt, pathetic pig! Jack's face twists into a terrifying shadow: leaving witnesses alive who know his true identity is a rookie mistake he never makes! Vig’s soul leaves his body as he shrieks in horror: killing a sitting imperial viscount will bring down the full wrath of Duke Aden von Agnus! BOOM! A colossal bolt of pure thunder erupts from the sky, vaporizing the traitorous viscount and his excuses into microscopic dust! Turning his back on the smoldering crater, Jack smirks pitilessly: who on earth would waste five seconds searching for a worthless scumbag like him? With his target confirmed, the Thunder Seat sets his sights on the capital, hungry to test the mysterious child holding the power of Bronto! The storm is racing toward Arcadia—and the peaceful days our boy Joshua hoped for in the capital are officially dead on arrival! From a discarded stable boy left to die in the mud, our boy Joshua has conquered his first trial, unlocked an ancient elven relic, and set the entire continent on edge. With a 6th-Class Thunder Archmage marching on the capital, did Joshua make a fatal mistake sending Sir Cain away? Drop your theories in the comments below! That wraps up Part 1! If you're hyped for the Imperial Academy war in Part 2, smash like and subscribe. In the meantime, binge our other manhwa recaps on the channel—and I'll catch you in the next one!
